AutoVPN lab â€” used source code

This appendix is the source that runs the Ubuntu AutoVPN gateway (Flask on :5006, SAW, Auto classify, client steering, dashboard, port-80 name, defense watchdog). Unused leftovers (gateway_engine.py) and one-off check scripts are omitted. The live VirtualBox guest was not started or changed.

FileLinesWhat it does
backend-api\app.py3129Flask control plane: status API, Auto, SAW host loop, speed test, consent, accounts.
backend-api\device_manager.py3763Per-device SAW, Auto classify, client tables 110+, LAN-proof, dummy deny.
backend-api\saw.py480Live-first SAW score, normalization, affinity cap, Fail scale.
backend-api\db.py619SQLite accounts, sessions, exclusive device bind, consent, delete device.
frontend\index.html3731Dashboard SPA served by Flask on :5006 / autovpn.lab.
scripts\autovpn-port80-redirect.py39iptables REDIRECT 80 to 5006 so http://autovpn.lab/ stays the name.
scripts\autovpn-port80.service14systemd unit for the port-80 redirect loop.
scripts\defense-watchdog.sh306Host default stays MikroTik; client tables pinned if a VPN is unsafe.

Line numbers are 1-based from the start of each file. Long lines wrap.

  292| 
  293| def log_event(msg: str) -> None:
  294|     line = f"{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')} {msg}"
  295|     HANDOVER_LOG.appendleft(line)
  296|     print(line, flush=True)
  297| 
  298| 
  299| def _log_body(line: str) -> str:
  300|     s = str(line or "")
  301|     if len(s) >= 9 and s[2:3] == ":" and s[5:6] == ":":
  302|         return s[9:]
  303|     return s
  304| 
  305| 
  306| _SYSTEM_LOG_RE = re.compile(
  307|     r"\b(WATCHDOG|DHCP|FORWARDING|GATEWAY|ENGINE ERROR|STATE |API ERROR|BUG #|"
  308|     r"BIND DEVICE|ADMIN FORCE|ROUTE SKIP|HANDOVER ->)\b"
  309| )
  310| 
  311| 
  312| def filter_handover_lines(lines, *, device_ip=None, device_name=None, host_only=False):
  313|     """Keep this device (or host) SAW/handover lines. Hide other clients and system noise."""
  314|     ip = (device_ip or "").strip()
  315|     name = (device_name or "").strip()
  316|     out = []
  317|     for line in lines or []:
  318|         body = _log_body(line)
  319|         if _SYSTEM_LOG_RE.search(body):
  320|             continue
  321|         if ip or name:
  322|             hit = bool(ip and ip in body)
  323|             if name and (
  324|                 body.startswith(f"SWITCH {name} ")
  325|                 or body.startswith(f"STILL {name} ")
  326|                 or body.startswith(f"TICK {name} ")
  327|                 or body.startswith(f"DEVICE {name} ")
  328|             ):
  329|                 hit = True
  330|             if hit:
  331|                 out.append(line)
  332|             continue
  333|         if host_only:
  334|             if body.startswith("TICK ") and not body.startswith("TICK host "):
  335|                 continue
  336|             if body.startswith("DEVICE "):
  337|                 continue
  338|             if body.startswith("SWITCH ") and not body.startswith("SWITCH host "):
  339|                 continue
  340|             if body.startswith("STILL ") and not body.startswith("STILL host "):
  341|                 continue
  342|         out.append(line)
  343|     return out
  344| 
  345| 
  346| def fail_ids_for_user(user_id: int | None) -> set[str]:
  347|     if user_id is None:
  348|         return set()
  349|     with _SIM_LOCK:
  350|         return set(SIMULATED_DOWN_BY_USER.get(int(user_id), set()))
  351| 
  352| 
  353| def fail_ids_for_ip(ip: str) -> set[str]:
  354|     return fail_ids_for_user(_owner_user_id_for_ip(ip))
  355| 
  356| 
  357| def _set_simulated_for_user(user_id: int, rid: str, down: bool) -> None:
  358|     with _SIM_LOCK:
  359|         bucket = SIMULATED_DOWN_BY_USER.setdefault(int(user_id), set())
  360|         if down:
  361|             bucket.add(rid)
  362|         else:
  363|             bucket.discard(rid)
  364|         SIMULATED_DOWN.clear()
  365|         for ids in SIMULATED_DOWN_BY_USER.values():
  366|             SIMULATED_DOWN.update(ids)
  367| 
  368| 
  369| def _set_simulated(rid: str, down: bool) -> None:
  370|     """Host/admin leftover: do not use for client ranking."""
  371|     _set_simulated_for_user(0, rid, down)
  372| 
  373| 
  374| def _resolve_regime_id(raw: str | None) -> str | None:
  375|     rid = (raw or "").strip()
  376|     if rid in REGIMES:
  377|         return rid
  378|     low = rid.lower()
  379|     for key, meta in REGIMES.items():
  380|         if str(meta.get("name") or "").lower() == low:
  381|             return key
  382|     return None
  383| 
  384| 
  385| def simulated_status(user_id: int | None = None) -> dict:
  386|     failed = fail_ids_for_user(user_id)
  387|     return {
  388|         "simulated_down": sorted(failed),
  389|         "wireguard_healthy": "my-wireguard" not in failed,
  390|         "paths": [
  391|             {
  392|                 "id": rid,
  393|                 "name": meta["name"],
  394|                 "simulated": rid in failed,
  395|             }
  396|             for rid, meta in REGIMES.items()
  397|             if meta.get("enabled", True)
  398|         ],
  399|     }
  400| 
  401| 
  402| def persist_engine_state() -> None:
  403|     try:
  404|         os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(_STATE_PATH), exist_ok=True)
  405|         with open(_STATE_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
  406|             json.dump({
  407|                 # Persist intent (default On). Live GATEWAY_ENABLED is only True
  408|                 # after forwarding is proven and DHCP may point at .10.
  409|                 "gateway_enabled": bool(GATEWAY_WANT_ON),
  410|                 "profile": CURRENT_PROFILE,
  411|             }, f)
  412|     except Exception as e:
  413|         log_event(f"STATE SAVE WARN: {e}")
  414| 
  415| 
  416| def load_engine_state() -> None:
  417|     global GATEWAY_ENABLED, GATEWAY_WANT_ON, CURRENT_PROFILE
  418|     GATEWAY_WANT_ON = True
  419|     GATEWAY_ENABLED = False
  420|     devices._ctx["GATEWAY_ENABLED"] = False
  421|     try:
  422|         with open(_STATE_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
  423|             data = json.load(f)
  424|     except FileNotFoundError:
  425|         persist_engine_state()
  426|         return
  427|     except Exception as e:
  428|         log_event(f"STATE LOAD WARN: {e}")
  429|         persist_engine_state()
  430|         return
  431|     if "gateway_enabled" in data:
  432|         GATEWAY_WANT_ON = bool(data["gateway_enabled"])
  433|     if data.get("profile") in ("auto", "balanced", "gaming", "streaming", "downloads", "custom"):
  434|         CURRENT_PROFILE = data["profile"]
  435|     persist_engine_state()
  436| 
  437| 
  438| def run(cmd, timeout=8, input_text=None):
  439|     try:
  440|         return subprocess.run(
  441|             cmd,
  442|             capture_output=True,
  443|             text=True,
  444|             timeout=timeout,
  445|             check=False,
  446|             input=input_text,
  447|         )
  448|     except Exception as e:
  449|         return subprocess.CompletedProcess(cmd, 1, "", str(e))
  450| 
  451| 
  452| def sudo_ip(args, timeout=5):
  453|     """Run ip with sudo, preferring password via stdin."""
  454|     cmd = [SUDO, "-S", "ip", *args]
  455|     r = run(cmd, timeout=timeout, input_text=SUDO_PASS + "\n")
  456|     if r.returncode != 0:
  457|         r2 = run(["ip", *args], timeout=timeout)
  458|         return r2
  459|     return r
  460| 
  461| 
  462| def iface_exists(iface: str) -> bool:
  463|     if not iface:
  464|         return False
  465|     r = run(["ip", "-o", "link", "show", "dev", iface], timeout=3)
  466|     return r.returncode == 0
  467| 
  468| 
  469| def iface_has_ipv4(iface: str) -> bool:
  470|     r = run(["ip", "-4", "-o", "addr", "show", "dev", iface], timeout=3)
  471|     return bool((r.stdout or "").strip())
  472| 
  473| 
  474| def iface_is_dummy(name: str) -> bool:
  475|     if not name or not iface_exists(name):
  476|         return False
  477|     det = run(["ip", "-details", "link", "show", "dev", name], timeout=3)
  478|     return "dummy" in ((det.stdout or "") + (det.stderr or "")).lower()
  479|

  492| _SPEED_TEST_URLS = (
  493|     "http://ipv4.download.thinkbroadband.com/512KB.zip",
  494|     "http://proof.ovh.net/files/1Mb.dat",
  495|     "http://speed.cloudflare.com/__down?bytes=600000",
  496| )
  497| 
  498| 
  499| def _iface_ipv4(iface: str) -> str:
  500|     if not iface:
  501|         return ""
  502|     r = run(["ip", "-4", "-o", "addr", "show", "dev", iface], timeout=3)
  503|     m = re.search(r"inet (\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", r.stdout or "")
  504|     return m.group(1) if m else ""
  505| 
  506| 
  507| def _path_fakes_speed(rid: str, iface: str) -> bool:
  508|     """Dummy leftover paths only. Live WAN tunnels are measured for real."""
  509|     if rid in _LEFTOVER_SPEED_IDS:
  510|         return True
  511|     if iface and iface_is_dummy(iface):
  512|         return True
  513|     return False
  514| 
  515| 
  516| def _fake_speed_mbps(rid: str) -> float:
  517|     base = float(_FAKE_SPEED_MBPS.get(rid, 28.0))
  518|     jitter = random.uniform(-0.07, 0.07)
  519|     return round(max(8.0, base * (1.0 + jitter)), 1)
  520| 
  521| 
  522| def _curl_speed(bind: str, url: str) -> float | None:
  523|     curl = shutil.which("curl")
  524|     if not curl or not bind:
  525|         return None
  526|     r = run(
  527|         [
  528|             curl, "--interface", bind, "-4", "--http1.1", "-s", "-o", "/dev/null",
  529|             "--max-redirs", "2", "-L", "--max-time", "10", "--connect-timeout", "5",
  530|             "--retry", "0", "-w", "%{speed_download}", url,
  531|         ],
  532|         timeout=14,
  533|     )
  534|     if r.returncode != 0:
  535|         return None
  536|     try:
  537|         bps = float((r.stdout or "").strip().split()[-1] if (r.stdout or "").strip() else "0")
  538|     except ValueError:
  539|         return None
  540|     if bps < 1500:
  541|         return None
  542|     return round(bps * 8.0 / 1_000_000.0, 1)
  543| 
  544| 
  545| def _measure_iface_mbps(iface: str) -> float | None:
  546|     if not iface or not iface_exists(iface):
  547|         return None
  548|     binds = [iface]
  549|     ip4 = _iface_ipv4(iface)
  550|     if ip4 and ip4 not in binds:
  551|         binds.append(ip4)
  552|     for bind in binds:
  553|         for url in _SPEED_TEST_URLS:
  554|             mbps = _curl_speed(bind, url)
  555|             if mbps:
  556|                 return mbps
  557|     return None
  558| 
  559| 
  560| def resolve_iface(regime_id: str, meta: dict) -> str:
  561|     preferred = meta.get("iface") or ""
  562|     if regime_id == "my-zerotier":
  563|         # Lab overlay only (ztfn5ujzh6 / 10.66.73.x). Never cloud ztdiysvwlf.
  564|         lab = devices.zerotier_lab_client_iface()
  565|         return lab or ""
  566|     if regime_id == "my-sstp":
  567|         for cand in ("sstp0", "ppp0", "ppp1"):
  568|             if iface_exists(cand) and not iface_is_dummy(cand):
  569|                 return cand
  570|         return preferred
  571|     if iface_exists(preferred):
  572|         return preferred
  573|     if regime_id == "my-openvpn":
  574|         if iface_exists("tun0"):
  575|             return "tun0"
  576|         r = run(["bash", "-c", "ip -o link show | awk -F': ' '/^ *[0-9]+: tun0/{print $2; exit}'"])
  577|         name = (r.stdout or "").strip()
  578|         if name:
  579|             return name.split("@")[0]
  580|     if regime_id == "my-openconnect":
  581|         if iface_exists("tun1"):
  582|             return "tun1"
  583|         r = run(["bash", "-c", "ip -o link show | awk -F': ' '/^ *[0-9]+: tun1/{print $2; exit}'"])
  584|         name = (r.stdout or "").strip()
  585|         if name:
  586|             return name.split("@")[0]
  587|     if regime_id == "my-nebula":
  588|         for cand in ("nebula1", "nebula0"):
  589|             if iface_exists(cand):
  590|                 return cand
  591|     if regime_id == "my-softether":
  592|         if iface_exists("tap_soft"):
  593|             return "tap_soft"
  594|     if regime_id == "my-ipsec":
  595|         for cand in ("vti0", "ipsec0", "xfrm0"):
  596|             if iface_exists(cand):
  597|                 return cand
  598|         r = run([
  599|             "bash", "-c",
  600|             "ip -o link show | awk -F': ' '/(ipsec|vti|xfrm)[0-9]/{print $2; exit}'"
  601|         ])
  602|         name = (r.stdout or "").strip()
  603|         if name:
  604|             return name.split("@")[0]
  605|     return preferred
  606| 
  607| 
  608| def ping_metrics(iface: str, probe: str, source: str | None = None):
  609|     """Return lat, jit, loss, thr, reorder, mos, mtu for probe via iface/source.
  610| 
  611|     Fast: 2 echoes, 1s each. Throughput is max(100-2*loss, 0.1) — never iperf.
  612|     MTU is a single DF ping around 1400 (0 means SAW uses the characteristic).
  613|     Callers must use cached_ping_metrics so the engine loop is not stalled.
  614|     """
  615|     bind = source or iface
  616|     if not bind or (not source and not iface_exists(iface)):
  617|         return 500.0, 100.0, 100.0, 0.1, 100.0, 1.0, 0.0
  618|     cmd = ["ping", "-I", bind, "-c", "2", "-W", "1", probe]
  619|     r = run(cmd, timeout=4)
  620|     out = (r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")
  621|     times = [float(x) for x in re.findall(r"time[=<]([\d.]+)", out)]
  622|     loss_m = re.findall(r"([\d.]+)% packet loss", out)
  623|     loss = float(loss_m[0]) if loss_m else (100.0 if not times else 0.0)
  624|     latency = max(statistics.mean(times), 0.1) if times else 500.0
  625|     if len(times) > 1:
  626|         jitter = max(statistics.stdev(times), 0.1)
  627|         # Two echoes make stdev half the gap — cap so one slow reply
  628|         # cannot tank SAW below a stable live peer.
  629|         if len(times) < 3:
  630|             jitter = min(jitter, 5.0)
  631|     else:
  632|         jitter = 100.0 if not times else 0.5
  633|     thr = max(100.0 - (loss * 2.0), 0.1) if times else 0.1
  634|     reorder = min(loss * 0.5, 100.0)
  635|     mos = max(1.0, min(4.5, 4.5 - (latency / 150.0) - (jitter / 40.0) - (loss / 25.0)))
  636|     mtu = 0.0
  637|     if times:
  638|         # ICMP payload 1372 + 20 IP + 8 ICMP = 1400. One packet, DF set.
  639|         df = run(
  640|             ["ping", "-I", bind, "-c", "1", "-W", "1", "-M", "do", "-s", "1372", probe],
  641|             timeout=3,
  642|         )
  643|         df_out = (df.stdout or "") + (df.stderr or "")
  644|         if df.returncode == 0 and re.search(r"time[=<]", df_out):
  645|             mtu = 1400.0
  646|     return latency, jitter, loss, thr, reorder, mos, mtu
  647| 
  648| 
  649| _LIVE_CACHE: dict[str, dict] = {}
  650| _LIVE_CACHE_LOCK = threading.Lock()
  651| _LIVE_CACHE_TTL = 2.5
  652| _LIVE_STALE_OK = 15.0
  653| _SNAPSHOT_HOLD = 8.0
  654| _LAST_SAMPLES: dict = {}
  655| _LAST_SAMPLES_TS = 0.0
  656| _EMA_ALPHA = 0.45
  657| 
  658| 
  659| def _ema_metrics(prev, new):
  660|     if not prev:
  661|         return new
  662|     out = []
  663|     for i, (o, n) in enumerate(zip(prev, new)):
  664|         # MTU 0 means DF-1400 failed — keep a previous live hit, don't average 0.
  665|         if i == 6 and n < 1200.0:
  666|             out.append(o if o >= 1200.0 else n)
  667|         else:
  668|             out.append(_EMA_ALPHA * n + (1.0 - _EMA_ALPHA) * o)
  669|     return tuple(out)
  670| 
  671| 
  672| def cached_ping_metrics(iface: str, probe: str, source: str | None = None):
  673|     """Return (metrics, ok). Shared by host and per-device evaluate.
  674| 
  675|     Fresh hits are reused for 2.5s. If a refresh fails, the last good live
  676|     sample is kept for 15s so one dropped ping does not flip the rank.
  677|     """
  678|     key = f"{iface or ''}|{probe}|{source or ''}"
  679|     now = time.time()
  680|     with _LIVE_CACHE_LOCK:
  681|         hit = _LIVE_CACHE.get(key)
  682|         if hit and (now - hit["ts"]) < _LIVE_CACHE_TTL:
  683|             return hit["metrics"], hit["ok"]
  684|     metrics = ping_metrics(iface, probe, source)
  685|     ok = bool(metrics[2] < 50.0 and metrics[0] < 400.0)
  686|     with _LIVE_CACHE_LOCK:
  687|         prev = _LIVE_CACHE.get(key) or {}
  688|         if ok:
  689|             blended = _ema_metrics(prev.get("good"), metrics)
  690|             _LIVE_CACHE[key] = {
  691|                 "ts": now, "metrics": blended, "ok": True,
  692|                 "good": blended, "good_ts": now,
  693|             }
  694|             return blended, True
  695|         good = prev.get("good")
  696|         good_ts = float(prev.get("good_ts") or 0.0)
  697|         if good and (now - good_ts) < _LIVE_STALE_OK:
  698|             _LIVE_CACHE[key] = {
  699|                 "ts": now, "metrics": good, "ok": True,
  700|                 "good": good, "good_ts": good_ts,
  701|             }
  702|             return good, True
  703|         _LIVE_CACHE[key] = {
  704|             "ts": now, "metrics": metrics, "ok": False,
  705|             "good": prev.get("good"), "good_ts": good_ts,
  706|         }
  707|     return metrics, False
  708| 
  709| 
  710| def collect_regime_samples() -> dict:
  711|     """Probe every enabled regime in parallel; cache hits are instant."""
  712|     jobs = []
  713|     for rid, meta in REGIMES.items():
  714|         if not meta.get("enabled", True):
  715|             continue
  716|         iface = resolve_iface(rid, meta)
  717|         probe = meta.get("probe") or "8.8.8.8"
  718|         source = meta.get("source")
  719|         display_iface = iface
  720|         can_probe = bool(source) or (iface and iface_exists(iface))
  721|         jobs.append((rid, iface, probe, source, display_iface, can_probe))
  722| 
  723|     probed: dict[str, tuple] = {}
  724| 
  725|     def _one(job):
  726|         rid, iface, probe, source, display_iface, _can = job
  727|         metrics, ok = cached_ping_metrics(iface, probe, source)
  728|         return rid, metrics, ok, display_iface
  729| 
  730|     to_probe = [j for j in jobs if j[5]]
  731|     if to_probe:
  732|         workers = min(8, len(to_probe))
  733|         with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as pool:
  734|             futs = [pool.submit(_one, job) for job in to_probe]
  735|             for fut in as_completed(futs):
  736|                 try:
  737|                     rid, metrics, ok, display_iface = fut.result()
  738|                     probed[rid] = (metrics, ok, display_iface)
  739|                 except Exception:
  740|                     pass
  741| 
  742|     samples = {}
  743|     for rid, _iface, _probe, _source, display_iface, _can in jobs:
  744|         if rid in probed:
  745|             metrics, ok, disp = probed[rid]
  746|             samples[rid] = {
  747|                 "metrics": metrics, "live": ok, "failed": False,
  748|                 "display_iface": disp,
  749|             }
  750|         else:
  751|             samples[rid] = {
  752|                 "metrics": None, "live": False, "failed": False,
  753|                 "display_iface": display_iface,
  754|             }
  755|     return samples
  756| 
  757| 
  758| def refresh_score_snapshot() -> dict:
  759|     """Probe once per engine tick; host and devices reuse this snapshot."""
  760|     global _LAST_SAMPLES, _LAST_SAMPLES_TS
  761|     _LAST_SAMPLES = collect_regime_samples()
  762|     _LAST_SAMPLES_TS = time.time()
  763|     return _LAST_SAMPLES
  764| 
  765| 
  766| def rank_regimes_live(profile_key: str, custom_weights=None, samples=None, failed_ids=None):
  767|     """Single live-first rank used by evaluate_once and evaluate_device.
  768| 
  769|     One sample snapshot is reused for the whole engine tick so host and
  770|     per-device scores cannot drift. failed_ids is per-account simulation.
  771|     """
  772|     global _LAST_SAMPLES, _LAST_SAMPLES_TS
  773|     now = time.time()
  774|     if samples is None:
  775|         # Hold the tick snapshot long enough for MikroTik lease sync
  776|         # so host and per-device evaluate cannot drift mid-cycle.
  777|         if _LAST_SAMPLES and (now - _LAST_SAMPLES_TS) < _SNAPSHOT_HOLD:
  778|             samples = _LAST_SAMPLES
  779|         else:
  780|             samples = collect_regime_samples()
  781|             _LAST_SAMPLES = samples
  782|             _LAST_SAMPLES_TS = now
  783|     else:
  784|         _LAST_SAMPLES = samples
  785|         _LAST_SAMPLES_TS = now
  786|     failed = set(failed_ids or ())
  787|     return saw.rank_regimes(
  788|         regimes=REGIMES,
  789|         characteristics=CHARACTERISTICS,
  790|         affinity_map=PROFILE_AFFINITY,
  791|         profiles=PROFILES,
  792|         profile_key=profile_key,
  793|         custom_weights=custom_weights,
  794|         samples=samples,
  795|         wg_healthy="my-wireguard" not in failed,
  796|         failed_ids=failed,
  797|     )
  798| 
  799| 
  800| def calculate_saw(metrics, sec, rel):
  801|     eff = effective_profile()
  802|     return calculate_saw_for(metrics, sec, rel, eff, CUSTOM_WEIGHTS if CURRENT_PROFILE == "custom" else None)
  803| 
  804| 
  805| def calculate_saw_for(metrics, sec, rel, profile_key: str, custom_weights=None):
  806|     weights = saw.resolve_weights(profile_key, PROFILES, custom_weights)
  807|     return saw.calculate_saw_core(metrics, sec, rel, weights)
  808| 
  809| 
  810| def effective_profile() -> str:
  811|     """Profile used for SAW weights/affinity (Auto resolves to detected mode)."""
  812|     if CURRENT_PROFILE == "auto":
  813|         return AUTO_DETECTED if AUTO_DETECTED in PROFILES else "balanced"
  814|     if CURRENT_PROFILE == "custom":
  815|         return "custom"
  816|     return CURRENT_PROFILE if CURRENT_PROFILE in PROFILES else "balanced"
  817| 
  818| 
  819| def saw_status_payload(profile: str, effective: str, custom_weights=None) -> dict:
  820|     auto = profile == "auto"
  821|     key = effective if effective in PROFILES or effective == "custom" else "balanced"
  822|     cw = custom_weights if key == "custom" else None
  823|     weights = saw.resolve_weights(key, PROFILES, cw)
  824|     prefers = (PROFILE_INFO.get(key) or {}).get("prefers") or []
  825|     return saw.math_payload(key, weights, auto=auto, labels=AUTO_LABELS, prefers=prefers)
  826| 
  827| 
  828| def mikrotik_run(cmd: str, timeout: int = 8) -> str:
  829|     """Run a RouterOS command over SSH (sshpass if available)."""
  830|     # Connection dumps are notorious for hanging — keep them short.
  831|     if "firewall connection" in cmd and "count-only" not in cmd:
  832|         timeout = min(timeout, 4)
  833|     sshpass = shutil.which("sshpass")
  834|     base = [
  835|         "ssh",
  836|         "-o", "StrictHostKeyChecking=no",
  837|         "-o", "ConnectTimeout=4",
  838|         "-o", "BatchMode=no",
  839|         f"{MIKROTIK_USER}@{MIKROTIK_HOST}",
  840|         cmd,
  841|     ]
  842|     if sshpass:
  843|         env_cmd = ["sshpass", "-p", MIKROTIK_PASS, *base]
  844|         r = run(env_cmd, timeout=timeout)
  845|     else:
  846|         # Fallback: ssh with password via SSH_ASKPASS is unreliable; try empty
  847|         r = run(base, timeout=timeout)
  848|     return (r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")
  849| 
  850| 
  851| def sample_local_rx_mbps() -> float:
  852|     """Estimate RX Mbps on the lab LAN iface (enp0s8) between samples."""
  853|     global _PREV_IFACE_BYTES, _PREV_IFACE_TS
  854|     iface = "enp0s8"
  855|     path = f"/sys/class/net/{iface}/statistics/rx_bytes"
  856|     try:
  857|         with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
  858|             rx = int(f.read().strip())
  859|     except OSError:
  860|         return 0.0
  861|     now = time.time()
  862|     prev = _PREV_IFACE_BYTES.get(iface)
  863|     prev_ts = _PREV_IFACE_TS
  864|     _PREV_IFACE_BYTES[iface] = rx
  865|     _PREV_IFACE_TS = now
  866|     if prev is None or prev_ts <= 0 or now <= prev_ts:
  867|         return 0.0
  868|     return max(0.0, (rx - prev) * 8.0 / (now - prev_ts) / 1_000_000.0)
  869| 
  870| 
  871| def sample_ss_udp_ratio() -> float:
  872|     """Fraction of non-DNS sockets that look UDP (0..1)."""
  873|     r = run(["ss", "-uH", "state", "connected"], timeout=3)
  874|     udp = len([ln for ln in (r.stdout or "").splitlines() if ln.strip()])
  875|     r2 = run(["ss", "-tH", "state", "established"], timeout=3)
  876|     tcp = len([ln for ln in (r2.stdout or "").splitlines() if ln.strip()])
  877|     total = udp + tcp
  878|     if total <= 0:
  879|         return 0.0
  880|     return udp / total
  881| 
  882| 
  883| def _parse_ros_rate(text: str, key: str = "rx-bits-per-second") -> float | None:
  884|     """Parse RouterOS rates like 64.7kbps / 1.2Mbps / 850bps into Mbps."""
  885|     m = re.search(
  886|         rf"{re.escape(key)}\s*:\s*([\d.]+)\s*([kmg]?bps)",
  887|         text,
  888|         flags=re.I,
  889|     )
  890|     if not m:
  891|         m = re.search(rf"{re.escape(key)}\s*:\s*(\d+)", text, flags=re.I)
  892|         if not m:
  893|             return None
  894|         return int(m.group(1)) / 1_000_000.0
  895|     val = float(m.group(1))
  896|     unit = m.group(2).lower()
  897|     if unit == "bps":
  898|         return val / 1_000_000.0
  899|     if unit == "kbps":
  900|         return val / 1_000.0
  901|     if unit == "mbps":
  902|         return val
  903|     if unit == "gbps":
  904|         return val * 1_000.0
  905|     return val / 1_000_000.0
  906| 
  907| 
  908| def mikrotik_iface_rx_mbps(iface: str) -> float | None:
  909|     out = mikrotik_run(f"/interface monitor-traffic {iface} once")
  910|     return _parse_ros_rate(out, "rx-bits-per-second")
  911| 
  912| 
  913| def mikrotik_iface_tx_mbps(iface: str) -> float | None:
  914|     out = mikrotik_run(f"/interface monitor-traffic {iface} once")
  915|     return _parse_ros_rate(out, "tx-bits-per-second")
  916| 
  917| 
  918| def mikrotik_wan_rx_mbps() -> float | None:
  919|     """Best-effort download rate seen by MikroTik (WAN + LAN bridge)."""
  920|     rates = []
  921|     for iface in ("ether1", "bridge", "ether3"):
  922|         # Download to clients often shows as TX on WAN and RX on bridge/LAN
  923|         rx = mikrotik_iface_rx_mbps(iface)
  924|         tx = mikrotik_iface_tx_mbps(iface)
  925|         for v in (rx, tx):
  926|             if v is not None:
  927|                 rates.append(v)
  928|     if not rates:
  929|         return None
  930|     return max(rates)
  931| 
  932| 
  933| _DNS_LAST_TTL: dict[str, int] = {}
  934| _DNS_FRESH_UNTIL: dict[str, float] = {}
  935| _DNS_SEEDED = False
  936| _DNS_FRESH_WINDOW = 75.0  # seconds a refreshed DNS name stays "active"
  937| 
  938| 
  939| def _ros_ttl_to_seconds(text: str) -> int | None:
  940|     """Parse RouterOS TTL like 2m28s / 1h / 30s into seconds."""
  941|     if not text:
  942|         return None
  943|     total = 0
  944|     parts = re.findall(r"(\d+)([smhdw]?)", text.strip().lower())
  945|     if not parts:
  946|         return None
  947|     mult = {"": 1, "s": 1, "m": 60, "h": 3600, "d": 86400, "w": 604800}
  948|     for num, unit in parts:
  949|         total += int(num) * mult.get(unit, 1)
  950|     return total
  951| 
  952| 
  953| def _parse_dns_cache_entries(out: str) -> list[tuple[str, int | None]]:
  954|     """Return (name, ttl_seconds|None) from detail or table DNS cache print."""
  955|     entries: list[tuple[str, int | None]] = []
  956|     # detail: name=www.youtube.com ... ttl=2m28s
  957|     for m in re.finditer(
  958|         r"name=([^\s]+).*?ttl=([0-9smhdw]+)",
  959|         out,
  960|         flags=re.I | re.S,
  961|     ):
  962|         entries.append((m.group(1).lower().strip('"'), _ros_ttl_to_seconds(m.group(2))))
  963|     if entries:
  964|         return entries
  965|     # table: "2 www.youtube.com  A  142…  2m28s" or without TTL
  966|     for ln in out.splitlines():
  967|         ln = ln.strip()
  968|         if not ln or ln.startswith("Flags") or ln.startswith("Columns") or ln.startswith("#"):
  969|             continue
  970|         m = re.match(
  971|             r"^\d+\s+[A-Z]*\s*([a-zA-Z0-9._-]+\.[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+)\b(.*)$",
  972|             ln,
  973|         ) or re.match(r"^\d+\s+([a-zA-Z0-9._-]+\.[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+)\b(.*)$", ln)
  974|         if not m:
  975|             continue
  976|         name = m.group(1).lower()
  977|         rest = m.group(2)
  978|         ttl_m = re.search(r"(\d+[smhdw](?:\d+[smhdw])*)\s*$", rest.strip(), flags=re.I)
  979|         ttl = _ros_ttl_to_seconds(ttl_m.group(1)) if ttl_m else None
  980|         entries.append((name, ttl))
  981|     return entries
  982| 
  983| 
  984| # Ephemeral video CDNs — if they are in cache, someone is watching
  985| _VIDEO_CDN_ALWAYS = (
  986|     "googlevideo", "ytimg", "ggpht", "youtu.be", "youtube-nocookie",
  987|     "youtubei", "nflxvideo", "nflxso", "nflximg", "ttvnw",
  988|     "akamaihd.net",
  989| )
  990| 
  991| 
  992| def mikrotik_dns_hints() -> tuple[str, ...]:
  993|     """DNS names from MikroTik cache that were freshly queried (TTL bump / new).
  994| 
  995|     Leftover googlevideo still sitting in cache is not returned — host Auto
  996|     must not show 'watching' from stale CDN entries on an idle stage.
  997|     """
  998|     global _DNS_SEEDED
  999|     filt = (
 1000|         "youtube|googlevideo|ytimg|ggpht|youtu|youtubei|netflix|nflx|twitch|ttvnw|"
 1001|         "akamaihd|akamaized|tiktok|spotify|steam|riot|epicgames|hoyoverse|genshin|"
 1002|         "roblox|rbxcdn|zoom|skype|webex|teams.microsoft|meet.google|webrtc|stun|"
 1003|         "discord.media|windowsupdate|dropbox"
 1004|     )
 1005|     out = mikrotik_run(
 1006|         f'/ip dns cache print detail without-paging where name~"{filt}"'
 1007|     )
 1008|     entries = _parse_dns_cache_entries(out)
 1009|     try:
 1010|         devices.remember_video_cdn_ips(out)
 1011|         devices.resolve_video_cdn_names(tuple(n for n, _ in entries))
 1012|     except Exception:
 1013|         pass
 1014|     if len(entries) < 2:
 1015|         out = mikrotik_run("/ip dns cache print detail without-paging")
 1016|         entries = _parse_dns_cache_entries(out)[:250]
 1017|         try:
 1018|             devices.remember_video_cdn_ips(out)
 1019|             devices.resolve_video_cdn_names(tuple(n for n, _ in entries))
 1020|         except Exception:
 1021|             pass
 1022| 
 1023|     now = time.time()
 1024|     current: dict[str, int | None] = {}
 1025|     for name, ttl in entries:
 1026|         if name not in current or (ttl is not None and (current[name] is None or ttl > current[name])):
 1027|             current[name] = ttl
 1028| 
 1029|     if not _DNS_SEEDED:
 1030|         for name, ttl in current.items():
 1031|             _DNS_LAST_TTL[name] = ttl if ttl is not None else -1
 1032|         _DNS_SEEDED = True
 1033|         # Seed only. Leftover googlevideo in MikroTik cache is not "watching".
 1034|         # Active video still flips Auto via video-like rate or a later TTL bump.
 1035|         return ()
 1036| 
 1037|     for name, ttl in current.items():
 1038|         prev = _DNS_LAST_TTL.get(name)
 1039|         if prev is None:
 1040|             _DNS_FRESH_UNTIL[name] = now + _DNS_FRESH_WINDOW
 1041|         elif ttl is not None and prev >= 0 and ttl > prev + 5:
 1042|             _DNS_FRESH_UNTIL[name] = now + _DNS_FRESH_WINDOW
 1043|         if ttl is not None:
 1044|             _DNS_LAST_TTL[name] = ttl
 1045|         elif name not in _DNS_LAST_TTL:
 1046|             _DNS_LAST_TTL[name] = -1
 1047| 
 1048|     for name in list(_DNS_LAST_TTL):
 1049|         if name not in current:
 1050|             _DNS_LAST_TTL.pop(name, None)
 1051|             _DNS_FRESH_UNTIL.pop(name, None)
 1052| 
 1053|     fresh = [n for n, until in _DNS_FRESH_UNTIL.items() if until > now and n in current]
 1054|     seen: set[str] = set()
 1055|     ordered: list[str] = []
 1056|     for n in fresh:
 1057|         if n not in seen:
 1058|             seen.add(n)
 1059|             ordered.append(n)
 1060|     return tuple(ordered)
 1061| 
 1062| 
 1063| def mikrotik_dhcp_clients() -> list[str]:
 1064|     out = mikrotik_run("/ip dhcp-server lease print")
 1065|     addrs = re.findall(r"(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", out)
 1066|     return [a for a in addrs if a.startswith("192.168.50.")]
 1067| 
 1068| 
 1069| _RATE_HIST: deque[float] = deque(maxlen=5)
 1070| _DNS_BY_IP: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
 1071| _DNS_BY_IP_TTL = 45.0
 1072| _DNS_LOG_TS = 0.0
 1073| _LAST_HOST_TICK = ""
 1074| 
 1075| 
 1076| def _parse_dns_query_logs(out: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
 1077|     """(client_ip, hostname) from RouterOS dns log lines."""
 1078|     return devices.parse_dns_query_logs(out)
 1079| 
 1080| 
 1081| def refresh_dns_by_ip() -> None:
 1082|     """Pull recent per-client DNS queries if RouterOS is logging them."""
 1083|     global _DNS_LOG_TS
 1084|     now = time.time()
 1085|     if now - _DNS_LOG_TS < 2.5:
 1086|         return
 1087|     _DNS_LOG_TS = now
 1088|     try:
 1089|         out = mikrotik_run('/log print without-paging where topics~"dns"', timeout=4)
 1090|     except Exception:
 1091|         return
 1092|     lines = "\n".join((out or "").splitlines()[-80:])
 1093|     for ip, name in _parse_dns_query_logs(lines):
 1094|         bucket = _DNS_BY_IP.setdefault(ip, {})
 1095|         bucket[name] = now + _DNS_BY_IP_TTL
 1096|     for ip in list(_DNS_BY_IP):
 1097|         live = {n: exp for n, exp in _DNS_BY_IP[ip].items() if exp > now}
 1098|         if live:
 1099|             _DNS_BY_IP[ip] = live
 1100|         else:
 1101|             _DNS_BY_IP.pop(ip, None)
 1102| 
 1103| 
 1104| def mikrotik_dns_hints_for_ip(ip: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
 1105|     """DNS names this LAN IP actually queried (empty if RouterOS has no per-IP log)."""
 1106|     if not ip or not ip.startswith("192.168.50."):
 1107|         return ()
 1108|     refresh_dns_by_ip()
 1109|     names = list(_DNS_BY_IP.get(ip, {}))
 1110|     return tuple(names)
 1111| 
 1112| 
 1113| def classify_activity() -> tuple[str, str]:
 1114|     """Host-wide guess from WAN/LAN rate + MikroTik DNS (not Ubuntu VPN UDP)."""
 1115|     local_mbps = sample_local_rx_mbps()
 1116|     wan_mbps = mikrotik_wan_rx_mbps()
 1117|     rate = max(local_mbps, wan_mbps or 0.0)
 1118|     try:
 1119|         # Per-IP samples look idle when traffic is hairpinned through a VPN.
 1120|         # Only force browsing when the house WAN is quiet too.
 1121|         if devices.lan_clients_all_idle() and rate < 0.22:
 1122|             return "balanced", "idle / light use"
 1123|     except Exception:
 1124|         pass
 1125|     _RATE_HIST.append(rate)
 1126|     bulk_sustained = sum(1 for r in _RATE_HIST if r >= 6.0) >= 2
 1127| 
 1128|     try:
 1129|         dns = mikrotik_dns_hints()
 1130|     except Exception:
 1131|         dns = ()
 1132| 
 1133|     # Do NOT use sample_ss_udp_ratio() — that is Ubuntu's own WG/ZT sockets,
 1134|     # which look like "calls/gaming" while the PC is just watching video.
 1135|     quic_https = False
 1136|     cdn_ip_hint = ""
 1137|     try:
 1138|         quic_https, cdn_ip_hint = devices.lan_forwarded_video_signals()
 1139|     except Exception:
 1140|         pass
 1141|     return devices.classify_from_signals(
 1142|         rate,
 1143|         tuple(dns or ()),
 1144|         bulk_sustained=bulk_sustained,
 1145|         quic_https=quic_https,
 1146|         cdn_ip_hint=cdn_ip_hint,
 1147|     )
 1148| 
 1149| 
 1150| def _maybe_log_host_auto_tick(mode: str, evidence: str) -> None:
 1151|     global _LAST_HOST_TICK
 1152|     fp = f"{mode}|{evidence}"
 1153|     if fp == _LAST_HOST_TICK:
 1154|         return
 1155|     _LAST_HOST_TICK = fp
 1156|     log_event(f"TICK host mode={mode} {evidence}")
 1157| 
 1158| 
 1159| def update_auto_detection() -> bool:
 1160|     """Update AUTO_DETECTED with hysteresis. Returns True if mode changed."""
 1161|     global AUTO_DETECTED, AUTO_EVIDENCE, AUTO_PENDING, AUTO_PENDING_HITS, AUTO_LAST_SWITCH
 1162|     if CURRENT_PROFILE != "auto":
 1163|         return False
 1164| 
 1165|     guess, evidence = classify_activity()
 1166|     shown = evidence
 1167|     if guess != AUTO_DETECTED:
 1168|         shown = f"leaning {AUTO_LABELS.get(guess, guess)} · {evidence}"
 1169|     AUTO_EVIDENCE = shown
 1170|     _maybe_log_host_auto_tick(AUTO_DETECTED if guess == AUTO_DETECTED else guess, shown)
 1171| 
 1172|     if guess == AUTO_DETECTED:
 1173|         AUTO_PENDING = None
 1174|         AUTO_PENDING_HITS = 0
 1175|         return False
 1176| 
 1177|     if guess == AUTO_PENDING:
 1178|         AUTO_PENDING_HITS += 1
 1179|     else:
 1180|         AUTO_PENDING = guess
 1181|         AUTO_PENDING_HITS = 1
 1182| 
 1183|     need_hits, need_gap = devices.auto_hysteresis_need(AUTO_DETECTED, guess, evidence)
 1184|     if AUTO_PENDING_HITS < need_hits:
 1185|         return False
 1186|     if time.time() - AUTO_LAST_SWITCH < need_gap:
 1187|         return False
 1188| 
 1189|     prev = AUTO_DETECTED
 1190|     AUTO_DETECTED = guess
 1191|     AUTO_EVIDENCE = evidence
 1192|     AUTO_PENDING = None
 1193|     AUTO_PENDING_HITS = 0
 1194|     AUTO_LAST_SWITCH = time.time()
 1195|     log_event(f"AUTO DETECT {prev} -> {guess} ({evidence})")
 1196|     _maybe_log_host_auto_tick(guess, evidence)
 1197|     return True
 1198| 
 1199| 
 1200| def apply_host_route(iface: str, regime_name: str, gateway: str | None = None) -> bool:
 1201|     """Record SAW handover. Never steal Ubuntu default or send 1.1.1.1 via table 200.
 1202| 
 1203|     Client tables 110+ handle LAN devices. Table 200 + `to 1.1.1.1` was leftover
 1204|     harm (1.1.1.1 followed WG while the host default stayed MikroTik).
 1205|     """
 1206|     if not GATEWAY_ENABLED:
 1207|         log_event(f"ROUTE SKIP {regime_name}: gateway off")
 1208|         return False
 1209| 
 1210|     # 10.66.x is the lab dummy overlay — never a default gateway.
 1211|     if gateway and str(gateway).startswith("10.66."):
 1212|         gateway = None
 1213| 
 1214|     use_tunnel = bool(iface) and iface_exists(iface) and devices.vpn_carries_internet(iface)
 1215|     via = iface if use_tunnel else "192.168.50.1"
 1216| 
 1217|     try:
 1218|         devices.drop_leftover_host_dns_policy()
 1219|     except Exception:
 1220|         for _ in range(8):
 1221|             if sudo_ip(["rule", "del", "to", "1.1.1.1", "table", "200"]).returncode != 0:
 1222|                 break
 1223|     sudo_ip(["route", "flush", "table", "200"])
 1224| 
 1225|     log_event(
 1226|         f"HANDOVER -> {regime_name} via {via} "
 1227|         f"({'tunnel' if use_tunnel else 'MikroTik'} ok)"
 1228|     )
 1229|     return True
 1230| 
 1231| 
 1232| def clear_host_route() -> None:
 1233|     """Remove leftover demo policy routing. Ubuntu default stays MikroTik/enp0s8."""
 1234|     try:
 1235|         devices.drop_leftover_host_dns_policy()
 1236|     except Exception:
 1237|         for _ in range(8):
 1238|             if sudo_ip(["rule", "del", "to", "1.1.1.1", "table", "200"]).returncode != 0:
 1239|                 break
 1240|     sudo_ip(["route", "flush", "table", "200"])
 1241|     log_event("GATEWAY OFF — policy routes cleared")
 1242| 
 1243| 
 1244| def container_running(name: str) -> bool:
 1245|     if name == "vpnserver":
 1246|         r = run(["pgrep", "-f", "vpnserver execsvc"], timeout=3)
 1247|         return r.returncode == 0
 1248|     if name == "strongswan":
 1249|         r = run(["ipsec", "status"], timeout=5)
 1250|         return "ESTABLISHED" in ((r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or ""))
 1251|     r = run(["docker", "inspect", "-f", "{{.State.Running}}", name], timeout=5)
 1252|     return (r.stdout or "").strip().lower() == "true"
 1253|

 1255| def build_status_payload(device_ip: str | None = None, user_id: int | None = None):
 1256|     eff = effective_profile()
 1257|     info = dict(PROFILE_INFO.get(CURRENT_PROFILE, PROFILE_INFO["balanced"]))
 1258|     if CURRENT_PROFILE == "auto":
 1259|         sub = PROFILE_INFO.get(AUTO_DETECTED, PROFILE_INFO["balanced"])
 1260|         info = {
 1261|             "blurb": f"Auto → {AUTO_LABELS.get(AUTO_DETECTED, AUTO_DETECTED)}. {AUTO_EVIDENCE}",
 1262|             "prefers": sub.get("prefers", []),
 1263|         }
 1264|     with ENGINE_LOCK:
 1265|         host_metrics = list(LATEST_METRICS)
 1266|         events = list(HANDOVER_LOG)
 1267|         switch_reason = LAST_SWITCH_REASON
 1268|     # Never hold ENGINE_LOCK across DEVICES_LOCK (list_devices) — that deadlocks Flask.
 1269|     switch_reasons = _collect_switch_reasons()
 1270|     sim_uid = user_id
 1271|     if device_ip:
 1272|         sim_uid = _owner_user_id_for_ip(device_ip) or user_id
 1273|     sim = simulated_status(sim_uid)
 1274|     payload = {
 1275|         "view": "host",
 1276|         "metrics": host_metrics,
 1277|         "active_path": next((m["name"] for m in host_metrics if m.get("is_active")), None),
 1278|         "profile": CURRENT_PROFILE,
 1279|         "effective_profile": eff,
 1280|         "profile_info": info,
 1281|         "gateway_enabled": GATEWAY_ENABLED,
 1282|         "auto": {
 1283|             "detected": AUTO_DETECTED,
 1284|             "label": AUTO_LABELS.get(AUTO_DETECTED, AUTO_DETECTED),
 1285|             "evidence": AUTO_EVIDENCE,
 1286|             "active": CURRENT_PROFILE == "auto",
 1287|         },
 1288|         "devices": devices.list_devices(),
 1289|         "updated_at": time.time(),
 1290|         "events": filter_handover_lines(events, host_only=True),
 1291|         "switch_reason": switch_reason,
 1292|         "switch_reasons": switch_reasons,
 1293|         "device_ready": True,
 1294|         "simulated_down": sim["simulated_down"],
 1295|         "wireguard_healthy": sim["wireguard_healthy"],
 1296|         "fail_paths": sim["paths"],
 1297|         "saw": saw_status_payload(
 1298|             CURRENT_PROFILE,
 1299|             eff,
 1300|             CUSTOM_WEIGHTS if CURRENT_PROFILE == "custom" else None,
 1301|         ),
 1302|     }
 1303|     if device_ip:
 1304|         # Device view must never paint host pick / evidence / ranked list.
 1305|         payload["view"] = "device"
 1306|         payload["device_ip"] = device_ip
 1307|         payload["controlling"] = True
 1308|         dev = devices.get_device(device_ip)
 1309|         if not dev:
 1310|             payload["device"] = None
 1311|             payload["metrics"] = []
 1312|             payload["active_path"] = None
 1313|             payload["events"] = []
 1314|             payload["auto"] = {
 1315|                 "detected": None, "label": None, "evidence": "device not on LAN", "active": False,
 1316|             }
 1317|             payload["device_ready"] = False
 1318|             payload.update(_qos_fields([], sim.get("simulated_down")))
 1319|             try:
 1320|                 payload["remote"] = viewer_payload()
 1321|             except Exception:
 1322|                 payload["remote"] = devices.classify_viewer("")
 1323|             return payload
 1324|         payload["device"] = dev
 1325|         payload["profile"] = dev["profile"]
 1326|         payload["effective_profile"] = dev["effective_profile"]
 1327|         payload["profile_info"] = dev.get("profile_info") or info
 1328|         payload["auto"] = dev.get("auto") or {
 1329|             "detected": None, "label": None, "evidence": "", "active": False,
 1330|         }
 1331|         rows = list(dev.get("metrics") or [])
 1332|         if not rows:
 1333|             try:
 1334|                 rows = list(devices.ensure_device_scores(device_ip) or [])
 1335|                 dev = devices.get_device(device_ip) or dev
 1336|                 payload["device"] = dev
 1337|             except Exception:
 1338|                 rows = []
 1339|         payload["metrics"] = rows
 1340|         payload["active_path"] = dev.get("active_name")
 1341|         payload["switch_reason"] = dev.get("switch_reason") or ""
 1342|         payload["device_ready"] = bool(payload["metrics"])
 1343|         payload["events"] = filter_handover_lines(
 1344|             events,
 1345|             device_ip=device_ip,
 1346|             device_name=dev.get("name"),
 1347|         )
 1348|         payload["saw"] = saw_status_payload(
 1349|             dev.get("profile") or "balanced",
 1350|             dev.get("effective_profile") or "balanced",
 1351|             dev.get("custom_weights"),
 1352|         )
 1353|     payload.update(_qos_fields(payload.get("metrics") or [], sim.get("simulated_down")))
 1354|     try:
 1355|         payload["remote"] = viewer_payload()
 1356|     except Exception:
 1357|         payload["remote"] = devices.classify_viewer("")
 1358|     return payload
 1359| 
 1360| 
 1361| def _collect_switch_reasons() -> list:
 1362|     """Newest-first SWITCH/STILL lines for the UI (host + each device)."""
 1363|     out = []
 1364|     if LAST_SWITCH_REASON:
 1365|         out.append({
 1366|             "who": "host",
 1367|             "device": "host",
 1368|             "text": LAST_SWITCH_REASON,
 1369|             "at": LAST_SWITCH_AT,
 1370|         })
 1371|     try:
 1372|         for d in devices.list_devices():
 1373|             text = d.get("switch_reason")
 1374|             if not text:
 1375|                 continue
 1376|             out.append({
 1377|                 "who": d.get("name") or d.get("ip"),
 1378|                 "device": d.get("ip"),
 1379|                 "text": text,
 1380|                 "at": d.get("switch_reason_at") or 0,
 1381|             })
 1382|     except Exception:
 1383|         pass
 1384|     out.sort(key=lambda x: float(x.get("at") or 0), reverse=True)
 1385|     return out
 1386| 
 1387| 
 1388| def evaluate_once():
 1389|     """One SAW decision cycle under the current traffic profile.
 1390| 
 1391|     Live probe QoS (latency, jitter, loss, MOS) drives the 7-metric score
 1392|     when a probe succeeds. Characteristics are fallback only. Affinity is
 1393|     a small capped tie-breaker. Host and per-device share rank_regimes_live.
 1394|     """
 1395|     global CURRENT_ACTIVE, LATEST_METRICS
 1396|     eff = effective_profile()
 1397|     custom = CUSTOM_WEIGHTS if CURRENT_PROFILE == "custom" else None
 1398|     scores, rows = rank_regimes_live(eff, custom)
 1399| 
 1400|     if FORCE_LOCK and FORCE_LOCK in scores:
 1401|         best = FORCE_LOCK
 1402|     else:
 1403|         best = max(scores, key=scores.get) if scores else CURRENT_ACTIVE
 1404| 
 1405|     prev_name = REGIMES.get(CURRENT_ACTIVE, {}).get("name", CURRENT_ACTIVE)
 1406|     switched = False
 1407|     if best != CURRENT_ACTIVE and best in REGIMES:
 1408|         if GATEWAY_ENABLED:
 1409|             iface = resolve_iface(best, REGIMES[best])
 1410|             apply_host_route(iface, REGIMES[best]["name"])
 1411|         CURRENT_ACTIVE = best
 1412|         switched = True
 1413| 
 1414|     for row in rows:
 1415|         row["is_active"] = row["id"] == CURRENT_ACTIVE
 1416|     rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["score"], reverse=True)
 1417|     _emit_switch_reason(
 1418|         rows,
 1419|         profile_key=eff,
 1420|         custom_weights=custom,
 1421|         device="host",
 1422|         from_name=prev_name,
 1423|         to_id=best,
 1424|         auto=CURRENT_PROFILE == "auto",
 1425|         switched=switched,
 1426|     )
 1427|     _maybe_log_saw(rows, eff)
 1428| 
 1429|     with ENGINE_LOCK:
 1430|         LATEST_METRICS = rows
 1431|     return switched, best
 1432| 
 1433| 
 1434| _LAST_SAW_FP = ""
 1435| 
 1436| 
 1437| def _emit_switch_reason(
 1438|     rows: list,
 1439|     *,
 1440|     profile_key: str,
 1441|     custom_weights,
 1442|     device: str,
 1443|     from_name: str,
 1444|     to_id: str,
 1445|     auto: bool,
 1446|     switched: bool,
 1447| ) -> str:
 1448|     """Log SWITCH on change; STILL at most every ~30s. Store for /api/status."""
 1449|     global LAST_SWITCH_REASON, LAST_SWITCH_AT, LAST_STILL_LOG
 1450|     weights = saw.resolve_weights(profile_key, PROFILES, custom_weights)
 1451|     reason = saw.explain_choice(
 1452|         rows,
 1453|         weights,
 1454|         profile_key=profile_key,
 1455|         device=device,
 1456|         from_name=from_name,
 1457|         to_id=to_id,
 1458|         auto=auto,
 1459|         still=not switched,
 1460|         labels=AUTO_LABELS,
 1461|     )
 1462|     now = time.time()
 1463|     if switched:
 1464|         log_event(reason)
 1465|         LAST_STILL_LOG = now
 1466|     elif now - LAST_STILL_LOG >= saw.STILL_LOG_INTERVAL:
 1467|         log_event(reason)
 1468|         LAST_STILL_LOG = now
 1469|     LAST_SWITCH_REASON = reason
 1470|     LAST_SWITCH_AT = now
 1471|     return reason
 1472| 
 1473| 
 1474| def _maybe_log_saw(rows: list, profile_key: str) -> None:
 1475|     """Emit live-vs-baseline numbers when the ranked list actually changes."""
 1476|     global _LAST_SAW_FP
 1477|     fp = "|".join(
 1478|         f"{r.get('id')}:{r.get('source')}:{r.get('score')}:{r.get('latency')}:{r.get('loss')}"
 1479|         for r in rows
 1480|     )
 1481|     if fp == _LAST_SAW_FP:
 1482|         return
 1483|     _LAST_SAW_FP = fp
 1484|     bits = [
 1485|         f"{r['name']}[{r.get('source','?')} lat={r.get('latency')} "
 1486|         f"loss={r.get('loss')} vsBase={r.get('char_latency')}]={r.get('score')}"
 1487|         for r in rows[:4]
 1488|     ]
 1489|     log_event(f"SAW {profile_key} " + " | ".join(bits))
 1490| 
 1491| 
 1492| # True only after a verified DHCP hijack to Ubuntu (.10).
 1493| UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN = False
 1494| 
 1495| 
 1496| def try_apply_gateway_on() -> bool:
 1497|     """Enable Gateway + DHCP .10 only when Ubuntu can forward. Else stay .1."""
 1498|     global GATEWAY_ENABLED, UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN
 1499|     try:
 1500|         devices.ensure_forwarding()
 1501|     except Exception as e:
 1502|         log_event(f"FORWARDING WARN: {e}")
 1503|     try:
 1504|         devices.ensure_mikrotik_lab()
 1505|     except Exception as e:
 1506|         log_event(f"DHCP DNS WARN: {e}")
 1507|     if not devices.forwarding_ready():
 1508|         current = None
 1509|         try:
 1510|             current = devices.get_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway()
 1511|         except Exception:
 1512|             current = None
 1513|         if current == "192.168.50.10":
 1514|             devices.revert_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway()
 1515|             log_event("DHCP reverted to 192.168.50.1: forwarding not proven (avoid blackhole)")
 1516|         else:
 1517|             log_event("Gateway want-on: forwarding not ready — DHCP stays 192.168.50.1")
 1518|         GATEWAY_ENABLED = False
 1519|         devices._ctx["GATEWAY_ENABLED"] = False
 1520|         UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN = False
 1521|         return False
 1522|     try:
 1523|         dhcp_ok = bool(devices.set_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway())
 1524|     except Exception as e:
 1525|         log_event(f"DHCP SET WARN: {e}")
 1526|         dhcp_ok = False
 1527|     if not dhcp_ok:
 1528|         try:
 1529|             devices.revert_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway()
 1530|         except Exception:
 1531|             pass
 1532|         GATEWAY_ENABLED = False
 1533|         devices._ctx["GATEWAY_ENABLED"] = False
 1534|         UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN = False
 1535|         log_event("DHCP .10 failed; staying on 192.168.50.1")
 1536|         return False
 1537|     GATEWAY_ENABLED = True
 1538|     devices._ctx["GATEWAY_ENABLED"] = True
 1539|     UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN = True
 1540|     log_event("DHCP gateway 192.168.50.10 (Gateway On, forwarding proven)")
 1541|     return True
 1542| 
 1543| 
 1544| _WATCHDOG_INTERVAL = 10.0
 1545| _FWD_FAIL_LIMIT = 3
 1546| _DHCP_REAPPLY_COOLDOWN = 45.0
 1547| _fwd_fail = 0
 1548| _last_watchdog = 0.0
 1549| _dhcp_reapply_after = 0.0
 1550| 
 1551| 
 1552| def _watchdog_revert_dhcp(reason: str) -> None:
 1553|     """Point DHCP at MikroTik .1 and stop using Ubuntu as LAN gateway."""
 1554|     global GATEWAY_ENABLED, UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN, _dhcp_reapply_after, _fwd_fail
 1555|     if "ipconfig /renew" not in reason:
 1556|         reason = (
 1557|             reason
 1558|             + " — Windows still needs ipconfig /renew; Ubuntu cannot renew Windows leases"
 1559|         )
 1560|     log_event(reason)
 1561|     try:
 1562|         devices.revert_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway()
 1563|     except Exception as e:
 1564|         log_event(f"DHCP REVERT WARN: {e}")
 1565|     GATEWAY_ENABLED = False
 1566|     devices._ctx["GATEWAY_ENABLED"] = False
 1567|     UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN = False
 1568|     clear_host_route()
 1569|     devices.clear_device_routes()
 1570|     _fwd_fail = 0
 1571|     _dhcp_reapply_after = time.time() + _DHCP_REAPPLY_COOLDOWN
 1572|     try:
 1573|         devices.trigger_crash_log(
 1574|             "dhcp-revert-.1 " + reason.split("WATCHDOG:", 1)[-1].strip()[:80],
 1575|             force=True,
 1576|         )
 1577|     except Exception as e:
 1578|         log_event(f"WATCHDOG crash-log WARN: {e}")
 1579| 
 1580| 
 1581| def defense_watchdog_tick() -> None:
 1582|     """Keep Ubuntu default via .1; pin dead VPN client tables; DHCP .1 only if enp0s8 dies.
 1583| 
 1584|     Ping 8.8.8.8 uses `ping -I enp0s8` (3 retries/tick). Crash-log or DHCP revert
 1585|     only after 3 consecutive ticks (~30s) AND forwarding_ready is false.
 1586|     Client VPN tables stay on a proven tunnel; a single wg0/tun0 ping -I blink
 1587|     does not pin table 110+ to .1. After a DHCP revert, wait _DHCP_REAPPLY_COOLDOWN
 1588|     before .10 again.
 1589|     """
 1590|     global GATEWAY_ENABLED, UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN, _fwd_fail, _last_watchdog
 1591|     try:
 1592|         devices.protect_host_default()
 1593|     except Exception as e:
 1594|         log_event(f"WATCHDOG ROUTE WARN: {e}")
 1595| 
 1596|     try:
 1597|         devices.guard_client_vpn_tables()
 1598|     except Exception as e:
 1599|         log_event(f"WATCHDOG CLIENT PATH WARN: {e}")
 1600| 
 1601|     now = time.time()
 1602|     if now - _last_watchdog < _WATCHDOG_INTERVAL:
 1603|         return
 1604|     _last_watchdog = now
 1605| 
 1606|     dhcp_gw = None
 1607|     try:
 1608|         dhcp_gw = devices.get_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway()
 1609|     except Exception:
 1610|         dhcp_gw = None
 1611| 
 1612|     if not GATEWAY_WANT_ON:
 1613|         _fwd_fail = 0
 1614|         if dhcp_gw and dhcp_gw != "192.168.50.1":
 1615|             _watchdog_revert_dhcp(f"WATCHDOG: Gateway Off — DHCP {dhcp_gw} -> 192.168.50.1")
 1616|         return
 1617| 
 1618|     wan_ok = False
 1619|     try:
 1620|         wan_ok = bool(devices.ping_wan_via_lan())
 1621|     except Exception as e:
 1622|         log_event(f"WATCHDOG WAN PING WARN: {e}")
 1623|         wan_ok = False
 1624| 
 1625|     if not wan_ok:
 1626|         _fwd_fail += 1
 1627|         log_event(
 1628|             f"WATCHDOG ping -I enp0s8 8.8.8.8 FAIL ({_fwd_fail}/{_FWD_FAIL_LIMIT})"
 1629|         )
 1630|         if _fwd_fail >= _FWD_FAIL_LIMIT and dhcp_gw == "192.168.50.10":
 1631|             ready = False
 1632|             try:
 1633|                 ready = bool(devices.forwarding_ready())
 1634|             except Exception:
 1635|                 ready = False
 1636|             if ready:
 1637|                 log_event(
 1638|                     "WATCHDOG enp0s8 ping missed consecutive ticks but "
 1639|                     "forwarding_ready true — leave DHCP .10"
 1640|                 )
 1641|                 _fwd_fail = 0
 1642|             else:
 1643|                 _watchdog_revert_dhcp(
 1644|                     "WATCHDOG: Ubuntu cannot ping 8.8.8.8 via enp0s8 and "
 1645|                     "forwarding is not ready — DHCP revert to 192.168.50.1"
 1646|                 )
 1647|         return
 1648| 
 1649|     _fwd_fail = 0
 1650|     try:
 1651|         ready = bool(devices.forwarding_ready())
 1652|     except Exception as e:
 1653|         log_event(f"WATCHDOG FWD WARN: {e}")
 1654|         ready = False
 1655| 
 1656|     if not ready:
 1657|         log_event(
 1658|             "WATCHDOG forwarding_ready FAIL — leaving DHCP .10 "
 1659|             "(Ubuntu ping via enp0s8 works); client tables guarded"
 1660|         )
 1661|         try:
 1662|             devices.ensure_forwarding()
 1663|         except Exception:
 1664|             pass
 1665|         return
 1666| 
 1667|     if GATEWAY_ENABLED and dhcp_gw == "192.168.50.10":
 1668|         return
 1669|     if now < _dhcp_reapply_after:
 1670|         return
 1671|     if not GATEWAY_ENABLED or dhcp_gw != "192.168.50.10":
 1672|         try_apply_gateway_on()
 1673| 
 1674| 
 1675| def engine_loop():
 1676|     global UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN, GATEWAY_ENABLED
 1677|     global AUTO_DETECTED, AUTO_EVIDENCE, AUTO_PENDING, AUTO_PENDING_HITS
 1678|     load_engine_state()
 1679|     log_event(
 1680|         f"Engine started (want_on={GATEWAY_WANT_ON} "
 1681|         f"profile={CURRENT_PROFILE})"
 1682|     )
 1683|     try:
 1684|         devices.ensure_forwarding()
 1685|         devices.ensure_mikrotik_lab()
 1686|         devices.sync_devices_from_leases()
 1687|         n_pins = devices.clear_all_force_regimes()
 1688|         if n_pins:
 1689|             log_event(f"AUTO: cleared {n_pins} forced VPN pin(s) so SAW can pick")
 1690|         if GATEWAY_WANT_ON:
 1691|             if not try_apply_gateway_on():
 1692|                 log_event("Boot: Gateway stays Off until forwarding is proven")
 1693|         else:
 1694|             if devices.revert_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway():
 1695|                 log_event("DHCP bootstrap: gateway 192.168.50.1 (Gateway Off)")
 1696|             GATEWAY_ENABLED = False
 1697|             devices._ctx["GATEWAY_ENABLED"] = False
 1698|             UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN = False
 1699|             clear_host_route()
 1700|             devices.clear_device_routes()
 1701|     except Exception as e:
 1702|         log_event(f"DEVICE BOOTSTRAP WARN: {e}")
 1703|     if CURRENT_PROFILE == "auto":
 1704|         try:
 1705|             guess, evidence = classify_activity()
 1706|             AUTO_DETECTED = guess
 1707|             AUTO_EVIDENCE = evidence
 1708|             AUTO_PENDING = None
 1709|             AUTO_PENDING_HITS = 0
 1710|             log_event(f"AUTO START -> {guess} ({evidence})")
 1711|         except Exception as e:
 1712|             log_event(f"AUTO START WARN: {e}")
 1713|     while True:
 1714|         try:
 1715|             defense_watchdog_tick()
 1716|             changed = update_auto_detection()
 1717|             refresh_score_snapshot()
 1718|             evaluate_once()
 1719|             devices.evaluate_all_devices()
 1720|             if changed:
 1721|                 RECOMPUTE.set()
 1722|             if not GATEWAY_ENABLED:
 1723|                 devices.clear_device_routes()
 1724|         except Exception as e:
 1725|             log_event(f"ENGINE ERROR: {e}")
 1726|         RECOMPUTE.wait(timeout=3)
 1727|         RECOMPUTE.clear()
 1728| 
 1729| 
 1730| @app.route("/")
 1731| def index():
 1732|     return send_from_directory(FRONTEND_DIR, "index.html")

 1734| 
 1735| def client_ip() -> str:
 1736|     """Peer IP from the TCP connection. Never trust X-Forwarded-For (spoofable).
 1737| 
 1738|     Do not conflate with selected device_ip (remote control).
 1739|     """
 1740|     return request.remote_addr or ""
 1741| 
 1742| 
 1743| def is_remote_viewer() -> bool:
 1744|     """ZeroTier hallway: overlay /24 peer or Host is Ubuntu ZT IP. Not X-Forwarded-For."""
 1745|     return devices.is_overlay_remote(client_ip(), request.host or "")
 1746| 
 1747| 
 1748| def viewer_payload() -> dict:
 1749|     info = devices.classify_viewer(client_ip())
 1750|     if is_remote_viewer():
 1751|         info["off_lan"] = True
 1752|         info["limited"] = True
 1753|         if info.get("via") in ("unknown", "lan", "hostonly", "lab", "local"):
 1754|             info["via"] = "zerotier"
 1755|         info["skip_login"] = True
 1756|         info["banner"] = REMOTE_BANNER
 1757|         return info
 1758|     info["skip_login"] = False
 1759|     info["banner"] = ""
 1760|     return info
 1761| 
 1762| 
 1763| def _remote_admin_user() -> dict:
 1764|     acct = accounts_db.get_first_admin()
 1765|     if acct:
 1766|         return {
 1767|             "id": acct["id"],
 1768|             "username": acct["username"],
 1769|             "role": "admin",
 1770|             "remote": True,
 1771|             "data_consent": bool(acct.get("data_consent")),
 1772|         }
 1773|     return {"id": 0, "username": "admin", "role": "admin", "remote": True, "data_consent": False}
 1774| 
 1775| 
 1776| def mark_viewer_online() -> None:
 1777|     """Browsing the UI from a lab or cloud-ZT IP is proof that device is alive."""
 1778|     rip = client_ip()
 1779|     try:
 1780|         if devices.is_zt_cloud_client(rip):
 1781|             devices.ensure_runtime_device(
 1782|                 rip, name="Remote", evidence="ZeroTier UI",
 1783|                 egress_reason="ZT remote — MikroTik until Gateway On",
 1784|             )
 1785|             return
 1786|         if not devices.is_lan_client(rip):
 1787|             return
 1788|         devices.ensure_runtime_device(
 1789|             rip, evidence="LAN UI",
 1790|             egress_reason="LAN UI — MikroTik until Gateway On",
 1791|         )
 1792|     except Exception:
 1793|         pass
 1794| 
 1795| 
 1796| def _resolve_bound_ip(
 1797|     ip: str | None, mac: str | None = None, viewer_ip: str | None = None,
 1798| ) -> str | None:
 1799|     """Follow MAC / live LAN after DHCP shuffle. Never SSH MikroTik on the request path."""
 1800|     try:
 1801|         return devices.live_ip_for_binding(ip, mac, viewer_ip=viewer_ip)
 1802|     except Exception:
 1803|         return (ip or "").strip() or None
 1804| 
 1805| 
 1806| def _follow_bound_row(row: dict, viewer_ip: str | None = None) -> dict:
 1807|     """Keep sqlite ip/mac in sync with the live engine row."""
 1808|     live_ip = _resolve_bound_ip(row.get("ip"), row.get("mac"), viewer_ip=viewer_ip)
 1809|     if live_ip and devices.is_steerable_client(live_ip) and not devices.get_device(live_ip):
 1810|         try:
 1811|             devices.ensure_runtime_device(
 1812|                 live_ip, name=row.get("name") or "", evidence="bound IP follow",
 1813|                 egress_reason="bound IP follow", online=True, rename=False,
 1814|             )
 1815|         except Exception:
 1816|             pass
 1817|     live = devices.get_device(live_ip) if live_ip else None
 1818|     mac = (live or {}).get("mac") or row.get("mac")
 1819|     owner = accounts_db.get_user_id_for_ip(live_ip) if live_ip else None
 1820|     if owner is not None and int(owner) != int(row.get("user_id") or 0):
 1821|         return row
 1822|     if live_ip and (
 1823|         live_ip != row.get("ip") or (mac and mac != (row.get("mac") or ""))
 1824|     ):
 1825|         try:
 1826|             updated = accounts_db.update_user_device_addr(
 1827|                 row["user_id"], row["id"], live_ip, mac,
 1828|             )
 1829|             if updated:
 1830|                 row = updated
 1831|         except Exception:
 1832|             row = {**row, "ip": live_ip, "mac": mac or row.get("mac")}
 1833|     return row
 1834| 
 1835| 
 1836| def _user_may_control_ip(user: dict | None, ip: str) -> bool:
 1837|     """True only for an authenticated owner (or admin). No anonymous control."""
 1838|     if not ip or not user:
 1839|         return False
 1840|     if user.get("role") == "admin":
 1841|         return True
 1842|     owner = accounts_db.get_user_id_for_ip(ip)
 1843|     if owner is not None:
 1844|         return int(owner) == int(user["id"])
 1845|     for row in accounts_db.list_user_devices(user["id"]):
 1846|         if row.get("ip") == ip:
 1847|             return True
 1848|         resolved = _resolve_bound_ip(row.get("ip"), row.get("mac"))
 1849|         if resolved == ip:
 1850|             claimed = accounts_db.get_user_id_for_ip(resolved)
 1851|             if claimed is None or int(claimed) == int(user["id"]):
 1852|                 return True
 1853|     return False
 1854| 
 1855| 
 1856| def _owner_user_id_for_ip(ip: str) -> int | None:
 1857|     """Account that bound this LAN/overlay IP, if any."""
 1858|     if not ip:
 1859|         return None
 1860|     try:
 1861|         exact = accounts_db.get_user_id_for_ip(ip)
 1862|         if exact is not None:
 1863|             return exact
 1864|         for acct in accounts_db.list_accounts():
 1865|             uid = int(acct["id"])
 1866|             for row in accounts_db.list_user_devices(uid):
 1867|                 if row.get("ip") == ip:
 1868|                     return uid
 1869|                 resolved = _resolve_bound_ip(row.get("ip"), row.get("mac"))
 1870|                 if resolved == ip:
 1871|                     claimed = accounts_db.get_user_id_for_ip(resolved)
 1872|                     if claimed is None or int(claimed) == uid:
 1873|                         return uid
 1874|     except Exception:
 1875|         return None
 1876|     return None
 1877| 
 1878| 
 1879| def _qos_fields(metrics: list, failed_ids=None) -> dict:
 1880|     """How many paths still count as live for this account.
 1881| 
 1882|     Fail/Restore drives the number. Simulated-down paths drop out of the
 1883|     live count; real tunnels stay up. Before the first SAW rows, treat
 1884|     every enabled path as live so the dashboard starts at 8 of 8.
 1885|     """
 1886|     failed = {str(x) for x in (failed_ids or []) if x}
 1887|     rows = metrics or []
 1888|     if rows:
 1889|         total = len(rows)
 1890|         down = 0
 1891|         for m in rows:
 1892|             rid = str(m.get("id") or "")
 1893|             if m.get("failed") or rid in failed:
 1894|                 down += 1
 1895|     else:
 1896|         total = sum(1 for meta in REGIMES.values() if meta.get("enabled", True))
 1897|         down = len(failed)
 1898|     live_n = max(0, total - down)
 1899|     if total == 0:
 1900|         mode, label = "none", "No scores yet"
 1901|     elif down == 0:
 1902|         mode, label = "live", f"{live_n} of {total} paths live"
 1903|     elif live_n == 0:
 1904|         mode, label = "failed", f"0 of {total} paths live"
 1905|     else:
 1906|         mode, label = "mixed", f"{live_n} of {total} paths live"
 1907|     return {
 1908|         "qos_mode": mode,
 1909|         "qos_live_count": live_n,
 1910|         "qos_total": total,
 1911|         "qos_label": label,
 1912|     }
 1913| 
 1914| 
 1915| def _bearer_token() -> str | None:
 1916|     auth = request.headers.get("Authorization") or ""
 1917|     if auth.lower().startswith("bearer "):
 1918|         return auth[7:].strip()
 1919|     data = request.get_json(force=True, silent=True) or {}
 1920|     return data.get("token") or request.args.get("token")
 1921| 
 1922| 
 1923| def current_user() -> dict | None:
 1924|     if is_remote_viewer():
 1925|         token_user = accounts_db.user_from_token(_bearer_token())
 1926|         if token_user and token_user.get("role") == "admin":
 1927|             return {**token_user, "remote": True}
 1928|         return _remote_admin_user()
 1929|     return accounts_db.user_from_token(_bearer_token())
 1930| 
 1931| 
 1932| def require_user():
 1933|     user = current_user()
 1934|     if not user:
 1935|         return None, (jsonify({"error": "login required"}), 401)
 1936|     return user, None
 1937| 
 1938| 
 1939| def require_admin():
 1940|     user, err = require_user()
 1941|     if err:
 1942|         return None, err
 1943|     if user.get("role") != "admin":
 1944|         return None, (jsonify({"error": "admin only"}), 403)
 1945|     return user, None
 1946| 
 1947| 
 1948| def _mac_for_ip(ip: str) -> str:
 1949|     try:
 1950|         with devices.DEVICES_LOCK:
 1951|             d = devices.DEVICES.get(ip)
 1952|             if d:
 1953|                 return (d.get("mac") or "").upper()
 1954|     except Exception:
 1955|         pass
 1956|     return ""

 1959| @app.route("/api/session")
 1960| def session_probe():
 1961|     """Unauthenticated bootstrap. Overlay hallway skips login as Advanced admin."""
 1962|     mark_viewer_online()
 1963|     viewer = viewer_payload()
 1964|     if is_remote_viewer():
 1965|         admin = _remote_admin_user()
 1966|         token = ""
 1967|         existing = accounts_db.user_from_token(_bearer_token())
 1968|         if existing and existing.get("role") == "admin":
 1969|             token = _bearer_token() or ""
 1970|         return jsonify({
 1971|             "ok": True,
 1972|             "remote": True,
 1973|             "skip_login": True,
 1974|             "role": "admin",
 1975|             "username": admin.get("username") or "admin",
 1976|             "user_id": admin.get("id"),
 1977|             "token": token,
 1978|             "data_consent": bool(admin.get("data_consent")),
 1979|             "viewer": viewer,
 1980|             "banner": REMOTE_BANNER,
 1981|         })
 1982|     user = accounts_db.user_from_token(_bearer_token())
 1983|     return jsonify({
 1984|         "ok": True,
 1985|         "remote": False,
 1986|         "skip_login": False,
 1987|         "login_required": user is None,
 1988|         "role": (user or {}).get("role"),
 1989|         "username": (user or {}).get("username"),
 1990|         "user_id": (user or {}).get("id"),
 1991|         "data_consent": bool((user or {}).get("data_consent")),
 1992|         "viewer": viewer,
 1993|         "banner": "",
 1994|     })

 1997| @app.route("/api/login", methods=["POST"])
 1998| def login():
 1999|     if is_remote_viewer():
 2000|         admin = _remote_admin_user()
 2001|         return jsonify({
 2002|             "username": admin.get("username") or "admin",
 2003|             "role": "admin",
 2004|             "user_id": admin.get("id"),
 2005|             "token": "",
 2006|             "data_consent": bool(admin.get("data_consent")),
 2007|             "remote": True,
 2008|             "skip_login": True,
 2009|             "banner": REMOTE_BANNER,
 2010|         })
 2011|     data = request.get_json(force=True, silent=True) or {}
 2012|     username = (data.get("username") or "").strip()
 2013|     password = data.get("password") or ""
 2014|     acct = accounts_db.authenticate(username, password)
 2015|     if not acct:
 2016|         return jsonify({"error": "invalid credentials"}), 401
 2017|     token = accounts_db.create_session(acct["id"])
 2018|     return jsonify({
 2019|         "username": acct["username"],
 2020|         "role": acct["role"],
 2021|         "user_id": acct["id"],
 2022|         "token": token,
 2023|         "data_consent": bool(acct.get("data_consent")),
 2024|     })

 2027| @app.route("/api/me")
 2028| def me():
 2029|     user, err = require_user()
 2030|     if err:
 2031|         return err
 2032|     remote = bool(user.get("remote") or is_remote_viewer())
 2033|     return jsonify({
 2034|         "user_id": user["id"],
 2035|         "username": user["username"],
 2036|         "role": user["role"],
 2037|         "remote": remote,
 2038|         "skip_login": remote,
 2039|         "data_consent": bool(user.get("data_consent")),
 2040|         "banner": REMOTE_BANNER if remote else "",
 2041|     })

 2044| @app.route("/api/signup", methods=["POST"])
 2045| def signup():
 2046|     if is_remote_viewer():
 2047|         return jsonify({"error": "sign up is for the lab LAN"}), 403
 2048|     data = request.get_json(force=True, silent=True) or {}
 2049|     username = (data.get("username") or "").strip()
 2050|     password = data.get("password") or ""
 2051|     if str(data.get("role") or "").lower() == "admin":
 2052|         return jsonify({"error": "cannot create admin accounts"}), 403
 2053|     bootstrap = not accounts_db.has_admin()
 2054|     try:
 2055|         acct = accounts_db.create_user(
 2056|             username,
 2057|             password,
 2058|             role="admin" if bootstrap else "user",
 2059|             allow_admin=bootstrap,
 2060|         )

 2106| @app.route("/api/speed-test", methods=["POST"])
 2107| def api_speed_test():
 2108|     """Mbps on the Fast.com hop: LAN client → Ubuntu → live tunnel.
 2109| 
 2110|     Leftover dummy paths (SSTP / OpenConnect / Nebula / ZeroTier) stay canned.
 2111|     Live tunnels never fall back to a fake Mbps.
 2112|     """
 2113|     user, err = require_user()
 2114|     if err:
 2115|         return err
 2116|     data = request.get_json(force=True, silent=True) or {}
 2117|     dip = (data.get("device_ip") or "").strip() or client_ip()
 2118|     if dip:
 2119|         if user.get("role") != "admin" and not _user_may_control_ip(user, dip):
 2120|             return jsonify({"error": "device not yours"}), 403
 2121|         if not devices.get_device(dip) and devices.is_steerable_client(dip):
 2122|             devices.ensure_runtime_device(
 2123|                 dip, name=devices.guess_name(dip, ""), evidence="speed test",
 2124|                 egress_reason="speed test", online=False,
 2125|             )
 2126|     try:
 2127|         devices._sync_device_egress_from_fib(dip, "speed test")
 2128|     except Exception:
 2129|         pass
 2130|     d = devices.get_device(dip) or {}
 2131|     rid = d.get("active_regime") or FORCE_LOCK or CURRENT_ACTIVE
 2132|     live_tun = ""
 2133|     if (d.get("egress") or "") == "tunnel":
 2134|         live_tun = (d.get("egress_dev") or "").strip()
 2135|     meta = REGIMES.get(rid) or {}
 2136|     if not meta:
 2137|         return jsonify({"error": "no VPN in use"}), 400
 2138|     name = meta.get("name") or rid
 2139|     saw_iface = resolve_iface(rid, meta)
 2140|     if _path_fakes_speed(rid, saw_iface):
 2141|         time.sleep(random.uniform(1.2, 2.0))
 2142|         return jsonify({
 2143|             "ok": True,
 2144|             "id": rid,
 2145|             "name": name,
 2146|             "mbps": _fake_speed_mbps(rid),
 2147|             "iface": saw_iface or "",
 2148|             "source": "dummy",
 2149|         })
 2150|     iface = ""
 2151|     if devices.is_client_wan_iface(saw_iface):
 2152|         iface = saw_iface
 2153|     elif devices.is_client_wan_iface(live_tun):
 2154|         iface = live_tun
 2155|         for r, m in REGIMES.items():
 2156|             if resolve_iface(r, m) == iface:
 2157|                 rid, meta, name = r, m, (m.get("name") or r)
 2158|                 break
 2159|     if not iface:
 2160|         log_event(f"SPEED {name}: no live iface fib={live_tun!r} saw={saw_iface!r}")
 2161|         return jsonify({
 2162|             "error": f"Could not measure {name} on the client path.",
 2163|             "id": rid,
 2164|             "name": name,
 2165|             "iface": "",
 2166|         }), 502
 2167|     mbps = None
 2168|     try:
 2169|         mbps = devices.measure_lan_client_mbps(iface)
 2170|     except Exception as e:
 2171|         log_event(f"SPEED ERROR {name} {iface}: {e}")
 2172|         mbps = None
 2173|     if mbps is None:
 2174|         return jsonify({
 2175|             "error": f"Could not measure {name} on the client path.",
 2176|             "id": rid,
 2177|             "name": name,
 2178|             "iface": iface or "",
 2179|         }), 502
 2180|     return jsonify({
 2181|         "ok": True,
 2182|         "id": rid,
 2183|         "name": name,
 2184|         "mbps": mbps,
 2185|         "iface": iface or "",
 2186|         "source": "lan-client",
 2187|     })

 2398| @app.route("/api/status")
 2399| def status():
 2400|     """Status for a specific device when device_ip is set (remote control).
 2401| 
 2402|     Without device_ip, fall back to the viewing client's LAN IP if known.
 2403|     Login required — unauthenticated callers do not get the device list.
 2404|     """
 2405|     user, err = require_user()
 2406|     if err:
 2407|         return err
 2408|     asked = (request.args.get("device_ip") or "").strip() or None
 2409|     dip = asked
 2410|     mark_viewer_online()
 2411|     if dip:
 2412|         dip = _resolve_bound_ip(dip, _mac_for_ip(dip), viewer_ip=client_ip()) or dip
 2413|         rows = []
 2414|         try:
 2415|             if user.get("role") == "admin":
 2416|                 for acct in accounts_db.list_accounts():
 2417|                     rows.extend(accounts_db.list_user_devices(acct["id"]))
 2418|             else:
 2419|                 rows = accounts_db.list_user_devices(user["id"])
 2420|         except Exception:
 2421|             rows = []
 2422|         for row in rows:
 2423|             if row.get("ip") in (asked, dip):
 2424|                 followed = _follow_bound_row(row, viewer_ip=client_ip())
 2425|                 dip = followed.get("ip") or dip
 2426|                 break
 2427|     if dip:
 2428|         if user.get("role") != "admin" and not _user_may_control_ip(user, dip):
 2429|             return jsonify({"error": "device not yours"}), 403
 2430|         if not devices.get_device(dip) and devices.is_steerable_client(dip):
 2431|             # Soft stub so UI can still show offline/selected device.
 2432|             # Do not sync leases or evaluate here — MikroTik / LAN-proof would wedge Flask.
 2433|             mac = _mac_for_ip(dip) if devices.is_lan_client(dip) else ""
 2434|             devices.ensure_runtime_device(
 2435|                 dip, name=devices.guess_name(dip, mac), mac=mac,
 2436|                 evidence="selected in UI", egress_reason="selected in UI",
 2437|                 online=False,
 2438|             )
 2439|     else:
 2440|         rip = client_ip()
 2441|         if devices.is_steerable_client(rip):
 2442|             if devices.get_device(rip):
 2443|                 dip = rip
 2444|     payload = build_status_payload(dip, user_id=user.get("id"))
 2445|     if user.get("role") != "admin":
 2446|         allowed = {
 2447|             (row.get("ip") or "")
 2448|             for row in accounts_db.list_user_devices(user["id"])
 2449|         }
 2450|         allowed.discard("")
 2451|         payload["devices"] = [
 2452|             d for d in (payload.get("devices") or [])
 2453|             if d.get("ip") in allowed
 2454|         ]
 2455|     return jsonify(payload)
 2456|
