 2546| @app.route("/api/gateway", methods=["POST"])
 2547| def set_gateway():
 2548|     global GATEWAY_ENABLED, GATEWAY_WANT_ON, FORCE_LOCK, UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN
 2549|     user, err = require_user()
 2550|     if err:
 2551|         return err
 2552|     if is_remote_viewer():
 2553|         return jsonify({
 2554|             "error": "Gateway is lab Wi-Fi only.",
 2555|             "gateway_enabled": GATEWAY_ENABLED,
 2556|         }), 403
 2557|     data = request.get_json(force=True, silent=True) or {}
 2558|     enabled = data.get("enabled")
 2559|     if enabled is None:
 2560|         return jsonify({"error": "need enabled true/false"}), 400
 2561|     GATEWAY_WANT_ON = bool(enabled)
 2562|     GATEWAY_ENABLED = bool(enabled)
 2563|     FORCE_LOCK = None
 2564|     devices._ctx["GATEWAY_ENABLED"] = GATEWAY_ENABLED
 2565|     persist_engine_state()
 2566|     if GATEWAY_ENABLED:
 2567|         log_event("GATEWAY ON")
 2568|         try:
 2569|             devices.ensure_forwarding()
 2570|         except Exception as e:
 2571|             log_event(f"FORWARDING WARN: {e}")
 2572|         if not devices.forwarding_ready():
 2573|             GATEWAY_ENABLED = False
 2574|             GATEWAY_WANT_ON = False
 2575|             devices._ctx["GATEWAY_ENABLED"] = False
 2576|             persist_engine_state()
 2577|             log_event("GATEWAY ON refused: Ubuntu cannot reach internet yet — DHCP stays .1")
 2578|             return jsonify({
 2579|                 "error": "Ubuntu is not forwarding to the internet yet. DHCP stays 192.168.50.1.",
 2580|                 "gateway_enabled": False,
 2581|                 "dhcp_gateway": "192.168.50.1",
 2582|             }), 409
 2583|         dhcp_ok = False
 2584|         try:
 2585|             dhcp_ok = bool(devices.set_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway())
 2586|         except Exception as e:
 2587|             log_event(f"DHCP SET WARN: {e}")

 2595|                 "gateway_enabled": False,
 2596|                 "dhcp_gateway": devices.get_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway(),
 2597|             }), 409
 2598|         UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN = True
 2599|         RECOMPUTE.set()
 2600|         switched, best = evaluate_once()
 2601|         devices.evaluate_all_devices()
 2602|         payload = build_status_payload()
 2603|         return jsonify({
 2604|             "status": "ok",
 2605|             "gateway_enabled": True,
 2606|             "dhcp_gateway": "192.168.50.10" if dhcp_ok else devices.get_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway(),
 2607|             "dhcp_ubuntu_gateway_applied": dhcp_ok,
 2608|             "active": REGIMES.get(best, {}).get("name") if best else None,
 2609|             "switched": switched,
 2610|             "metrics": payload["metrics"],
 2611|             "qos_mode": payload.get("qos_mode"),
 2612|             "qos_label": payload.get("qos_label"),
 2613|         })
 2614|     clear_host_route()
 2615|     devices.clear_device_routes()
 2616|     GATEWAY_WANT_ON = False
 2617|     GATEWAY_ENABLED = False
 2618|     devices._ctx["GATEWAY_ENABLED"] = False
 2619|     UBUNTU_DHCP_OPTED_IN = False
 2620|     persist_engine_state()
 2621|     try:
 2622|         # Internet first: clients should use MikroTik directly when adaptive GW is off
 2623|         devices.revert_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway()
 2624|     except Exception as e:

 2627|     payload = build_status_payload()

 2630|         "gateway_enabled": False,

 2632|         "active": None,

 2783| @app.route("/api/toggle-failure", methods=["POST"])
 2784| def toggle_failure():
 2785|     user, err = require_user()
 2786|     if err:
 2787|         return err
 2788|     global FORCE_LOCK
 2789|     data = request.get_json(force=True, silent=True) or {}
 2790|     rid = _resolve_regime_id(data.get("id") or data.get("regime") or data.get("name"))
 2791|     if not rid:
 2792|         rid = "my-wireguard"
 2793|     if rid not in REGIMES:
 2794|         return jsonify({"error": "unknown path"}), 400
 2795|     target_uid = int(user["id"])
 2796|     dip = (data.get("device_ip") or "").strip()
 2797|     if dip:
 2798|         owner = _owner_user_id_for_ip(dip)
 2799|         # Per-account simulation: never 403 the Fail buttons.
 2800|         # Admin on another user's device dashboard applies that owner's set.
 2801|         # Unknown or unbound IPs stay on the logged-in account.
 2802|         if owner is not None and (
 2803|             user.get("role") == "admin" or int(owner) == int(user["id"])
 2804|         ):
 2805|             target_uid = int(owner)
 2806|     current = fail_ids_for_user(target_uid)
 2807|     down = rid not in current
 2808|     if "failed" in data:
 2809|         down = bool(data.get("failed"))
 2810|     elif "simulated" in data or "down" in data:
 2811|         down = bool(data.get("simulated", data.get("down")))
 2812|     _set_simulated_for_user(target_uid, rid, down)
 2813|     FORCE_LOCK = None
 2814|     label = REGIMES[rid]["name"]
 2815|     log_event(f"FAILSIM user={target_uid} {label} simulated={'down' if down else 'up'}")
 2816|     RECOMPUTE.set()
 2817|     switched, best = evaluate_once()
 2818|     try:
 2819|         devices.evaluate_all_devices()
 2820|     except Exception:
 2821|         pass
 2822|     sim = simulated_status(target_uid)
 2823|     return jsonify({
 2824|         "status": "ok",
 2825|         "id": rid,
 2826|         "name": label,
 2827|         "simulated": down,
 2828|         "active": REGIMES.get(best, {}).get("name") if best else None,
 2829|         "switched": switched,
 2830|         **sim,
 2831|     })

 2833| 
 2834| def _iface_health(name: str) -> dict:
 2835|     """Honest iface snapshot: present / up / ipv4. Never invent UP."""
 2836|     if not name or not iface_exists(name):
 2837|         return {"name": name, "present": False, "up": False, "ipv4": None}
 2838|     r = run(["ip", "-br", "addr", "show", "dev", name], timeout=3)
 2839|     line = (r.stdout or "").strip()
 2840|     parts = line.split()
 2841|     state = parts[1] if len(parts) >= 2 else ""
 2842|     up = state in ("UP", "UNKNOWN")
 2843|     addrs = re.findall(r"(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", line)
 2844|     return {
 2845|         "name": name,
 2846|         "present": True,
 2847|         "up": up,
 2848|         "ipv4": addrs[0] if addrs else None,
 2849|         "state": state or None,
 2850|     }
 2851| 
 2852| 
 2853| def _proc_running(pattern: str) -> bool:
 2854|     r = run(["pgrep", "-f", pattern], timeout=3)
 2855|     return r.returncode == 0
 2856| 
 2857| 
 2858| def _unit_state(name: str) -> dict:
 2859|     active = (run(["systemctl", "is-active", name], timeout=3).stdout or "").strip()
 2860|     enabled = (run(["systemctl", "is-enabled", name], timeout=3).stdout or "").strip()
 2861|     return {"active": active == "active", "state": active or "missing", "enabled": enabled}

 2864| @app.route("/api/admin/health")

 2866|     user, err = require_admin()
 2867|     if err:
 2868|         return err
 2869|     containers = []
 2870|     for rid, meta in REGIMES.items():
 2871|         iface = resolve_iface(rid, meta)
 2872|         running = container_running(meta["container"])
 2873|         if rid == "my-nebula" and not running:
 2874|             running = _proc_running("/nebula") or _proc_running("nebula -config")
 2875|         if rid == "my-openvpn" and not running:
 2876|             running = _proc_running("openvpn --config") or _proc_running("ovpn-lab-c")
 2877|         if rid == "my-ipsec" and not running:
 2878|             running = _proc_running("charon") or iface_exists("vti0")
 2879|         if rid == "my-sstp" and not running:
 2880|             running = _proc_running("sstpc ")
 2881|         if rid == "my-openconnect" and not running:
 2882|             running = _proc_running("openconnect")
 2883|         if rid == "my-softether" and not running:
 2884|             running = _proc_running("vpnclient") or _proc_running("vpnserver")
 2885|         iface_up = iface_exists(iface)
 2886|         # lo is not an IPsec tunnel — do not report it as the path being up
 2887|         if rid == "my-ipsec" and iface in ("", "lo"):
 2888|             iface_up = False
 2889|         dummy = False
 2890|         if iface_up:
 2891|             det = run(["ip", "-details", "link", "show", "dev", iface], timeout=3)
 2892|             dummy = "dummy" in ((det.stdout or "") + (det.stderr or "")).lower()

 2916|             "lan_proof_reason": lan_why,

 3068|     user, err = require_admin()
 3069|     if err:
 3070|         return err
 3071|     global FORCE_LOCK, CURRENT_ACTIVE
 3072|     data = request.get_json(force=True, silent=True) or {}
 3073|     regime = data.get("regime")
 3074|     if regime not in REGIMES:
 3075|         for rid, meta in REGIMES.items():
 3076|             if meta["name"] == regime:
 3077|                 regime = rid
 3078|                 break
 3079|     if regime not in REGIMES:
 3080|         return jsonify({"error": "unknown regime"}), 400
 3081|     FORCE_LOCK = regime
 3082|     iface = resolve_iface(regime, REGIMES[regime])
 3083|     apply_host_route(iface, REGIMES[regime]["name"])
 3084|     CURRENT_ACTIVE = regime
 3085|     log_event(f"ADMIN FORCE -> {REGIMES[regime]['name']}")
 3086|     RECOMPUTE.set()
 3087|     evaluate_once()
 3088|     return jsonify({"status": "forced", "regime": regime})

   83| def _alloc_mark(*, remote: bool = False) -> int:
   84|     """fwmark == table. LAN starts at 110. Cloud ZT remotes start at 112 (never 110/111)."""
   85|     global _NEXT_MARK, _NEXT_REMOTE_MARK
   86|     used = _used_marks()
   87|     if remote:
   88|         m = max(_REMOTE_MARK_MIN, _NEXT_REMOTE_MARK)
   89|         while m in used or m in _KIYO_PHONE_TABLES:
   90|             m += 1
   91|         _NEXT_REMOTE_MARK = m + 1
   92|         return m
   93|     m = max(110, _NEXT_MARK)
   94|     while m in used:
   95|         m += 1
   96|     _NEXT_MARK = m + 1
   97|     return m

  106| def _iptables_ensure(args: list[str], insert: bool = True) -> None:
  107|     """Ensure an iptables rule exists. Check with -C; add with -I 1 or -A."""

  179| def _live_cloud_iface_cidrs() -> list:
  180|     """IPv4 networks on the cloud ZT iface (after Central Auth). Cached briefly."""
  181|     global _ZT_LIVE_CACHE
  182|     now = time.time()
  183|     cached_ts, cached_nets = _ZT_LIVE_CACHE
  184|     if cached_nets and (now - cached_ts) < 8.0:
  185|         return list(cached_nets)
  186|     nets: list = []
  187|     if not _ctx.get("run"):
  188|         _ZT_LIVE_CACHE = (now, nets)
  189|         return nets
  190|     try:
  191|         r = _run(["ip", "-o", "-4", "addr", "show", "dev", _ZT_CLOUD_IFACE], timeout=3)
  192|     except Exception:
  193|         _ZT_LIVE_CACHE = (now, nets)
  194|         return nets
  195|     blob = (r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")
  196|     for m in re.finditer(r"inet\s+(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)/(\d+)", blob):
  197|         ip_s, plen_s = m.group(1), m.group(2)
  198|         try:
  199|             plen = int(plen_s)
  200|             if plen == 32:
  201|                 plen = 24
  202|             net = ipaddress.ip_network(f"{ip_s}/{plen}", strict=False)
  203|             if net.prefixlen < 16:
  204|                 continue
  205|             if net in (_VIEWER_ZEROTIER_LAB,):
  206|                 continue
  207|             if str(net).startswith("192.168.50.") or str(net).startswith("192.168.56."):
  208|                 continue
  209|             if net not in nets:
  210|                 nets.append(net)
  211|         except ValueError:
  212|             continue
  213|     _ZT_LIVE_CACHE = (now, list(nets))
  214|     return nets

  246| def _host_without_port(host_header: str) -> str:
  247|     """Host header without port. Never reads X-Forwarded-*. """
  248|     raw = (host_header or "").strip().split("%")[0]
  249|     if not raw:
  250|         return ""
  251|     if raw.startswith("["):
  252|         end = raw.find("]")
  253|         return raw[1:end] if end > 0 else raw
  254|     if raw.count(":") == 1:
  255|         return raw.split(":", 1)[0].strip()
  256|     return raw

  259| def is_overlay_remote(remote_addr: str, host_header: str = "") -> bool:
  260|     """True for ZeroTier hallway: overlay /24 peer, or Host is Ubuntu ZT IP.
  261| 
  262|     Uses the TCP peer (remote_addr) and Host header only — not X-Forwarded-For.
  263|     """
  264|     rip = (remote_addr or "").split("%")[0].strip()

  266|         addr = ipaddress.ip_address(rip)

  274| def classify_viewer(ip: str) -> dict:
  275|     """Where the browser's TCP peer sits (quiet off-LAN UI line)."""
  276|     raw = (ip or "").split("%")[0].strip()
  277|     out = {"off_lan": False, "via": "unknown", "limited": False, "ip": raw}
  278|     if not raw:
  279|         return out
  280|     try:
  281|         addr = ipaddress.ip_address(raw)
  282|     except ValueError:
  283|         return out
  284|     if addr.is_loopback:
  285|         out["via"] = "local"
  286|         return out
  287|     for net in _VIEWER_ON_LAN:
  288|         if addr in net:
  289|             n = str(net)
  290|             if n.startswith("192.168.50."):
  291|                 out["via"] = "lan"
  292|             elif n.startswith("192.168.56."):
  293|                 out["via"] = "hostonly"
  294|             else:
  295|                 out["via"] = "lab"
  296|             return out
  297|     zt_nets = [_VIEWER_ZEROTIER_LAB, *zt_cloud_networks()]
  298|     for net in zt_nets:
  299|         if addr in net:
  300|             out["off_lan"] = True
  301|             out["via"] = "zerotier"
  302|             out["limited"] = True
  303|             return out
  304|     out["off_lan"] = True
  305|     out["via"] = "remote"
  306|     out["limited"] = True
  307|     return out
  308|

  330| def is_steerable_client(ip: str) -> bool:

  335| def live_ip_for_binding(
  336|     ip: str | None,
  337|     mac: str | None = None,
  338|     viewer_ip: str | None = None,
  339| ) -> str | None:
  340|     """Follow a bound PC when DHCP hands out a new 192.168.50.x (or overlay) address.
  341| 
  342|     Prefer an online row with the same MAC. If the saved IP is dead, adopt
  343|     this browser only when it is the same host (same MAC or same IP).
  344|     """
  345|     saved = (ip or "").strip() or None
  346|     mac_u = (mac or "").strip().upper()
  347|     viewer = (viewer_ip or "").strip() or None
  348|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
  349|         if not mac_u and saved and saved in DEVICES:
  350|             mac_u = (DEVICES[saved].get("mac") or "").strip().upper()
  351| 
  352|         def _online_with_mac(want: str) -> str | None:
  353|             for d in DEVICES.values():
  354|                 if not d.get("online"):
  355|                     continue
  356|                 if (d.get("mac") or "").strip().upper() == want:
  357|                     return d.get("ip")
  358|             return None
  359| 
  360|         if mac_u:
  361|             hit = _online_with_mac(mac_u)
  362|             if hit:
  363|                 return hit
  364|         if saved:
  365|             d = DEVICES.get(saved)
  366|             if d and d.get("online"):
  367|                 return saved
  368|         # Adopt this browser only when it is the same host (same MAC, or same IP).
  369|         # Never steal the only other Ethernet client on the MikroTik.
  370|         if viewer and is_steerable_client(viewer):
  371|             if viewer == saved:
  372|                 return viewer
  373|             vd = DEVICES.get(viewer) or {}
  374|             vmac = (vd.get("mac") or "").strip().upper()
  375|             if mac_u and vmac and vmac == mac_u:
  376|                 return viewer
  377|     return saved
  378| 
  379| 
  380| def _drop_vbox_stolen_mikrotik_route() -> None:
  381|     """Remove VBox NAT host-routes that steal lab WAN / MikroTik off enp0s8.
  382| 
  383|     VBox NAT DHCP advertises the Windows host DNS (192.168.50.1). networkd
  384|     then installs `192.168.50.1 via 10.0.2.2 dev enp0s3`. WireGuard's
  385|     endpoint is that same .1, so tunnel packets hairpin through Windows and
  386|     blackhole any client whose gateway is Ubuntu .10.
  387| 
  388|     Static DNS 8.8.8.8/1.1.1.1 on enp0s3 is worse: networkd installs
  389|     `8.8.8.8 via 10.0.2.2 proto dhcp`. forwarding_ready() then pings a
  390|     NAT blackhole even though MikroTik WAN on enp0s8 works.
  391|     """
  392|     _sudo_run(["ip", "route", "del", _MIKROTIK_IP, "via", _VBOX_NAT_GW, "dev", _NAT_UPLINK])
  393|     for host in ("8.8.8.8", "1.1.1.1"):
  394|         _sudo_run(["ip", "route", "del", host, "via", _VBOX_NAT_GW, "dev", _NAT_UPLINK])
  395|     # Dead leftover default on the same NIC; metric 50 would win if MikroTik's 10 is gone.
  396|     _sudo_run(["ip", "route", "del", "default", "via", "192.168.100.1", "dev", _LAN_IFACE])

  411|     ], insert=True)
