  449| def _restore_lab_default() -> None:
  450|     """Keep host default on MikroTik/enp0s8, not a VPN tunnel or VBox DNS route.
  451| 
  452|     Only delete VPN defaults that `ip route show table main` actually lists.
  453|     A bare `del default dev wg0` can also drop client table 110+ `default dev wg0`.
  454|     """
  455|     _drop_vbox_stolen_mikrotik_route()
  456|     for dev in _main_default_devs():
  457|         if _is_vpn_tunnel_dev(dev):
  458|             _sudo_run(["ip", "route", "del", "default", "dev", dev, "table", "main"])
  459|             _log(f"Uplink: dropped stolen main default via {dev}")
  460|     _sudo_run(["ip", "route", "del", "default", "via", "192.168.100.1", "dev", _LAN_IFACE, "table", "main"])
  461|     _sudo_run([
  462|         "ip", "route", "replace", "default",
  463|         "via", _MIKROTIK_IP, "dev", _LAN_IFACE, "metric", "10", "table", "main",
  464|     ])
  465|     _sudo_run([
  466|         "ip", "route", "replace", "default",
  467|         "via", _VBOX_NAT_GW, "dev", _NAT_UPLINK, "metric", "100", "table", "main",
  468|     ])
  469|     drop_leftover_host_dns_policy()
  470| 
  471| 
  472| def drop_leftover_host_dns_policy() -> None:
  473|     """Remove leftover `to 1.1.1.1 lookup 200` (demo rule that sent 1.1.1.1 via WG)."""
  474|     for _ in range(8):
  475|         r = _sudo_run(["ip", "rule", "del", "to", "1.1.1.1", "table", "200"])
  476|         if getattr(r, "returncode", 1) != 0:
  477|             break
  478| 
  479| 
  480| def protect_host_default() -> None:
  481|     """Public alias: Ubuntu default must stay via 192.168.50.1 dev enp0s8."""
  482|     _restore_lab_default()
  483| 
  484| 
  485| def ensure_forwarding() -> None:
  486|     """Make Ubuntu able to route LAN clients toward VPNs / MikroTik / NAT uplink."""
  487|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", "net.ipv4.ip_forward=1"])
  488|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", "net.ipv4.conf.all.forwarding=1"])
  489|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", "net.ipv4.conf.default.forwarding=1"])
  490|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", f"net.ipv4.conf.{_LAN_IFACE}.forwarding=1"])
  491|     # Loose rp_filter: WAN replies return on a tunnel while the host default
  492|     # is still MikroTik/enp0s8. Strict (1) drops those replies (LAN blackhole).
  493|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", "net.ipv4.conf.all.rp_filter=2"])
  494|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", "net.ipv4.conf.default.rp_filter=2"])
  495|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", f"net.ipv4.conf.{_LAN_IFACE}.rp_filter=2"])
  496|     tuns = list(_VPN_DEFAULT_IFACES)
  497|     zt_lab = zerotier_lab_client_iface()
  498|     if zt_lab and zt_lab not in tuns:
  499|         tuns.append(zt_lab)
  500|     for tun in tuns:
  501|         _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", f"net.ipv4.conf.{tun}.rp_filter=2"])
  502|         _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", f"net.ipv4.conf.{tun}.forwarding=1"])
  503|     _restore_lab_default()
  504| 
  505|     # NAT: MikroTik path (enp0s8) + VBox NAT uplink fallback (enp0s3)
  506|     for out_if in (_LAN_IFACE, _NAT_UPLINK):
  507|         check = _sudo_run(["iptables", "-t", "nat", "-C", "POSTROUTING", "-o", out_if, "-j", "MASQUERADE"])
  508|         if check.returncode != 0:
  509|             _sudo_run(["iptables", "-t", "nat", "-A", "POSTROUTING", "-o", out_if, "-j", "MASQUERADE"])
  510| 
  511|     # FORWARD accepts MUST be inserted before Docker's terminal DROP
  512|     for rule in (
  513|         ["FORWARD", "-i", _LAN_IFACE, "-j", "ACCEPT"],
  514|         ["FORWARD", "-o", _LAN_IFACE, "-m", "conntrack", "--ctstate", "RELATED,ESTABLISHED", "-j", "ACCEPT"],
  515|         ["FORWARD", "-i", _LAN_IFACE, "-o", _NAT_UPLINK, "-j", "ACCEPT"],
  516|         ["FORWARD", "-i", _NAT_UPLINK, "-o", _LAN_IFACE, "-m", "conntrack", "--ctstate", "RELATED,ESTABLISHED", "-j", "ACCEPT"],
  517|         ["FORWARD", "-i", _LAN_IFACE, "-o", _LAN_IFACE, "-j", "ACCEPT"],
  518|     ):
  519|         _iptables_ensure(rule, insert=True)
  520| 
  521|     # Default: MikroTik when reachable (give ARP/ICMP 2s). Do not flap to
  522|     # VBox NAT on a single 1s timeout — that is what made ping 8.8.8.8 fail.
  523|     mikrotik_up = _run(["ping", "-c", "1", "-W", "2", _MIKROTIK_IP], timeout=5).returncode == 0
  524|     if mikrotik_up:
  525|         _restore_lab_default()
  526|         _log("Uplink: MikroTik primary, VBox NAT backup")
  527|     else:
  528|         _sudo_run(["ip", "route", "del", "default", "via", _MIKROTIK_IP, "dev", _LAN_IFACE])
  529|         _sudo_run(["ip", "route", "replace", "default", "via", _VBOX_NAT_GW, "dev", _NAT_UPLINK, "metric", "10"])
  530|         _sudo_run(["ip", "route", "replace", "default", "via", _MIKROTIK_IP, "dev", _LAN_IFACE, "metric", "200"])
  531|         _log("Uplink: MikroTik unreachable — VBox NAT primary (internet first)")
  532| 
  533| 
  534| _FWD_READY_CACHE: tuple[float, bool] | None = None
  535| 
  536| 
  537| def _ping_wan_via_lan() -> bool:
  538|     """Ping 8.8.8.8 out enp0s8. Retry: lab RTT can exceed 300ms with occasional loss."""
  539|     for _ in range(3):
  540|         r = _run(
  541|             ["ping", "-I", _LAN_IFACE, "-c", "1", "-W", "2", "8.8.8.8"],
  542|             timeout=5,
  543|         )
  544|         if r.returncode == 0:
  545|             return True
  546|     return False
  547| 
  548| 
  549| def ping_wan_via_lan() -> bool:
  550|     """Public alias: Ubuntu can reach 8.8.8.8 via enp0s8 (not a VPN)."""
  551|     return _ping_wan_via_lan()
  552| 
  553| 
  554| def forwarding_ready() -> bool:
  555|     """True when Ubuntu can hairpin LAN clients out enp0s8 to the internet.
  556| 
  557|     Enough for DHCP .10: ip_forward + MASQUERADE out enp0s8 + ping 8.8.8.8
  558|     via the lab NIC (not via a VPN tunnel or VBox NAT DNS host-route).
  559|     Does not require vpn_carries_internet — client tables stay on MikroTik .1.
  560|     """
  561|     global _FWD_READY_CACHE
  562|     now = time.time()
  563|     if _FWD_READY_CACHE:
  564|         ts, ok = _FWD_READY_CACHE
  565|         ttl = 8.0 if ok else 0.4
  566|         if (now - ts) < ttl:
  567|             return ok
  568|     try:
  569|         _restore_lab_default()
  570|     except Exception:
  571|         try:
  572|             _drop_vbox_stolen_mikrotik_route()
  573|         except Exception:
  574|             pass
  575|     fwd = _run(["sysctl", "-n", "net.ipv4.ip_forward"], timeout=3)
  576|     if fwd.returncode != 0 or (fwd.stdout or "").strip() != "1":
  577|         _log("forwarding_ready FAIL: ip_forward != 1")
  578|         _FWD_READY_CACHE = (now, False)
  579|         return False
  580|     if not _masquerade_out(_LAN_IFACE):
  581|         _log("forwarding_ready FAIL: no MASQUERADE out enp0s8")
  582|         _FWD_READY_CACHE = (now, False)
  583|         return False
  584|     # Bind to enp0s8 so a stolen default via tun1/wg0 cannot fake WAN.
  585|     if not _ping_wan_via_lan():
  586|         _log("forwarding_ready FAIL: ping 8.8.8.8 via enp0s8")
  587|         _FWD_READY_CACHE = (now, False)
  588|         return False
  589|     _FWD_READY_CACHE = (now, True)
  590|     return True
  591| 
  592| 
  593| def set_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway(*, force: bool = False) -> bool:
  594|     """Point lab DHCP clients at Ubuntu so per-device VPN routes apply.
  595| 
  596|     Opt-in / safety: refuses unless forwarding is verified (or force=True).
  597|     Returns True if MikroTik DHCP gateway was set to Ubuntu.
  598|     """
  599|     if not force and not forwarding_ready():
  600|         _log(
  601|             f"DHCP gateway NOT set to {_GATEWAY_IP}: "
  602|             "forwarding/uplink not verified (internet-first safety)"
  603|         )
  604|         return False
  605|     set_cmd = (
  606|         f'/ip dhcp-server network set [find address="192.168.50.0/24"] '
  607|         f'gateway={_GATEWAY_IP} dns-server={_MIKROTIK_IP}'
  608|     )
  609|     gw = None
  610|     for _ in range(3):
  611|         _mikrotik(set_cmd, timeout=12)
  612|         gw = get_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway()
  613|         if gw == _GATEWAY_IP:
  614|             _log(f"DHCP gateway set to {_GATEWAY_IP} (per-device routing)")
  615|             return True
  616|         time.sleep(0.4)
  617|     _log(f"DHCP SET WARN: wanted {_GATEWAY_IP}, MikroTik still {gw}")
  618|     return False
  619| 
  620| 
  621| def ensure_mikrotik_dns_server() -> None:
  622|     """Point lab DHCP DNS at MikroTik so Auto can see client queries."""
  623|     try:
  624|         _mikrotik(
  625|             f'/ip dhcp-server network set [find address="192.168.50.0/24"] '
  626|             f"dns-server={_MIKROTIK_IP}"
  627|         )
  628|         _log(f"DHCP dns-server set to {_MIKROTIK_IP} (Auto DNS hints)")
  629|     except Exception as e:
  630|         _log(f"DHCP DNS WARN: {e}")
  631| 
  632| 
  633| def ensure_mikrotik_dns_query_log() -> None:
  634|     """Best-effort per-client DNS: RouterOS query-logging + a memory log rule."""
  635|     try:
  636|         _mikrotik("/ip dns set allow-remote-requests=yes")
  637|     except Exception:
  638|         pass
  639|     try:
  640|         # RouterOS 7 — ignored on v6
  641|         _mikrotik("/ip dns set query-logging=yes")
  642|     except Exception:
  643|         pass
  644|     try:
  645|         out = _mikrotik("/system logging print")
  646|         blob = (out or "").lower()
  647|         if "autovpn-dns" not in blob:
  648|             _mikrotik(
  649|                 '/system logging add topics=dns,!packet action=memory '
  650|                 'prefix=autovpn-dns'
  651|             )
  652|             _log("MikroTik DNS query log enabled (per-device Auto)")
  653|     except Exception as e:
  654|         _log(f"DNS LOG WARN: {e}")
  655| 
  656| 
  657| def ensure_mikrotik_lab() -> None:
  658|     """Idempotent lab DNS + hotspot off. Does not change the DHCP gateway."""
  659|     ensure_mikrotik_dns_server()
  660|     ensure_mikrotik_dns_query_log()
  661|     try:
  662|         _mikrotik("/ip dns set allow-remote-requests=yes")
  663|         out = _mikrotik("/ip dns static print")
  664|         wanted = ("autovpn", "autovpn.lab", "www.autovpn.lab")
  665|         missing = [n for n in wanted if n not in (out or "")]
  666|         for name in missing:
  667|             _mikrotik(
  668|                 f'/ip dns static add name={name} address={_GATEWAY_IP} '
  669|                 f"ttl=1d comment=AutoVPN-UI"
  670|             )
  671|         _mikrotik("/ip hotspot set [find] disabled=yes")
  672|         if missing:
  673|             _log("MikroTik static DNS added: " + ", ".join(missing))
  674|     except Exception as e:
  675|         _log(f"MikroTik lab DNS WARN: {e}")
  676| 
  677| 
  678| def revert_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway() -> bool:
  679|     """Restore MikroTik as DHCP default gateway so clients get internet without Ubuntu."""
  680|     try:
  681|         _mikrotik(
  682|             f'/ip dhcp-server network set [find address="192.168.50.0/24"] '
  683|             f'gateway={_MIKROTIK_IP} dns-server={_MIKROTIK_IP}'
  684|         )
  685|         _log(f"DHCP gateway reverted to {_MIKROTIK_IP} (direct MikroTik internet)")
  686|         return True
  687|     except Exception as e:
  688|         _log(f"DHCP REVERT FAILED: {e}")
  689|         return False
  690| 
  691| 
  692| def get_mikrotik_dhcp_gateway() -> str | None:
  693|     """Best-effort parse of current DHCP network gateway for 192.168.50.0/24."""
  694|     try:
  695|         out = _mikrotik("/ip dhcp-server network print detail")
  696|     except Exception:
  697|         return None
  698|     # Prefer the 192.168.50.0/24 block if present
  699|     blocks = re.split(r"\n\s*\d+\s+", "\n" + out)
  700|     for block in blocks:
  701|         if "192.168.50.0/24" not in block and "address=192.168.50.0/24" not in block:
  702|             continue
  703|         m = re.search(r"gateway=(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", block)
  704|         if m:
  705|             return m.group(1)
  706|     m = re.search(r"gateway=(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", out)
  707|     return m.group(1) if m else None
  708| 
  709| 
  710| _LEASE_FAIL_MARKERS = (
  711|     "timed out", "connection refused", "no route to host",
  712|     "connection reset", "permission denied", "connection timed out",
  713|     "network is unreachable",
  714| )
  715| _ONLINE_GRACE_SEC = 180.0
  716| 
  717| 
  718| def fetch_dhcp_leases() -> tuple[list[dict], bool]:
  719|     """Return (leases, ok). ok=False means MikroTik dump failed — do not mass-offline."""
  720|     try:
  721|         out = _mikrotik("/ip dhcp-server lease print detail")
  722|     except Exception:
  723|         return [], False
  724|     text = out or ""
  725|     low = text.lower()
  726|     if not text.strip():
  727|         return [], False
  728|     if any(m in low for m in _LEASE_FAIL_MARKERS) and "address=" not in low:
  729|         return [], False
  730|     blocks = re.split(r"\n\s*\d+\s+", "\n" + text)
  731|     leases = []
  732|     for block in blocks:
  733|         addr_m = re.search(r"address=(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", block)
  734|         if not addr_m:
  735|             continue
  736|         ip = addr_m.group(1)
  737|         if not ip.startswith("192.168.50."):
  738|             continue
  739|         if ip.endswith(".1") or ip == _GATEWAY_IP:
  740|             continue
  741|         mac_m = re.search(r"mac-address=([0-9A-Fa-f:]+)", block)
  742|         host_m = re.search(r'host-name="([^"]+)"', block) or re.search(r"host-name=([^\s]+)", block)
  743|         status_m = re.search(r"status=(\w+)", block)
  744|         leases.append({
  745|             "ip": ip,
  746|             "mac": (mac_m.group(1) if mac_m else "").upper(),
  747|             "name": (host_m.group(1) if host_m else "") or guess_name(ip, mac_m.group(1) if mac_m else ""),
  748|             "status": status_m.group(1) if status_m else "unknown",
  749|         })
  750|     return leases, True
  751| 
  752| 
  753| def parse_dhcp_leases() -> list[dict]:
  754|     leases, _ok = fetch_dhcp_leases()
  755|     return leases
  756| 
  757| 
  758| _LAB_PING_HOSTS = (
  759|     ("192.168.50.253", "Phone"),
  760|     ("192.168.50.254", "kiyo"),
  761| )
  762| _PING_LIVE: dict[str, tuple[float, bool]] = {}
  763| _PING_LIVE_TTL = 25.0
  764| 
  765| 
  766| def lab_host_is_reachable(ip: str) -> bool:
  767|     """ICMP to a known lab host. Cached. Fail closed. Not WAN / FORWARD proof."""
  768|     if not ip or not _ctx.get("run"):
  769|         return False
  770|     now = time.time()
  771|     cached = _PING_LIVE.get(ip)
  772|     if cached and (now - cached[0]) < _PING_LIVE_TTL:
  773|         return cached[1]
  774|     try:
  775|         r = _run(["ping", "-c", "1", "-W", "1", ip], timeout=3)
  776|         ok = r.returncode == 0
  777|     except Exception:
  778|         ok = False
  779|     _PING_LIVE[ip] = (now, ok)
  780|     return ok
  781| 
  782| 
  783| def lan_neighbors() -> dict[str, str]:
  784|     """IP -> MAC for lab neighbors that look alive (ARP/NDISC)."""
  785|     r = _run(["ip", "neigh", "show", "dev", _LAN_IFACE], timeout=3)
  786|     out = (r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")
  787|     found: dict[str, str] = {}
  788|     alive = {"REACHABLE", "DELAY", "STALE", "PROBE", "PERMANENT"}
  789|     for ln in out.splitlines():
  790|         # 192.168.50.253 lladdr aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff REACHABLE
  791|         m = re.search(
  792|             r"(192\.168\.50\.\d+)\s+.*lladdr\s+([0-9a-fA-F:]+)\s+(\w+)",
  793|             ln,
  794|         )
  795|         if not m:
  796|             continue
  797|         ip, mac, st = m.group(1), m.group(2).upper(), m.group(3).upper()
  798|         if ip.endswith(".1") or ip == _GATEWAY_IP:
  799|             continue
  800|         if st in alive:
  801|             found[ip] = mac
  802|     return found
  803| 
  804| 
  805| def _device_has_recent_traffic(ip: str) -> bool:
  806|     try:
  807|         return float(sample_device_lan_mbps(ip) or 0.0) >= 0.02
  808|     except Exception:
  809|         return False
  810| 
  811| 
  812| def guess_name(ip: str, mac: str) -> str:
  813|     if is_zt_cloud_client(ip):
  814|         return "Remote"
  815|     if ip.endswith(".254"):
  816|         return "kiyo"
  817|     if ip.endswith(".253"):
  818|         return "Phone"
  819|     short = mac.replace(":", "")[-4:] if mac else ip.split(".")[-1]
  820|     return f"Device-{short or ip.split('.')[-1]}"
  821| 
  822| 
  823| def _new_device_row(
  824|     ip: str,
  825|     *,
  826|     name: str,
  827|     mark: int,
  828|     mac: str = "",
  829|     evidence: str = "",
  830|     egress_reason: str = "",
  831|     online: bool = True,
  832|     last_seen: float | None = None,
  833| ) -> dict:
  834|     now = time.time()
  835|     if last_seen is None:
  836|         last_seen = now if online else 0.0
  837|     kind = "remote" if is_zt_cloud_client(ip) else "lan"
  838|     return {
  839|         "ip": ip,
  840|         "mac": mac or "",
  841|         "name": name or guess_name(ip, mac or ""),
  842|         "kind": kind,
  843|         "profile": "auto",
  844|         "auto_detected": "balanced",
  845|         "evidence": evidence or ("ZeroTier overlay" if kind == "remote" else "New device"),
  846|         "active_regime": "my-wireguard",
  847|         "active_name": "WireGuard",
  848|         "mark": mark,
  849|         "table": mark,
  850|         "pending": None,
  851|         "pending_hits": 0,
  852|         "last_switch": 0.0,
  853|         "last_seen": last_seen,
  854|         "online": online,
  855|         "metrics": [],
  856|         "custom_weights": None,
  857|         "force_regime": None,
  858|         "egress": "mikrotik",
  859|         "egress_via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
  860|         "egress_dev": _LAN_IFACE,
  861|         "egress_reason": egress_reason or (
  862|             "new remote — MikroTik until Gateway On" if kind == "remote"
  863|             else "new device — MikroTik until Gateway On"
  864|         ),
  865|     }
  866| 
  867| 
  868| def ensure_runtime_device(
  869|     ip: str,
  870|     *,
  871|     name: str = "",
  872|     mac: str = "",
  873|     evidence: str = "",
  874|     egress_reason: str = "",
  875|     online: bool = True,
  876|     rename: bool = False,
  877| ) -> dict:
  878|     """Create or touch a device. Overlay IPs get mark/table 112+ (never 110/111)."""
  879|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
  880|         return _ensure_runtime_device_locked(
  881|             ip,
  882|             name=name,
  883|             mac=mac,
  884|             evidence=evidence,
  885|             egress_reason=egress_reason,
  886|             online=online,
  887|             rename=rename,
  888|         )
  889| 
  890| 
  891| def _ensure_runtime_device_locked(
  892|     ip: str,
  893|     *,
  894|     name: str = "",
  895|     mac: str = "",
  896|     evidence: str = "",
  897|     egress_reason: str = "",
  898|     online: bool = True,
  899|     rename: bool = False,
  900| ) -> dict:
  901|     now = time.time()
  902|     if ip in DEVICES:
  903|         d = DEVICES[ip]
  904|         if mac:
  905|             d["mac"] = mac
  906|         if rename and name:
  907|             d["name"] = name
  908|         elif name and (not d.get("name") or str(d.get("name")).startswith("Device-")):
  909|             d["name"] = name
  910|         if online:
  911|             d["online"] = True
  912|             d["last_seen"] = now
  913|         if not d.get("kind"):
  914|             d["kind"] = "remote" if is_zt_cloud_client(ip) else "lan"
  915|         return d
  916|     mark = _alloc_mark(remote=is_zt_cloud_client(ip))
  917|     row = _new_device_row(
  918|         ip,
  919|         name=name or guess_name(ip, mac),
  920|         mark=mark,
  921|         mac=mac,
  922|         evidence=evidence,
  923|         egress_reason=egress_reason,
  924|         online=online,
  925|         last_seen=(now if online else 0.0),
  926|     )
  927|     DEVICES[ip] = row
  928|     _log(f"DEVICE ADD {row['name']} ({ip}) mark={mark}")
  929|     return row
  930| 
  931| 
  932| def zt_cloud_neighbors() -> dict[str, str]:
  933|     """IP -> MAC on the cloud overlay iface. Not DHCP. Empty if iface/runtime missing."""
  934|     if not _ctx.get("run"):
  935|         return {}
  936|     r = _run(["ip", "neigh", "show", "dev", _ZT_CLOUD_IFACE], timeout=3)
  937|     out: dict[str, str] = {}
  938|     alive = {"REACHABLE", "DELAY", "STALE", "PROBE", "PERMANENT"}
  939|     skip = _zt_cloud_skip_ips()
  940|     for ln in ((r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")).splitlines():
  941|         m = re.search(
  942|             r"(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)\s+.*lladdr\s+([0-9a-fA-F:]+)\s+(\w+)",
  943|             ln,
  944|         )
  945|         if not m:
  946|             continue
  947|         ip, mac, st = m.group(1), m.group(2).upper(), m.group(3).upper()
  948|         if ip in skip:
  949|             continue
  950|         if ip.startswith(("10.66.73.", "192.168.50.", "192.168.56.", "10.0.2.", "127.")):
  951|             continue
  952|         if st in alive:
  953|             out[ip] = mac
  954|     return out
  955| 
  956| 
  957| def _zt_overlay_ips_from_conntrack() -> set[str]:
  958|     found: set[str] = set()
  959|     try:
  960|         blob = _conntrack_blob()
  961|     except Exception:
  962|         return found
  963|     for m in re.finditer(r"\bsrc=(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)\b", blob or ""):
  964|         ip = m.group(1)
  965|         if is_zt_cloud_client(ip):
  966|             found.add(ip)
  967|     return found
  968| 
  969| 
  970| def zt_cloud_peers() -> dict[str, str]:
  971|     """Connected cloud ZT overlay IPs -> MAC (MAC may be empty). Not DHCP."""
  972|     out: dict[str, str] = {}
  973|     skip = _zt_cloud_skip_ips()
  974|     try:
  975|         out.update(zt_cloud_neighbors())
  976|     except Exception:
  977|         pass
  978|     try:
  979|         for ip in _zt_overlay_ips_from_conntrack():
  980|             out.setdefault(ip, "")
  981|     except Exception:
  982|         pass
  983|     return {ip: mac for ip, mac in out.items() if ip not in skip}
  984| 
  985| 
  986| def sync_devices_from_leases() -> None:
  987|     leases, lease_ok = fetch_dhcp_leases()
  988|     now = time.time()
  989|     neigh = {}
  990|     try:
  991|         neigh = lan_neighbors()
  992|     except Exception:
  993|         neigh = {}
  994|     zt_peers: dict[str, str] = {}
  995|     zt_ok = True
  996|     try:
  997|         zt_peers = zt_cloud_peers()
  998|     except Exception:
  999|         zt_ok = False
 1000|         zt_peers = {}
 1001|     seen = set()
 1002|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 1003|         for lease in leases:
 1004|             ip = lease["ip"]
 1005|             seen.add(ip)
 1006|             if ip not in DEVICES:
 1007|                 mark = _alloc_mark()
 1008|                 DEVICES[ip] = _new_device_row(
 1009|                     ip,
 1010|                     name=lease["name"],
 1011|                     mark=mark,
 1012|                     mac=lease["mac"],
 1013|                     evidence="New device",
 1014|                     egress_reason="new device — MikroTik until Gateway On",
 1015|                     last_seen=now,
 1016|                 )
 1017|                 _log(f"DEVICE ADD {lease['name']} ({ip}) mark={mark}")
 1018|             else:
 1019|                 d = DEVICES[ip]
 1020|                 d["mac"] = lease["mac"] or d["mac"]
 1021|                 if lease["name"] and not lease["name"].startswith("Device-"):
 1022|                     d["name"] = lease["name"]
 1023|                 d["last_seen"] = now
 1024|                 d["online"] = True
 1025|         # ARP / recent traffic can prove a device is live even if DHCP dump missed it
 1026|         for ip, mac in neigh.items():
 1027|             seen.add(ip)
 1028|             if ip in DEVICES:
 1029|                 d = DEVICES[ip]
 1030|                 d["mac"] = mac or d.get("mac")
 1031|                 d["last_seen"] = now
 1032|                 d["online"] = True
 1033|             else:
 1034|                 mark = _alloc_mark()
 1035|                 DEVICES[ip] = _new_device_row(
 1036|                     ip,
 1037|                     name=guess_name(ip, mac),
 1038|                     mark=mark,
 1039|                     mac=mac,
 1040|                     evidence="ARP on lab LAN",
 1041|                     egress_reason="ARP live — MikroTik until Gateway On",
 1042|                     last_seen=now,
 1043|                 )
 1044|                 _log(f"DEVICE ADD {DEVICES[ip]['name']} ({ip}) via ARP mark={mark}")
 1045|         # Phone/PC on MikroTik Wi-Fi may miss Ubuntu ARP when DHCP is .1.
 1046|         # Ping them if DHCP/ARP did not already prove live.
 1047|         for ip, default_name in _LAB_PING_HOSTS:
 1048|             if ip in seen:
 1049|                 continue
 1050|             if not lab_host_is_reachable(ip):
 1051|                 continue
 1052|             seen.add(ip)
 1053|             mac = neigh.get(ip, "")
 1054|             if ip not in DEVICES:
 1055|                 mark = _alloc_mark()
 1056|                 DEVICES[ip] = _new_device_row(
 1057|                     ip,
 1058|                     name=default_name,
 1059|                     mark=mark,
 1060|                     mac=mac,
 1061|                     evidence="ICMP on lab LAN",
 1062|                     egress_reason="ICMP live — MikroTik until Gateway On",
 1063|                     last_seen=now,
 1064|                 )
 1065|                 _log(f"DEVICE ADD {default_name} ({ip}) via ICMP mark={mark}")
 1066|             else:
 1067|                 d = DEVICES[ip]
 1068|                 d["last_seen"] = now
 1069|                 d["online"] = True
 1070|                 if mac:
 1071|                     d["mac"] = mac or d.get("mac")
 1072|                 if not d.get("name") or str(d.get("name")).startswith("Device-"):
 1073|                     d["name"] = default_name
 1074|         # Cloud ZeroTier remotes (live overlay /24, leftover 10.61.136.0/24).
 1075|         for ip, mac in zt_peers.items():
 1076|             seen.add(ip)
 1077|             if ip in DEVICES:
 1078|                 d = DEVICES[ip]
 1079|                 if mac:
 1080|                     d["mac"] = mac
 1081|                 d["kind"] = "remote"
 1082|                 d["last_seen"] = now
 1083|                 d["online"] = True
 1084|                 if not d.get("name") or str(d.get("name")).startswith("Device-"):
 1085|                     d["name"] = "Remote"
 1086|             else:
 1087|                 mark = _alloc_mark(remote=True)
 1088|                 DEVICES[ip] = _new_device_row(
 1089|                     ip,
 1090|                     name="Remote",
 1091|                     mark=mark,
 1092|                     mac=mac,
 1093|                     evidence="ZeroTier overlay",
 1094|                     egress_reason="ZT remote — MikroTik until Gateway On",
 1095|                     last_seen=now,
 1096|                 )
 1097|                 _log(f"DEVICE ADD Remote ({ip}) via ZT overlay mark={mark}")
 1098|         for ip, d in list(DEVICES.items()):
 1099|             if ip in seen:
 1100|                 continue
 1101|             if _device_has_recent_traffic(ip):
 1102|                 d["last_seen"] = now
 1103|                 d["online"] = True
 1104|                 continue
 1105|             last = float(d.get("last_seen") or 0.0)
 1106|             if last and (now - last) < _ONLINE_GRACE_SEC:
 1107|                 d["online"] = True
 1108|                 continue
 1109|             # Failed/empty MikroTik dump must not flip LAN clients offline
 1110|             if not lease_ok and not is_zt_cloud_client(ip):
 1111|                 continue
 1112|             # Failed overlay scan must not flip remotes offline
 1113|             if is_zt_cloud_client(ip) and not zt_ok:
 1114|                 continue
 1115|             d["online"] = False
 1116| 
 1117| 
 1118| _DEVICE_BYTE_SNAP: dict[str, tuple[int, float]] = {}
 1119| 
 1120| 
 1121| def _ensure_device_acct(ip: str) -> None:
 1122|     """Count download to this LAN IP. Must not MARK — that would steal replies."""
 1123|     if not ip or not is_lan_client(ip):
 1124|         return
 1125|     check = _sudo_run(
 1126|         ["iptables", "-t", "mangle", "-C", "FORWARD", "-d", ip, "-j", "ACCEPT"]
 1127|     )
 1128|     if check.returncode != 0:
 1129|         _sudo_run(
 1130|             ["iptables", "-t", "mangle", "-A", "FORWARD", "-d", ip, "-j", "ACCEPT"]
 1131|         )
 1132| 
 1133| 
 1134| def _mangle_bytes_for_ip(ip: str) -> int:
 1135|     """Sum mangle counters: PREROUTING -s (up) + FORWARD -d (down)."""
 1136|     total = 0
 1137|     for chain in ("PREROUTING", "FORWARD"):
 1138|         r = _sudo_run(
 1139|             ["iptables", "-t", "mangle", "-L", chain, "-n", "-v", "-x"], timeout=5,
 1140|         )
 1141|         out = (r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")
 1142|         for ln in out.splitlines():
 1143|             if ip not in ln:
 1144|                 continue
 1145|             nums = re.findall(r"\b(\d+)\b", ln)
 1146|             # pkts bytes ... source dest
 1147|             if len(nums) >= 2:
 1148|                 try:
 1149|                     total += int(nums[1])
 1150|                 except ValueError:
 1151|                     continue
 1152|     return total
 1153| 
 1154| 
 1155| def sample_device_lan_mbps(ip: str) -> float:
 1156|     """Per-device LAN→gateway throughput from iptables mangle counters (Mbps)."""
 1157|     now = time.time()
 1158|     cur = _mangle_bytes_for_ip(ip)
 1159|     prev = _DEVICE_BYTE_SNAP.get(ip)
 1160|     _DEVICE_BYTE_SNAP[ip] = (cur, now)
 1161|     if not prev:
 1162|         return 0.0
 1163|     prev_b, prev_ts = prev
 1164|     dt = now - prev_ts
 1165|     if dt <= 0.2:
 1166|         return 0.0
 1167|     # Counter reset / rule rebuild
 1168|     if cur < prev_b:
 1169|         return 0.0
 1170|     return max(0.0, (cur - prev_b) * 8.0 / dt / 1_000_000.0)
 1171| 
 1172| 
 1173| def _purge_source_marks(ip: str) -> None:
 1174|     """Remove every mangle MARK rule for this source (avoids duplicate marks)."""
 1175|     sudo = _ctx.get("SUDO") or "sudo"
 1176|     passwd = _ctx.get("SUDO_PASS") or "user"
 1177|     for chain in ("PREROUTING", "OUTPUT"):
 1178|         for _ in range(16):
 1179|             listed = _sudo_run(["iptables", "-t", "mangle", "-S", chain], timeout=5)
 1180|             victim = None
 1181|             for ln in (listed.stdout or "").splitlines():
 1182|                 # -A PREROUTING -s 192.168.50.254/32 -j MARK --set-xmark 0x6f/0xffffffff
 1183|                 if not ln.startswith("-A "):
 1184|                     continue
 1185|                 if f"-s {ip}/32" not in ln and f"-s {ip} " not in ln:
 1186|                     continue
 1187|                 if "-j MARK" not in ln:
 1188|                     continue
 1189|                 victim = ln
 1190|                 break
 1191|             if not victim:
 1192|                 break
 1193|             delete = victim.replace("-A ", "-D ", 1)
 1194|             # delete is like: -D PREROUTING -s 192.168.50.254/32 -j MARK ...
 1195|             _run(
 1196|                 [sudo, "-S", "iptables", "-t", "mangle", *delete.split()],
 1197|                 input_text=passwd + "\n",
 1198|                 timeout=5,
 1199|             )
 1200| 
 1201| 
 1202| # UDP/443 and UDP/80 are HTTP/3 / QUIC media — ports only, never decrypt.
 1203| _QUIC_PORTS = frozenset({443, 80})
 1204| # Dest-IP cache from DNS A records we actually saw (googlevideo / nflx / twitch).
 1205| _CDN_IP_NAME_KEYS = (
 1206|     "googlevideo", "nflxvideo", "nflxso", "nflximg", "ttvnw",
 1207| )
 1208| _CDN_IPS: dict[str, tuple[float, str]] = {}
 1209| _CDN_IP_TTL = 900.0
 1210| _CDN_RESOLVE_TS = 0.0
 1211| _CT_BLOB = ""
 1212| _CT_BLOB_TS = 0.0
 1213| 
 1214| 
 1215| def _empty_conn_stats() -> dict:
 1216|     return {
 1217|         "tcp": 0, "udp": 0, "total": 0, "udp_ratio": 0.0,
 1218|         "rx_mbps": 0.0, "tx_mbps": 0.0, "bidir": False,
 1219|         "udp_quic": 0, "udp_voice": 0, "voice_bidir": False, "cdn_hint": "",
 1220|     }
 1221| 
 1222| 
 1223| def _is_public_dst(addr: str) -> bool:
 1224|     if not addr or addr.startswith(("192.168.", "10.", "127.", "0.", "169.254.")):
 1225|         return False
 1226|     if addr.startswith("172."):
 1227|         try:
 1228|             second = int(addr.split(".")[1])
 1229|         except (ValueError, IndexError):
 1230|             return False
 1231|         if 16 <= second <= 31:
 1232|             return False
 1233|     return True
 1234| 
 1235| 
 1236| def _cdn_key_from_name(name: str) -> str:
 1237|     n = (name or "").lower().strip().strip('"')
 1238|     for k in _CDN_IP_NAME_KEYS:
 1239|         if k in n:
 1240|             return k
 1241|     return ""
 1242| 
 1243| 
 1244| def remember_video_cdn_ips(text: str) -> None:
 1245|     """Cache A records for googlevideo/nflx/twitch CDNs. Not decryption."""
 1246|     now = time.time()
 1247|     for ln in (text or "").splitlines():
 1248|         nm = re.search(r'name="?([^"\s]+)"?', ln, flags=re.I)
 1249|         ipm = re.search(
 1250|             r"\b(?:data|address)=(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)\b", ln, flags=re.I,
 1251|         )
 1252|         if not ipm:
 1253|             ipm = re.search(
 1254|                 r"\b([a-z0-9._-]+\.[a-z0-9._-]{2,})\b.*?\bA\b.*?(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)",
 1255|                 ln,
 1256|                 flags=re.I,
 1257|             )
 1258|             if ipm and not nm:
 1259|                 key = _cdn_key_from_name(ipm.group(1))
 1260|                 if key and _is_public_dst(ipm.group(2)):
 1261|                     _CDN_IPS[ipm.group(2)] = (now + _CDN_IP_TTL, key)
 1262|                 continue
 1263|         name = nm.group(1) if nm else ""
 1264|         addr = ipm.group(1) if ipm else ""
 1265|         key = _cdn_key_from_name(name)
 1266|         if key and _is_public_dst(addr):
 1267|             _CDN_IPS[addr] = (now + _CDN_IP_TTL, key)
 1268| 
 1269| 
 1270| def cdn_hint_for_ip(addr: str) -> str:
 1271|     rec = _CDN_IPS.get(addr or "")
 1272|     if not rec:
 1273|         return ""
 1274|     until, key = rec
 1275|     if until < time.time():
 1276|         _CDN_IPS.pop(addr, None)
 1277|         return ""
 1278|     return key
 1279| 
 1280| 
 1281| def resolve_video_cdn_names(names: tuple[str, ...], *, limit: int = 2) -> None:
 1282|     """Best-effort A lookups for CDN names already in cache. Never decrypts."""
 1283|     global _CDN_RESOLVE_TS
 1284|     now = time.time()
 1285|     if now - _CDN_RESOLVE_TS < 20.0:
 1286|         return
 1287|     wanted = [n for n in names if _cdn_key_from_name(n)]
 1288|     if not wanted:
 1289|         return
 1290|     try:
 1291|         import socket
 1292|     except Exception:
 1293|         return
 1294|     _CDN_RESOLVE_TS = now
 1295|     looked = 0
 1296|     for name in wanted:
 1297|         if looked >= limit:
 1298|             break
 1299|         key = _cdn_key_from_name(name)
 1300|         if not key:
 1301|             continue
 1302|         looked += 1
 1303|         try:
 1304|             infos = socket.getaddrinfo(name, None, socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
 1305|         except OSError:
 1306|             continue
 1307|         for info in infos:
 1308|             addr = info[4][0]
 1309|             if _is_public_dst(addr):
 1310|                 _CDN_IPS[addr] = (now + _CDN_IP_TTL, key)
 1311| 
 1312| 
 1313| def parse_quic_flows_conntrack(blob: str, ip: str) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
 1314|     """Original UDP/443 or UDP/80 tuples for this LAN src from nf_conntrack."""
 1315|     if not blob or not ip:
 1316|         return 0, []
 1317|     udp_quic = 0
 1318|     dsts: list[str] = []
 1319|     seen: set[str] = set()
 1320|     src_re = re.compile(
 1321|         r"src=" + re.escape(ip) + r"\s+dst=(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)\s+sport=\d+\s+dport=(\d+)"
 1322|     )
 1323|     for ln in blob.splitlines():
 1324|         if ip not in ln or "udp" not in ln.lower():
 1325|             continue
 1326|         m = src_re.search(ln)
 1327|         if not m:
 1328|             continue
 1329|         dst, port = m.group(1), int(m.group(2))
 1330|         if port not in _QUIC_PORTS or not _is_public_dst(dst):
 1331|             continue
 1332|         udp_quic += 1
 1333|         if dst not in seen:
 1334|             seen.add(dst)
 1335|             dsts.append(dst)
 1336|     return udp_quic, dsts
 1337| 
 1338| 
 1339| def parse_quic_flows_ros(blob: str, ip: str) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
 1340|     """UDP/443 or UDP/80 from a RouterOS connection terse dump for this LAN IP."""
 1341|     if not blob or not ip:
 1342|         return 0, []
 1343|     udp_quic = 0
 1344|     dsts: list[str] = []
 1345|     seen: set[str] = set()
 1346|     src_ok = re.compile(rf"src-address={re.escape(ip)}(?::|\b)", re.I)
 1347|     dst_re = re.compile(r"dst-address=(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+):(\d+)", re.I)
 1348|     for ln in blob.splitlines():
 1349|         if ip not in ln or "udp" not in ln.lower():
 1350|             continue
 1351|         if not src_ok.search(ln):
 1352|             continue
 1353|         m = dst_re.search(ln)
 1354|         if not m:
 1355|             continue
 1356|         dst, port = m.group(1), int(m.group(2))
 1357|         if port not in _QUIC_PORTS or not _is_public_dst(dst):
 1358|             continue
 1359|         udp_quic += 1
 1360|         if dst not in seen:
 1361|             seen.add(dst)
 1362|             dsts.append(dst)
 1363|     return udp_quic, dsts
 1364| 
 1365| 
 1366| def _conntrack_blob() -> str:
 1367|     """One nf_conntrack read per engine tick. Empty on Windows unit tests."""
 1368|     global _CT_BLOB, _CT_BLOB_TS
 1369|     now = time.time()
 1370|     if _CT_BLOB and (now - _CT_BLOB_TS) < 2.2:
 1371|         return _CT_BLOB
 1372|     blob = ""
 1373|     try:
 1374|         with open("/proc/net/nf_conntrack", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
 1375|             blob = f.read()
 1376|     except OSError:
 1377|         try:
 1378|             if _ctx.get("run"):
 1379|                 r = _sudo_run(["cat", "/proc/net/nf_conntrack"], timeout=3)
 1380|                 blob = r.stdout or ""
 1381|         except Exception:
 1382|             blob = ""
 1383|     _CT_BLOB = blob
 1384|     _CT_BLOB_TS = now
 1385|     return blob
 1386| 
 1387| 
 1388| def parse_udp_voice_conntrack(blob: str, ip: str) -> tuple[int, bool]:
 1389|     """Non-HTTP/3 public UDP for this LAN src, plus two-way reply.
 1390| 
 1391|     Discord voice / Go Live uses UDP to media hosts, often not port 443.
 1392|     """
 1393|     if not blob or not ip:
 1394|         return 0, False
 1395|     src_re = re.compile(
 1396|         r"src=" + re.escape(ip)
 1397|         + r"\s+dst=(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)\s+sport=\d+\s+dport=(\d+)"
 1398|     )
 1399|     count = 0
 1400|     bidir = False
 1401|     for ln in blob.splitlines():
 1402|         if ip not in ln or "udp" not in ln.lower():
 1403|             continue
 1404|         m = src_re.search(ln)
 1405|         if not m:
 1406|             continue
 1407|         dst, port = m.group(1), int(m.group(2))
 1408|         if not _is_public_dst(dst) or port in _QUIC_PORTS:
 1409|             continue
 1410|         count += 1
 1411|         low = ln.lower()
 1412|         if "unreplied" in low:
 1413|             continue
 1414|         reply_pkts = re.search(r"dst=" + re.escape(ip) + r".*?packets=(\d+)", ln)
 1415|         if reply_pkts and int(reply_pkts.group(1)) > 0:
 1416|             bidir = True
 1417|         elif "packets=" in low and ln.lower().count("packets=") >= 2:
 1418|             bidir = True
 1419|     return count, bidir
 1420| 
 1421| 
 1422| def forwarded_flow_signals(ip: str, ros_sample: str = "") -> dict:
 1423|     """Per-device QUIC-port + CDN dest-IP hints from forwarded flows."""
 1424|     udp_quic = 0
 1425|     udp_voice = 0
 1426|     voice_bidir = False
 1427|     dsts: list[str] = []
 1428|     try:
 1429|         n, d = parse_quic_flows_conntrack(_conntrack_blob(), ip)
 1430|         udp_quic += n
 1431|         dsts.extend(d)
 1432|     except Exception:
 1433|         pass
 1434|     try:
 1435|         n, d = parse_quic_flows_ros(ros_sample, ip)
 1436|         udp_quic += n
 1437|         for addr in d:
 1438|             if addr not in dsts:
 1439|                 dsts.append(addr)
 1440|     except Exception:
 1441|         pass
 1442|     try:
 1443|         udp_voice, voice_bidir = parse_udp_voice_conntrack(_conntrack_blob(), ip)
 1444|     except Exception:
 1445|         udp_voice, voice_bidir = 0, False
 1446|     hint = ""
 1447|     for dst in dsts:
 1448|         hint = cdn_hint_for_ip(dst)
 1449|         if hint:
 1450|             break
 1451|     return {
 1452|         "udp_quic": udp_quic,
 1453|         "udp_voice": udp_voice,
 1454|         "voice_bidir": voice_bidir,
 1455|         "cdn_hint": hint,
 1456|     }
 1457| 
 1458| 
 1459| def device_connection_stats(ip: str) -> dict:
 1460|     """Fast, non-blocking connection counts for one client.
 1461| 
 1462|     Full `/ip firewall connection print` can hang RouterOS SSH — never dump all.
 1463|     Overlay remotes never hit MikroTik; Ubuntu conntrack only.
 1464|     """
 1465|     tcp = udp = total = 0
 1466|     sample = ""
 1467|     if is_zt_cloud_client(ip):
 1468|         out = _empty_conn_stats()
 1469|         try:
 1470|             out.update(forwarded_flow_signals(ip, ""))
 1471|         except Exception:
 1472|             out.setdefault("udp_quic", 0)
 1473|             out.setdefault("cdn_hint", "")
 1474|         return out
 1475|     try:
 1476|         # count-only is cheap; treat missing/timeout as inactive
 1477|         raw = _mikrotik(
 1478|             f'/ip firewall connection print count-only where src-address~"{ip}"'
 1479|         )
 1480|         m = re.search(r"(\d+)", (raw or "").strip())
 1481|         if m:
 1482|             total = int(m.group(1))
 1483|         if total > 0:
 1484|             sample = _mikrotik(
 1485|                 f'/ip firewall connection print terse without-paging '
 1486|                 f'where src-address~"{ip}"'
 1487|             )
 1488|             sample = "\n".join((sample or "").splitlines()[:80])
 1489|             tcp = len(re.findall(r"\bprotocol=tcp\b|\btcp\b", sample, flags=re.I))
 1490|             udp = len(re.findall(r"\bprotocol=udp\b|\budp\b", sample, flags=re.I))
 1491|     except Exception:
 1492|         sample = sample or ""
 1493|     if tcp + udp == 0 and total:
 1494|         # Unknown mix — assume mostly TCP streaming-like
 1495|         tcp, udp = total, 0
 1496|     mix_total = max(tcp + udp, total)
 1497|     rx_mbps, tx_mbps = _conn_sample_rates_mbps(sample)
 1498|     out = {
 1499|         "tcp": tcp,
 1500|         "udp": udp,
 1501|         "total": mix_total,
 1502|         "udp_ratio": (udp / mix_total) if mix_total else 0.0,
 1503|         "rx_mbps": rx_mbps,
 1504|         "tx_mbps": tx_mbps,
 1505|         "bidir": rx_mbps >= 0.02 and tx_mbps >= 0.02,
 1506|     }
 1507|     try:
 1508|         out.update(forwarded_flow_signals(ip, sample))
 1509|     except Exception:
 1510|         out.setdefault("udp_quic", 0)
 1511|         out.setdefault("udp_voice", 0)
 1512|         out.setdefault("voice_bidir", False)
 1513|         out.setdefault("cdn_hint", "")
 1514|     voice_n = int(out.get("udp_voice") or 0)
 1515|     if voice_n:
 1516|         udp = max(udp, voice_n)
 1517|         mix_total = max(tcp + udp, total, mix_total)
 1518|         out["udp"] = udp
 1519|         out["total"] = mix_total
 1520|         out["udp_ratio"] = (udp / mix_total) if mix_total else 0.0
 1521|     if out.get("voice_bidir"):
 1522|         out["bidir"] = True
 1523|     return out
 1524| 
 1525| 
 1526| def _ros_rate_to_mbps(num: str, unit: str) -> float:
 1527|     try:
 1528|         n = float(num)
 1529|     except ValueError:
 1530|         return 0.0
 1531|     u = (unit or "").lower()
 1532|     if u == "bps":
 1533|         return n / 1_000_000.0
 1534|     if u in ("kbps", "kbit", "kb"):
 1535|         return n / 1_000.0
 1536|     return n
 1537| 
 1538| 
 1539| def _conn_sample_rates_mbps(sample: str) -> tuple[float, float]:
 1540|     """Best-effort (download, upload) Mbps from RouterOS connection terse."""
 1541|     rx = tx = 0.0
 1542|     for m in re.finditer(
 1543|         r"repl-rate=([0-9.]+)\s*(bps|kbps|mbps|kbit|mbit)?",
 1544|         sample or "",
 1545|         flags=re.I,
 1546|     ):
 1547|         rx += _ros_rate_to_mbps(m.group(1), m.group(2) or "bps")
 1548|     for m in re.finditer(
 1549|         r"orig-rate=([0-9.]+)\s*(bps|kbps|mbps|kbit|mbit)?",
 1550|         sample or "",
 1551|         flags=re.I,
 1552|     ):
 1553|         tx += _ros_rate_to_mbps(m.group(1), m.group(2) or "bps")
 1554|     return rx, tx
 1555| 
 1556| 
 1557| def parse_dns_query_logs(out: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
 1558|     """(client_ip, hostname) from RouterOS dns log lines."""
 1559|     found: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
 1560|     for ln in (out or "").splitlines():
 1561|         m = re.search(
 1562|             r"query from (192\.168\.50\.\d+)\S*.*?"
 1563|             r"([a-zA-Z0-9._-]+\.[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{2,})",
 1564|             ln,
 1565|             flags=re.I,
 1566|         )
 1567|         if not m:
 1568|             m = re.search(
 1569|                 r"(192\.168\.50\.\d+)\S*.*?(?:query|dns).*?"
 1570|                 r"([a-zA-Z0-9._-]+\.[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{2,})",
 1571|                 ln,
 1572|                 flags=re.I,
 1573|             )
 1574|         if not m:
 1575|             continue
 1576|         name = m.group(2).lower().rstrip(".")
 1577|         if name.count(".") < 1 or not re.search(r"[a-zA-Z]", name):
 1578|             continue
 1579|         if re.match(r"^\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+$", name):
 1580|             continue
 1581|         found.append((m.group(1), name))
 1582|     return found
 1583| 
 1584| 
 1585| # Strong in-game / matchmaking — Steam tray/store is NOT this
 1586| _REAL_GAME_DNS = (
 1587|     "hoyoverse", "mihoyo", "yuanshen", "genshin", "honkai",
 1588|     "riotgames", "riotcdn", "roblox", "rbxcdn", "rbx.com",
 1589|     "faceit", "wargaming", "epicgames",
 1590|     "steamserver", "valve",
 1591| )
 1592| _STEAM_TRAY_DNS = (
 1593|     "steamcommunity", "store.steampowered", "api.steampowered",
 1594|     "login.steampowered", "steampowered",
 1595| )
 1596| _GAME_DOWNLOAD_DNS = (
 1597|     "steamcontent", "steamstatic", "gamedl",
 1598| )
 1599| _VIDEO_CDN_KEYS = (
 1600|     "googlevideo", "ytimg", "ggpht", "youtu.be", "youtube-nocookie",
 1601|     "youtubei", "nflxvideo", "nflxso", "nflximg", "ttvnw",
 1602|     "akamaihd.net",
 1603| )
 1604| _REAL_CALL_DNS = (
 1605|     "zoom.us", "zoomcdn", "meet.google", "teams.microsoft",
 1606|     "skype", "webex", "stun.", "turn.", "webrtc",
 1607|     "discord.media", "discord-gateway", "rtc.discord",
 1608| )
 1609| _WEAK_CALL_DNS = (
 1610|     "discord.com", "discord.gg", "discordapp.com",
 1611| )
 1612| 
 1613| 
 1614| # Never appear in evidence / TICK / Auto UI. DNS may still classify internally.
 1615| _PUBLIC_EVIDENCE_BAN = (
 1616|     "roblox", "rbxcdn", "rbx.com", "youtube", "googlevideo", "ytimg", "youtu",
 1617|     "ggpht", "netflix", "nflx", "steam", "hoyoverse", "genshin", "mihoyo",
 1618|     "twitch", "ttvnw", "discord", "zoom", "webex", "akamai", "spotify",
 1619|     "tiktok", "riot", "epicgames", "honkai", "yuanshen",
 1620| )
 1621| 
 1622| 
 1623| def _mix_label(*, udp_ratio: float, udp_count: int, quic_https: bool) -> str:
 1624|     if quic_https:
 1625|         return "UDP/443"
 1626|     if udp_count >= 3 or udp_ratio >= 0.25:
 1627|         return "UDP"
 1628|     if udp_count <= 0 and udp_ratio <= 0:
 1629|         return "TCP"
 1630|     return "TCP/UDP"
 1631| 
 1632| 
 1633| def _metric_interactive(
 1634|     rate: float,
 1635|     *,
 1636|     udp_ratio: float,
 1637|     udp_count: int,
 1638|     bidir: bool,
 1639|     idle: bool,
 1640|     quic_https: bool = False,
 1641| ) -> bool:
 1642|     """Game/call-like: modest rate plus UDP mix and/or bidirectional flows."""
 1643|     if idle or rate >= 0.6:
 1644|         return False
 1645|     # YouTube HTTP/3 is UDP/443 without two-way call flows.
 1646|     if quic_https and not bidir and udp_count < 3:
 1647|         return False
 1648|     return (
 1649|         (udp_ratio >= 0.25 and udp_count >= 3)
 1650|         or (bidir and udp_count >= 3)
 1651|         or (bidir and udp_ratio >= 0.25)
 1652|     )
 1653| 
 1654| 
 1655| def _clue_is_public(text: str) -> bool:
 1656|     low = (text or "").lower()
 1657|     return not any(b in low for b in _PUBLIC_EVIDENCE_BAN)
 1658| 
 1659| 
 1660| def _is_game_download(name: str) -> bool:
 1661|     n = (name or "").lower()
 1662|     return any(k in n for k in _GAME_DOWNLOAD_DNS)
 1663| 
 1664| 
 1665| def _is_steam_tray_name(name: str) -> bool:
 1666|     n = (name or "").lower()
 1667|     if _is_game_download(n) or any(k in n for k in ("steamserver", "valve")):
 1668|         return False
 1669|     if any(k in n for k in _STEAM_TRAY_DNS):
 1670|         return True
 1671|     return "steam" in n
 1672| 
 1673| 
 1674| def _is_real_game_name(name: str) -> bool:
 1675|     n = (name or "").lower()
 1676|     if _is_game_download(n) or _is_steam_tray_name(n):
 1677|         return False
 1678|     return any(k in n for k in _REAL_GAME_DNS)
 1679| 
 1680| 
 1681| def _is_real_call_name(name: str) -> bool:
 1682|     n = (name or "").lower()
 1683|     if any(k in n for k in _WEAK_CALL_DNS) and not any(k in n for k in _REAL_CALL_DNS):
 1684|         return False
 1685|     return any(k in n for k in _REAL_CALL_DNS)
 1686| 
 1687| 
 1688| def _names_have_discord(names: tuple[str, ...] | list[str]) -> bool:
 1689|     keys = _WEAK_CALL_DNS + (
 1690|         "discord.media", "discord-gateway", "rtc.discord", "discordapp",
 1691|     )
 1692|     for n in names or ():
 1693|         low = (n or "").lower()
 1694|         if any(k in low for k in keys):
 1695|             return True
 1696|     return False
 1697| 
 1698| 
 1699| def _call_like_traffic(
 1700|     rate: float,
 1701|     udp_ratio: float,
 1702|     udp_count: int,
 1703|     *,
 1704|     bidir: bool = False,
 1705| ) -> bool:
 1706|     """VoIP/WebRTC: UDP plus two-way or a real call host. Chat HTTPS is not a call."""
 1707|     idle = rate < 0.08 and udp_count < 2 and not bidir
 1708|     if idle or rate >= 6.0:
 1709|         return False
 1710|     if bidir and (udp_count >= 2 or udp_ratio >= 0.2):
 1711|         return True
 1712|     if udp_count >= 3 and rate >= 0.08:
 1713|         return True
 1714|     return False
 1715| 
 1716| 
 1717| def filter_shared_dns_for_device(
 1718|     shared: tuple[str, ...],
 1719|     rate: float,
 1720|     *,
 1721|     udp_count: int = 0,
 1722|     sole: bool = False,
 1723|     unique_busy: bool = False,
 1724| ) -> tuple[str, ...]:
 1725|     """Do not paint an idle phone with the PC's Steam/YouTube cache.
 1726| 
 1727|     Per-IP logs win elsewhere. This is the fallback: only hand a shared
 1728|     hint to the device whose LAN rate / flows match that hint.
 1729|     """
 1730|     if not shared:
 1731|         return ()
 1732|     if sole:
 1733|         return tuple(shared)
 1734|     stream_k = tuple(_ctx.get("STREAMING_DNS") or ()) + _VIDEO_CDN_KEYS
 1735|     game_k = tuple(_ctx.get("GAMING_DNS") or ()) + _REAL_GAME_DNS + _STEAM_TRAY_DNS
 1736|     call_k = tuple(_ctx.get("CALL_DNS") or ()) + _REAL_CALL_DNS + _WEAK_CALL_DNS
 1737|     dl_k = tuple(_ctx.get("DOWNLOAD_DNS") or ()) + _GAME_DOWNLOAD_DNS
 1738|     video_like = rate >= 0.22
 1739|     bulk_like = rate >= 1.5
 1740|     game_like = (0.08 <= rate < 0.45) or (udp_count >= 3 and rate < 1.0)
 1741|     out: list[str] = []
 1742|     seen: set[str] = set()
 1743|     for n in shared:
 1744|         keep = False
 1745|         is_video = any(k in n for k in stream_k)
 1746|         is_dl = _is_game_download(n) or any(k in n for k in dl_k)
 1747|         is_game = any(k in n for k in game_k) and not is_dl
 1748|         is_call = _is_real_call_name(n) or any(k in n for k in call_k)
 1749|         is_discord = _names_have_discord((n,))
 1750|         if is_discord:
 1751|             keep = True
 1752|         elif is_video and video_like:
 1753|             keep = True
 1754|         elif is_dl and bulk_like:
 1755|             keep = True
 1756|         elif (is_game or is_call) and (game_like or udp_count >= 2):
 1757|             keep = True
 1758|         if keep and n not in seen:
 1759|             seen.add(n)
 1760|             out.append(n)
 1761|     return tuple(out)
 1762| 
 1763| 
 1764| def classify_from_signals(
 1765|     rate: float,
 1766|     dns: tuple[str, ...] = (),
 1767|     *,
 1768|     udp_ratio: float = 0.0,
 1769|     udp_count: int = 0,
 1770|     extra_clues: list[str] | None = None,
 1771|     bulk_sustained: bool = False,
 1772|     bidir: bool = False,
 1773|     quic_https: bool = False,
 1774|     cdn_ip_hint: str = "",
 1775|     rx_mbps: float = 0.0,
 1776|     tx_mbps: float = 0.0,
 1777| ) -> tuple[str, str]:
 1778|     """Map live traffic shape to a profile. DNS/CDN may help; UI stays metric-only.
 1779| 
 1780|     Idle / light / Discord chat + tabs → Everyday browsing.
 1781|     YouTube/Netflix CDN while still pulling → Video streaming.
 1782|     Discord voice / Go Live (bidirectional call UDP or upload) → Gaming & calls.
 1783|     Sustained high rate / bulk → Big downloads.
 1784|     """
 1785|     votes = {"balanced": 1.0, "gaming": 0.0, "streaming": 0.0, "downloads": 0.0}
 1786|     clues: list[str] = []
 1787| 
 1788|     stream_k = tuple(_ctx.get("STREAMING_DNS") or ()) + _VIDEO_CDN_KEYS
 1789|     game_k = tuple(_ctx.get("GAMING_DNS") or ()) + _REAL_GAME_DNS
 1790|     call_k = tuple(_ctx.get("CALL_DNS") or ()) + _REAL_CALL_DNS
 1791|     dl_k = tuple(_ctx.get("DOWNLOAD_DNS") or ()) + _GAME_DOWNLOAD_DNS
 1792| 
 1793|     def _hit(keys, pred=None) -> str | None:
 1794|         if not keys:
 1795|             return None
 1796|         for n in dns:
 1797|             if any(k in n for k in keys) and (pred is None or pred(n)):
 1798|                 return n
 1799|         return None
 1800| 
 1801|     video_cdn = _hit(_VIDEO_CDN_KEYS)
 1802|     if not video_cdn and cdn_ip_hint:
 1803|         video_cdn = _cdn_key_from_name(cdn_ip_hint) or cdn_ip_hint.lower()
 1804|     sticky_stream = _hit(
 1805|         stream_k,
 1806|         pred=lambda n: not any(k in n for k in _VIDEO_CDN_KEYS),
 1807|     )
 1808|     dl_hit = _hit(dl_k)
 1809|     real_game_hit = next((n for n in dns if _is_real_game_name(n)), None)
 1810|     steam_tray_hit = next((n for n in dns if _is_steam_tray_name(n)), None)
 1811|     call_hit = next((n for n in dns if _is_real_call_name(n)), None)
 1812|     if not call_hit:
 1813|         call_hit = _hit(call_k, pred=lambda n: not any(w in n for w in _WEAK_CALL_DNS))
 1814|     game_hit = _hit(game_k, pred=lambda n: not _is_game_download(n))
 1815|     discord_app = _names_have_discord(dns)
 1816| 
 1817|     idle = rate < 0.08 and udp_count < 2 and not bidir
 1818|     play_floor = 0.22
 1819|     rx = float(rx_mbps or 0.0)
 1820|     tx = float(tx_mbps or 0.0)
 1821|     if rx <= 0 and tx <= 0 and rate > 0:
 1822|         rx = float(rate)
 1823|     download_heavy = rx >= 0.22 and rx > max(tx, 0.01) * 2.5
 1824|     upload_heavy = tx >= 0.2 and tx >= rx * 0.35
 1825|     video_like = 0.22 <= rate < 6.0
 1826|     bulk = (rate >= 8.0 or bulk_sustained) and not video_cdn
 1827|     game_activity = (not idle) and rate < 0.6
 1828|     mix = _mix_label(udp_ratio=udp_ratio, udp_count=udp_count, quic_https=quic_https)
 1829|     metric_game = _metric_interactive(
 1830|         rate,
 1831|         udp_ratio=udp_ratio,
 1832|         udp_count=udp_count,
 1833|         bidir=bidir,
 1834|         idle=idle,
 1835|         quic_https=quic_https,
 1836|     )
 1837|     steam_live = (
 1838|         bool(steam_tray_hit)
 1839|         and rate >= 0.08
 1840|         and (udp_count >= 3 or udp_ratio >= 0.25)
 1841|     )
 1842|     if steam_live and not real_game_hit:
 1843|         real_game_hit = steam_tray_hit
 1844| 
 1845|     leftover_video_on_gamer = (
 1846|         bool(video_cdn)
 1847|         and game_activity
 1848|         and not video_like
 1849|         and bool(real_game_hit)
 1850|         and not quic_https
 1851|     )
 1852|     pulling_video = bool(video_cdn) or bool(cdn_ip_hint)
 1853|     leftover_site = bool(sticky_stream) and download_heavy
 1854|     # Discord chat + extra tabs must not count as video. Everything else in
 1855|     # the 0.22–6 Mbps band (YouTube hairpin included) still does.
 1856|     if discord_app:
 1857|         watching = (
 1858|             rate >= 0.45
 1859|             and not leftover_video_on_gamer
 1860|             and not upload_heavy
 1861|             and (pulling_video or leftover_site or download_heavy)
 1862|         )
 1863|     else:
 1864|         watching = (
 1865|             (pulling_video or leftover_site)
 1866|             and rate >= play_floor
 1867|             and not leftover_video_on_gamer
 1868|             and not upload_heavy
 1869|         )
 1870|         if video_like and not dl_hit and not watching and not leftover_video_on_gamer:
 1871|             watching = True
 1872|     live_call_udp = (
 1873|         not idle
 1874|         and rate < 6.0
 1875|         and (
 1876|             (bidir and (udp_count >= 2 or udp_ratio >= 0.2))
 1877|             or (udp_count >= 3 and rate >= 0.08)
 1878|         )
 1879|     )
 1880|     golive = upload_heavy and rate < 8.0 and not bool(video_cdn)
 1881|     if discord_app and (live_call_udp or golive) and not pulling_video:
 1882|         watching = False
 1883|     call_active = (
 1884|         not watching
 1885|         and not bulk
 1886|         and (
 1887|             (
 1888|                 bool(call_hit)
 1889|                 and _call_like_traffic(rate, udp_ratio, udp_count, bidir=bidir)
 1890|             )
 1891|             or (discord_app and (live_call_udp or golive))
 1892|             or (golive and live_call_udp)
 1893|         )
 1894|     )
 1895| 
 1896|     if watching:
 1897|         if video_cdn:
 1898|             votes["streaming"] += 8.0
 1899|         elif sticky_stream:
 1900|             votes["streaming"] += 4.0
 1901|         if quic_https:
 1902|             votes["streaming"] += 2.0
 1903|         if video_like and not dl_hit:
 1904|             votes["streaming"] += 4.0
 1905|         votes["streaming"] += 2.0
 1906|         votes["gaming"] = 0.0
 1907|         clues.append(f"steady downstream {rate:.1f} Mbps")
 1908|         if quic_https:
 1909|             clues.append("UDP/443")
 1910|         elif mix != "TCP":
 1911|             clues.append(mix)
 1912|     elif bulk:
 1913|         votes["downloads"] += 6.0
 1914|         clues.append(f"bulk {rate:.1f} Mbps")
 1915|         if dl_hit:
 1916|             votes["downloads"] += 4.0
 1917|         if mix == "TCP":
 1918|             clues.append("TCP")
 1919|         elif mix != "TCP/UDP":
 1920|             clues.append(mix)
 1921|     else:
 1922|         gaming_ready = bool(call_active or (real_game_hit and not idle) or metric_game)
 1923|         if call_active:
 1924|             votes["gaming"] += 6.0
 1925|         if real_game_hit and not idle:
 1926|             votes["gaming"] += 6.0
 1927|         elif game_hit and 0.08 <= rate < 0.6 and not call_active:
 1928|             votes["gaming"] += 1.2
 1929|         elif metric_game:
 1930|             votes["gaming"] += 6.0
 1931|         if (
 1932|             (real_game_hit or call_active or metric_game)
 1933|             and udp_ratio >= 0.25
 1934|             and udp_count >= 3
 1935|         ):
 1936|             votes["gaming"] += 1.5
 1937|         if dl_hit and rate >= 1.5:
 1938|             votes["downloads"] += 4.0
 1939| 
 1940|         if gaming_ready:
 1941|             clues.append(f"interactive {mix}")
 1942|             if rate < 0.6:
 1943|                 clues.append("low rate")
 1944|             clues.append(f"{rate:.2f} Mbps")
 1945|             if bidir:
 1946|                 clues.append("bidir")
 1947|         elif dl_hit and rate >= 1.5:
 1948|             clues.append(f"bulk {rate:.1f} Mbps")
 1949|         elif rate >= 0.15:
 1950|             clues.append(f"light {mix}")
 1951|             clues.append(f"{rate:.1f} Mbps")
 1952|         else:
 1953|             clues.append("idle / light use")
 1954| 
 1955|     for extra in extra_clues or ():
 1956|         if extra and extra not in clues and "flows" not in extra and _clue_is_public(extra):
 1957|             clues.append(extra)
 1958| 
 1959|     best = max(votes, key=votes.get)
 1960|     if votes[best] < 2.5:
 1961|         best = "balanced"
 1962|         useful = [
 1963|             c for c in clues
 1964|             if _clue_is_public(c) and any(
 1965|                 x in c for x in (
 1966|                     "Mbps", "UDP", "TCP", "interactive", "bulk", "idle",
 1967|                     "steady", "bidir", "light",
 1968|                 )
 1969|             )
 1970|         ]
 1971|         clues = useful[:3] if useful else ["idle / light use"]
 1972| 
 1973|     seen: set[str] = set()
 1974|     ordered: list[str] = []
 1975|     for c in clues:
 1976|         if c not in seen and _clue_is_public(c):
 1977|             seen.add(c)
 1978|             ordered.append(c)
 1979|     return best, " · ".join(ordered[:4]) if ordered else "idle / light use"
 1980| 
 1981| 
 1982| def auto_hysteresis_need(current: str, guess: str, evidence: str) -> tuple[int, float]:
 1983|     """Hits + gap before Auto commits. Fast onto video; faster off it when idle."""
 1984|     if current == "gaming" and guess == "streaming":
 1985|         return 1, 0.0
 1986|     if guess == "streaming":
 1987|         return 1, 0.0
 1988|     if current == "streaming" and guess == "downloads":
 1989|         return 2, 8.0
 1990|     if current == "streaming" and guess == "balanced":
 1991|         return 1, 0.0
 1992|     if guess == "gaming":
 1993|         return 2, 8.0
 1994|     if guess == "downloads":
 1995|         return 2, 6.0
 1996|     if current != "balanced" and guess == "balanced":
 1997|         return 1, 2.0
 1998|     return 2, 6.0
 1999| 
 2000| 
 2001| def _device_is_moving(rate: float, stats: dict) -> bool:
 2002|     rx = float(stats.get("rx_mbps") or 0.0)
 2003|     tx = float(stats.get("tx_mbps") or 0.0)
 2004|     udp = int(stats.get("udp") or 0)
 2005|     return (
 2006|         rate >= 0.12 or rx >= 0.12 or tx >= 0.12
 2007|         or int(stats.get("total") or 0) >= 8
 2008|         or (udp >= 3 and (rate >= 0.08 or bool(stats.get("bidir"))))
 2009|     )
 2010| 
 2011| 
 2012| def lan_clients_all_idle() -> bool:
 2013|     """True when we know LAN devices and none of them are moving.
 2014| 
 2015|     Empty snapshot is not idle — do not force Everyday browsing on an
 2016|     unknown lab. Used by host Auto so leftover video-CDN cache cannot
 2017|     paint streaming while kiyo/phone sit idle on stage.
 2018|     """
 2019|     try:
 2020|         snap = _snapshot_device_activity()
 2021|     except Exception:
 2022|         return False
 2023|     if not snap:
 2024|         return False
 2025|     return not any(_device_is_moving(r, st) for r, st in snap.values())
 2026| 
 2027| 
 2028| _ACTIVITY_CACHE: dict[str, tuple[float, float, dict]] = {}
 2029| _ACTIVITY_CACHE_TS = 0.0
 2030| _WAN_LAN_CACHE: tuple[float, float, float] | None = None
 2031| 
 2032| 
 2033| def _snapshot_device_activity() -> dict[str, tuple[float, dict]]:
 2034|     """One rate + flow sample per online IP per engine tick (avoids double-count)."""
 2035|     global _ACTIVITY_CACHE_TS
 2036|     now = time.time()
 2037|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 2038|         online = [d["ip"] for d in DEVICES.values() if d.get("online")]
 2039|     if _ACTIVITY_CACHE and (now - _ACTIVITY_CACHE_TS) < 2.2:
 2040|         return {ip: (r, st) for ip, (_t, r, st) in _ACTIVITY_CACHE.items() if ip in online}
 2041|     snap: dict[str, tuple[float, dict]] = {}
 2042|     for other in online:
 2043|         try:
 2044|             r = float(sample_device_lan_mbps(other) or 0.0)
 2045|         except Exception:
 2046|             r = 0.0
 2047|         try:
 2048|             st = device_connection_stats(other)
 2049|         except Exception:
 2050|             st = {
 2051|                 "tcp": 0, "udp": 0, "total": 0, "udp_ratio": 0.0,
 2052|                 "rx_mbps": 0.0, "tx_mbps": 0.0, "bidir": False,
 2053|             }
 2054|         r = max(
 2055|             r,
 2056|             float(st.get("rx_mbps") or 0.0),
 2057|             float(st.get("tx_mbps") or 0.0),
 2058|         )
 2059|         snap[other] = (r, st)
 2060|         _ACTIVITY_CACHE[other] = (now, r, st)
 2061|     _ACTIVITY_CACHE_TS = now
 2062|     return snap
 2063| 
 2064| 
 2065| def _wan_lan_rates() -> tuple[float, float]:
 2066|     global _WAN_LAN_CACHE
 2067|     now = time.time()
 2068|     if _WAN_LAN_CACHE and (now - _WAN_LAN_CACHE[0]) < 2.2:
 2069|         return _WAN_LAN_CACHE[1], _WAN_LAN_CACHE[2]
 2070|     wan_fn = _ctx.get("mikrotik_wan_rx_mbps")
 2071|     lan_fn = _ctx.get("sample_local_rx_mbps")
 2072|     try:
 2073|         wan = float(wan_fn() or 0.0) if wan_fn else 0.0
 2074|     except Exception:
 2075|         wan = 0.0
 2076|     try:
 2077|         lan = float(lan_fn() or 0.0) if lan_fn else 0.0
 2078|     except Exception:
 2079|         lan = 0.0
 2080|     _WAN_LAN_CACHE = (now, wan, lan)
 2081|     return wan, lan
 2082| 
 2083| 
 2084| def classify_device(ip: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
 2085|     """Per-device guess: this IP's rate/flows + its DNS, never a sibling's."""
 2086|     snap = _snapshot_device_activity()
 2087|     rate, stats = snap.get(ip, (0.0, {"tcp": 0, "udp": 0, "total": 0, "udp_ratio": 0.0}))
 2088|     online_ips = list(snap.keys())
 2089|     busy_ips = [o for o, (r, st) in snap.items() if _device_is_moving(r, st)]
 2090|     sole = len(online_ips) <= 1
 2091|     unique_busy = len(busy_ips) == 1 and busy_ips[0] == ip
 2092|     rx = float(stats.get("rx_mbps") or 0.0)
 2093|     tx = float(stats.get("tx_mbps") or 0.0)
 2094|     rate = max(rate, rx, tx)
 2095|     wan, lan = _wan_lan_rates()
 2096|     agg = max(wan, lan)
 2097|     cdn_hint = str(stats.get("cdn_hint") or "")
 2098|     bidir = bool(stats.get("bidir")) or (rx >= 0.02 and tx >= 0.02)
 2099| 
 2100|     per_ip_fn = _ctx.get("mikrotik_dns_hints_for_ip")
 2101|     dns_fn: Callable = _ctx.get("mikrotik_dns_hints")
 2102|     per_ip: tuple[str, ...] = ()
 2103|     shared: tuple[str, ...] = ()
 2104|     # Cafe DNS never hits MikroTik. Do not paint a ZT remote with LAN DNS.
 2105|     if not is_zt_cloud_client(ip):
 2106|         if per_ip_fn:
 2107|             try:
 2108|                 per_ip = tuple(per_ip_fn(ip) or ())
 2109|             except Exception:
 2110|                 per_ip = ()
 2111|         if dns_fn:
 2112|             try:
 2113|                 shared = tuple(dns_fn() or ())
 2114|             except Exception:
 2115|                 shared = ()
 2116| 
 2117|     def _classifiable(names: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
 2118|         keys = (
 2119|             tuple(_ctx.get("STREAMING_DNS") or ())
 2120|             + tuple(_ctx.get("GAMING_DNS") or ())
 2121|             + tuple(_ctx.get("CALL_DNS") or ())
 2122|             + _VIDEO_CDN_KEYS
 2123|             + _REAL_GAME_DNS
 2124|             + _STEAM_TRAY_DNS
 2125|             + _REAL_CALL_DNS
 2126|             + _WEAK_CALL_DNS
 2127|         )
 2128|         return any(any(k in n for k in keys) for n in names)
 2129| 
 2130|     attributed = filter_shared_dns_for_device(
 2131|         shared,
 2132|         rate,
 2133|         udp_count=int(stats.get("udp") or 0),
 2134|         sole=sole,
 2135|         unique_busy=unique_busy,
 2136|     )
 2137|     if per_ip and _classifiable(per_ip):
 2138|         # This IP's queries win. Sole device may also keep shared cache hints
 2139|         # (there is no sibling to steal from).
 2140|         dns = tuple(dict.fromkeys(per_ip + attributed)) if sole else per_ip
 2141|     else:
 2142|         dns = attributed
 2143| 
 2144|     # VPN hairpin: mangle looks idle while WAN is YouTube. Copy house rate
 2145|     # onto this PC when WAN is actually pulling. Discord chat is not video
 2146|     # unless WAN is already in the play band with CDN evidence.
 2147|     house_pull = 0.22 <= agg < 6.0
 2148|     quic = int(stats.get("udp_quic") or 0) >= 1
 2149|     discordish = _names_have_discord(dns)
 2150|     videoish = bool(cdn_hint) or any(
 2151|         any(k in (n or "").lower() for k in _VIDEO_CDN_KEYS) for n in dns
 2152|     )
 2153|     if house_pull and discordish and not videoish and agg < 0.45:
 2154|         pass
 2155|     elif house_pull and (sole or unique_busy or quic or cdn_hint or videoish or not busy_ips):
 2156|         rate = max(rate, agg)
 2157|         rx = max(rx, agg)
 2158| 
 2159|     return classify_from_signals(
 2160|         rate,
 2161|         dns,
 2162|         udp_ratio=float(stats.get("udp_ratio") or 0.0),
 2163|         udp_count=int(stats.get("udp") or 0),
 2164|         bidir=bidir,
 2165|         quic_https=int(stats.get("udp_quic") or 0) >= 1,
 2166|         cdn_ip_hint=cdn_hint,
 2167|         rx_mbps=rx,
 2168|         tx_mbps=tx,
 2169|     )
 2170| 
 2171| 
 2172| def lan_forwarded_video_signals() -> tuple[bool, str]:
 2173|     """UDP/443 or CDN dest while the house WAN is actually pulling."""
 2174|     try:
 2175|         snap = _snapshot_device_activity()
 2176|     except Exception:
 2177|         return False, ""
 2178|     try:
 2179|         wan, lan = _wan_lan_rates()
 2180|     except Exception:
 2181|         wan, lan = 0.0, 0.0
 2182|     pulling = max(wan, lan) >= 0.22
 2183|     quic = False
 2184|     hint = ""
 2185|     for _ip, (rate, st) in snap.items():
 2186|         has_quic = int(st.get("udp_quic") or 0) >= 1
 2187|         h = str(st.get("cdn_hint") or "")
 2188|         if not pulling and not _device_is_moving(rate, st):
 2189|             continue
 2190|         if h and not hint:
 2191|             hint = h
 2192|         if has_quic and pulling:
 2193|             quic = True
 2194|     if not pulling:
 2195|         return False, ""
 2196|     return quic, hint
 2197|

 2201|         return d.get("auto_detected") or "balanced"
 2202|     if d["profile"] == "custom":
 2203|         return "custom"
 2204|     return d["profile"]

 2207| def score_regimes_for_profile(profile_key: str, custom_weights=None, failed_ids=None) -> tuple[dict, list]:
 2208|     """Same live-first rank as host evaluate_once (shared cache + formula)."""
 2209|     rank = _ctx.get("rank_regimes_live")
 2210|     if rank:
 2211|         return rank(profile_key, custom_weights, failed_ids=failed_ids)
 2212|     return {}, []

 2214| 
 2215| # Hardcoded leftovers only. Runtime dummy-type ifaces are also blocked.
 2216| _LAB_ONLY_IFACES: set[str] = set()
 2217| # Overlay/mesh that can ping -I (or even pass LAN-proof) but must never be
 2218| # a LAN client's default. SAW may still rank them. nebula1 blackholed
 2219| # Windows when SAW swapped onto it (unsafe_routes ≠ forwarded internet).
 2220| # wg0 here is MikroTik wg-server → the same ether1 WAN; encrypting LAN
 2221| # clients through it capped Fast.com (~70 Mbps vs ~400 off the hEX).
 2222| _CLIENT_EGRESS_DENY = frozenset({"nebula1"})
 2223| _NOT_TUNNEL_IFACES = {"", "lo", "enp0s3", "enp0s8", "enp0s9"}
 2224| _VPN_INET_CACHE: dict[str, tuple[float, bool]] = {}
 2225| # Consecutive ping -I misses before LAN-proof is dropped / client table pinned.
 2226| # One blink used to yank table 110+ back to MikroTik while the UI still said tunnel.
 2227| _PING_I_MISS: dict[str, int] = {}
 2228| _PING_I_MISS_LIMIT = 3
 2229| # Global kill-switch leftover. Do NOT set True — that would allow every
 2230| # tunnel. Client egress is per-iface via lan_forward_proven() only.
 2231| _ALLOW_VPN_CLIENT_EGRESS = False
 2232| _LAN_SUBNET = "192.168.50.0/24"
 2233| _LAN_PROOF_TTL = 30.0
 2234| _LAN_PROOF_CACHE: dict[str, tuple[float, bool, str]] = {}
 2235| _LAN_PROOF_LOCK = threading.Lock()
 2236| _LAN_PROOF_STAMP_DIR = "/run/autovpn-lan-proof"
 2237| _CLIENT_TABLE_MIN = 110
 2238| _CLIENT_TABLE_MAX = 125
 2239| _PROOF_NS = "agproof"
 2240| _PROOF_VETH_H = "agph"
 2241| _PROOF_VETH_N = "agpn"
 2242| _PROOF_HOST_IP = "10.99.50.1"
 2243| _PROOF_NS_IP = "10.99.50.2"
 2244| _PROOF_TABLE = 250
 2245| _PROOF_RULE_PREF = 49
 2246| _PROOF_DSTS = ("8.8.8.8", "1.1.1.1")
 2247| # Prove in this order. Dummy SSTP / cloud ZT / nebula overlay are skipped.
 2248| _PROOF_ORDER = ("wg0", "tun0", "tun1", "vti0", "tap_soft", "sstp0")

 2250| 
 2251| def _iface_is_dummy(iface: str) -> bool:
 2252|     """True when the kernel iface is a dummy (10.66 loop), not a real tunnel."""
 2253|     if not iface:
 2254|         return False
 2255|     run = _ctx.get("run")
 2256|     if not run:
 2257|         return False
 2258|     try:
 2259|         r = run(["ip", "-details", "link", "show", "dev", iface], timeout=3)
 2260|     except Exception:
 2261|         return False
 2262|     blob = ((r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")).lower()
 2263|     return "dummy" in blob
 2264| 
 2265| 
 2266| # Leftover dummy peers on lo — never a WAN next-hop.
 2267| _DUMMY_NEXTHOPS = {"10.66.70.1", "10.66.71.1"}
 2268| # vpn-stack-up.sh policy tables (never device marks 110+).
 2269| _POLICY_TABLE = {
 2270|     "tap_soft": 30,
 2271|     "tun0": 68,
 2272|     "vti0": 67,
 2273|     "tun1": 71,
 2274|     "nebula1": 72,
 2275|     "sstp0": 70,
 2276|     "ppp0": 70,
 2277|     "ppp1": 70,
 2278| }
 2279| 
 2280| 
 2281| def _policy_table_id(iface: str) -> int | None:
 2282|     if not iface:
 2283|         return None
 2284|     if iface in _POLICY_TABLE:
 2285|         return _POLICY_TABLE[iface]
 2286|     if iface.startswith("zt"):
 2287|         ip_s = _iface_ipv4(iface)
 2288|         if ip_s and ip_s.startswith(_ZT_LAB_NET):
 2289|             return 74
 2290|     return None
 2291| 
 2292| 
 2293| def _iface_ipv4(iface: str) -> str | None:
 2294|     r = _run(["ip", "-o", "-4", "addr", "show", "dev", iface], timeout=3)
 2295|     m = re.search(r"inet\s+(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", r.stdout or "")
 2296|     return m.group(1) if m else None
 2297| 
 2298| 
 2299| def _tunnel_nexthop(iface: str) -> str | None:
 2300|     """On-link gateway for TAP /24 or GRE/OpenVPN p-t-p. Never dummy/lo.
 2301| 
 2302|     SoftEther TAP needs via 192.168.30.1 (SecureNAT). `default dev tap_soft`
 2303|     ARPs for 8.8.8.8 on L2 and LAN proof fails. WireGuard /32 is cryptokey
 2304|     routed — return None so callers use `default dev iface`.
 2305|     """
 2306|     if not iface or _iface_is_dummy(iface):
 2307|         return None
 2308|     r = _run(["ip", "-o", "-4", "addr", "show", "dev", iface], timeout=3)
 2309|     blob = r.stdout or ""
 2310|     peer = re.search(
 2311|         r"inet\s+\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:/\d+)?\s+peer\s+(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)",
 2312|         blob,
 2313|     )
 2314|     if peer:
 2315|         hop = peer.group(1)
 2316|         return None if hop in _DUMMY_NEXTHOPS else hop
 2317|     cidr = re.search(r"inet\s+(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)/(\d+)", blob)
 2318|     if not cidr:
 2319|         return None
 2320|     ip_s, plen = cidr.group(1), int(cidr.group(2))
 2321|     if plen >= 32 or plen != 24:
 2322|         return None
 2323|     a, b, c, _d = (int(x) for x in ip_s.split("."))
 2324|     gw = f"{a}.{b}.{c}.1"
 2325|     if gw == ip_s or gw in _DUMMY_NEXTHOPS:
 2326|         return None
 2327|     return gw
 2328| 
 2329| 
 2330| def _ensure_tunnel_policy(iface: str) -> None:
 2331|     """oif/from policy so TAP/subnet tunnels use their on-link gw, not main.
 2332| 
 2333|     Never installs 8.8.8.8/32 on main. Never touches device mark tables 110+.
 2334|     """
 2335|     table = _policy_table_id(iface)
 2336|     via = _tunnel_nexthop(iface)
 2337|     src = _iface_ipv4(iface)
 2338|     if not table or not src:
 2339|         return
 2340|     if via:
 2341|         _sudo_ip(["route", "replace", f"{via}/32", "dev", iface, "table", str(table)])
 2342|         _sudo_ip(["route", "replace", "default", "via", via, "dev", iface, "table", str(table)])
 2343|     else:
 2344|         _sudo_ip(["route", "replace", "default", "dev", iface, "table", str(table)])
 2345|     for _ in range(8):
 2346|         if _sudo_ip(["rule", "del", "table", str(table)]).returncode != 0:
 2347|             break
 2348|     _sudo_ip(["rule", "add", "oif", iface, "table", str(table), "priority", str(table)])
 2349|     _sudo_ip(["rule", "add", "from", src, "table", str(table), "priority", str(table + 1)])
 2350| 
 2351| 
 2352| def _ping_i_misses(iface: str) -> int:
 2353|     return int(_PING_I_MISS.get(iface) or 0)
 2354| 
 2355| 
 2356| def _record_ping_i(iface: str, ok: bool) -> None:
 2357|     """Update ping -I cache. Drop LAN-proof only after consecutive misses."""
 2358|     if not iface:
 2359|         return
 2360|     now = time.time()
 2361|     if ok:
 2362|         _PING_I_MISS[iface] = 0
 2363|         _VPN_INET_CACHE[iface] = (now, True)
 2364|         return
 2365|     _PING_I_MISS[iface] = _ping_i_misses(iface) + 1
 2366|     _VPN_INET_CACHE[iface] = (now, False)
 2367|     if _ping_i_misses(iface) >= _PING_I_MISS_LIMIT:
 2368|         invalidate_lan_proof(
 2369|             iface,
 2370|             f"{iface} ping -I 8.8.8.8 failed ×{_ping_i_misses(iface)}",
 2371|         )
 2372| 
 2373| 
 2374| def _vpn_carries_internet(iface: str) -> bool:
 2375|     """True only if this tunnel can actually reach the public internet."""
 2376|     if not iface or not _iface_exists(iface):
 2377|         return False
 2378|     if iface in _LAB_ONLY_IFACES or iface in _NOT_TUNNEL_IFACES:
 2379|         return False
 2380|     if _iface_is_dummy(iface):
 2381|         return False
 2382|     now = time.time()
 2383|     cached = _VPN_INET_CACHE.get(iface)
 2384|     # Keep PASS longer; do not lock FAIL for 15s (that pinned LAN clients on MikroTik).
 2385|     if cached:
 2386|         age = now - cached[0]
 2387|         if cached[1] and age < 15.0:
 2388|             return True
 2389|         if (not cached[1]) and age < 2.0:
 2390|             return False
 2391|     try:
 2392|         _ensure_tunnel_policy(iface)
 2393|     except Exception:
 2394|         pass
 2395|     ok = False
 2396|     for _ in range(3):
 2397|         r = _run(["ping", "-I", iface, "-c", "1", "-W", "2", "8.8.8.8"], timeout=5)
 2398|         if r.returncode == 0:
 2399|             ok = True
 2400|             break
 2401|     _record_ping_i(iface, ok)
 2402|     return ok
 2403| 
 2404| 
 2405| def vpn_carries_internet(iface: str) -> bool:
 2406|     """Public alias: tunnel may carry WAN only if 8.8.8.8 answers on that iface."""
 2407|     return _vpn_carries_internet(iface)
 2408|
