 2410| def _host_default_safe() -> bool:
 2411|     """True when Ubuntu's own default is not a VPN tunnel."""
 2412|     dev = _route_get_dev("8.8.8.8")
 2413|     if not dev:
 2414|         return False
 2415|     return not _is_vpn_tunnel_dev(dev)
 2416| 
 2417| 
 2418| def _persist_lan_proof_stamp(iface: str, ok: bool, reason: str) -> None:
 2419|     """Stamp file so the bash watchdog can see LAN-proof FAIL without Python."""
 2420|     try:
 2421|         os.makedirs(_LAN_PROOF_STAMP_DIR, exist_ok=True)
 2422|         safe = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]", "_", iface)
 2423|         path = os.path.join(_LAN_PROOF_STAMP_DIR, safe)
 2424|         with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
 2425|             f.write(("PASS" if ok else "FAIL") + "\n" + (reason or "") + "\n")
 2426|     except Exception:
 2427|         pass
 2428| 
 2429| 
 2430| def _cache_lan_proof(iface: str, ok: bool, reason: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
 2431|     _LAN_PROOF_CACHE[iface] = (time.time(), ok, reason)
 2432|     _persist_lan_proof_stamp(iface, ok, reason)
 2433|     return ok, reason
 2434| 
 2435| 
 2436| def invalidate_lan_proof(iface: str, reason: str) -> None:
 2437|     """Drop a PASS cache immediately — never keep LAN clients on a dead ping -I.
 2438| 
 2439|     Does not refresh _VPN_INET_CACHE (that TTL must be allowed to expire so a
 2440|     recovered tunnel can be probed again).
 2441|     """
 2442|     if not iface:
 2443|         return
 2444|     _LAN_PROOF_CACHE[iface] = (time.time(), False, reason)
 2445|     _persist_lan_proof_stamp(iface, False, reason)
 2446| 
 2447| 
 2448| def _forward_pkts(in_if: str, out_if: str) -> int:
 2449|     """FORWARD packet counter for -i in_if -o out_if (0 if missing)."""
 2450|     r = _sudo_run(["iptables", "-L", "FORWARD", "-n", "-v", "-x"], timeout=5)
 2451|     total = 0
 2452|     for ln in (r.stdout or "").splitlines():
 2453|         if in_if not in ln or out_if not in ln:
 2454|             continue
 2455|         nums = re.findall(r"\b(\d+)\b", ln)
 2456|         if nums:
 2457|             try:
 2458|                 total += int(nums[0])
 2459|             except ValueError:
 2460|                 continue
 2461|     return total
 2462| 
 2463| 
 2464| def _ensure_lan_probe_ns() -> str | None:
 2465|     """Idempotent veth netns that looks like a LAN client. None on failure."""
 2466|     if not _ctx.get("run"):
 2467|         return "no runtime"
 2468|     listed = _sudo_run(["ip", "netns", "list"], timeout=5)
 2469|     if _PROOF_NS not in (listed.stdout or ""):
 2470|         add = _sudo_run(["ip", "netns", "add", _PROOF_NS], timeout=5)
 2471|         if add.returncode != 0:
 2472|             return f"netns add failed: {(add.stderr or add.stdout or '').strip()}"
 2473|     link = _run(["ip", "link", "show", "dev", _PROOF_VETH_H], timeout=3)
 2474|     if link.returncode != 0:
 2475|         _sudo_run(["ip", "link", "add", _PROOF_VETH_H, "type", "veth", "peer", "name", _PROOF_VETH_N])
 2476|         _sudo_run(["ip", "link", "set", _PROOF_VETH_N, "netns", _PROOF_NS])
 2477|     _sudo_run(["ip", "addr", "replace", f"{_PROOF_HOST_IP}/24", "dev", _PROOF_VETH_H])
 2478|     _sudo_run(["ip", "link", "set", _PROOF_VETH_H, "up"])
 2479|     _sudo_run(["ip", "netns", "exec", _PROOF_NS, "ip", "addr", "replace",
 2480|                f"{_PROOF_NS_IP}/24", "dev", _PROOF_VETH_N], timeout=8)
 2481|     _sudo_run(["ip", "netns", "exec", _PROOF_NS, "ip", "link", "set", _PROOF_VETH_N, "up"], timeout=8)
 2482|     _sudo_run(["ip", "netns", "exec", _PROOF_NS, "ip", "link", "set", "lo", "up"], timeout=8)
 2483|     _sudo_run(["ip", "netns", "exec", _PROOF_NS, "ip", "route", "replace",
 2484|                "default", "via", _PROOF_HOST_IP], timeout=8)
 2485|     # ip netns exec does not see systemd-resolved on 127.0.0.53 — curls to
 2486|     # hostnames otherwise fail and the gauge shows "Could not measure".
 2487|     _sudo_run(["mkdir", "-p", f"/etc/netns/{_PROOF_NS}"], timeout=5)
 2488|     _sudo_run([
 2489|         "bash", "-c",
 2490|         f"printf 'nameserver 8.8.8.8\\nnameserver 1.1.1.1\\n' > /etc/netns/{_PROOF_NS}/resolv.conf",
 2491|     ], timeout=5)
 2492|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", f"net.ipv4.conf.{_PROOF_VETH_H}.rp_filter=2"])
 2493|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", f"net.ipv4.conf.{_PROOF_VETH_H}.forwarding=1"])
 2494|     _iptables_ensure(["FORWARD", "-i", _PROOF_VETH_H, "-j", "ACCEPT"], insert=True)
 2495|     _iptables_ensure(
 2496|         ["FORWARD", "-o", _PROOF_VETH_H,
 2497|          "-m", "conntrack", "--ctstate", "RELATED,ESTABLISHED", "-j", "ACCEPT"],
 2498|         insert=True,
 2499|     )
 2500|     # Policy table used only by the probe source — never table 110 / main.
 2501|     _sudo_ip(["rule", "del", "from", f"{_PROOF_NS_IP}/32", "table", str(_PROOF_TABLE)])
 2502|     _sudo_ip([
 2503|         "rule", "add", "from", f"{_PROOF_NS_IP}/32",
 2504|         "table", str(_PROOF_TABLE), "priority", str(_PROOF_RULE_PREF),
 2505|     ])
 2506|     return None
 2507| 
 2508| 
 2509| def _probe_lan_forward(iface: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
 2510|     """Simulate a LAN client and require FORWARD+NAT out *this* iface.
 2511| 
 2512|     ping -I is not used. Fail closed. Must not steal Ubuntu's default.
 2513|     """
 2514|     if not iface or iface in _NOT_TUNNEL_IFACES or iface in _LAB_ONLY_IFACES:
 2515|         return False, f"{iface} is not a VPN tunnel"
 2516|     if not _iface_exists(iface):
 2517|         return False, f"{iface} missing"
 2518|     if _iface_is_dummy(iface):
 2519|         return False, f"{iface} is dummy (never WAN)"
 2520|     fwd = _run(["sysctl", "-n", "net.ipv4.ip_forward"], timeout=3)
 2521|     if fwd.returncode != 0 or (fwd.stdout or "").strip() != "1":
 2522|         return False, "ip_forward != 1"
 2523|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", f"net.ipv4.conf.{iface}.rp_filter=2"])
 2524|     _sudo_run(["sysctl", "-w", f"net.ipv4.conf.{iface}.forwarding=1"])
 2525|     _ensure_egress_nat(iface)
 2526|     if not _masquerade_out(iface):
 2527|         return False, f"no MASQUERADE out {iface}"
 2528|     _clamp_tunnel_mss(iface)
 2529|     err = _ensure_lan_probe_ns()
 2530|     if err:
 2531|         return False, err
 2532|     _sudo_ip(["route", "replace", f"{_PROOF_NS_IP}/32", "dev", _PROOF_VETH_H,
 2533|               "table", str(_PROOF_TABLE)])
 2534|     _sudo_ip(["route", "replace", "10.99.50.0/24", "dev", _PROOF_VETH_H,
 2535|               "table", str(_PROOF_TABLE)])
 2536|     try:
 2537|         _ensure_tunnel_policy(iface)
 2538|     except Exception:
 2539|         pass
 2540|     _flush_table_defaults(_PROOF_TABLE)
 2541|     via = _tunnel_nexthop(iface)
 2542|     # TAP/subnet tunnels need an on-link gw (SecureNAT .1). Never dummy 10.66.70.1.
 2543|     if via:
 2544|         _sudo_ip(["route", "replace", f"{via}/32", "dev", iface, "table", str(_PROOF_TABLE)])
 2545|         rdef = _sudo_ip([
 2546|             "route", "replace", "default", "via", via, "dev", iface,
 2547|             "table", str(_PROOF_TABLE),
 2548|         ])
 2549|     else:
 2550|         rdef = _sudo_ip(["route", "replace", "default", "dev", iface, "table", str(_PROOF_TABLE)])
 2551|     if rdef.returncode != 0:
 2552|         return False, f"proof table default dev {iface} failed"
 2553|     _iptables_ensure(["FORWARD", "-i", _PROOF_VETH_H, "-o", iface, "-j", "ACCEPT"], insert=True)
 2554|     _iptables_ensure(
 2555|         ["FORWARD", "-i", iface, "-o", _PROOF_VETH_H,
 2556|          "-m", "conntrack", "--ctstate", "RELATED,ESTABLISHED", "-j", "ACCEPT"],
 2557|         insert=True,
 2558|     )
 2559|     check = _sudo_run([
 2560|         "iptables", "-t", "nat", "-C", "POSTROUTING",
 2561|         "-s", f"{_PROOF_NS_IP}/32", "-o", iface, "-j", "MASQUERADE",
 2562|     ])
 2563|     if check.returncode != 0:
 2564|         _sudo_run([
 2565|             "iptables", "-t", "nat", "-I", "POSTROUTING", "1",
 2566|             "-s", f"{_PROOF_NS_IP}/32", "-o", iface, "-j", "MASQUERADE",
 2567|         ])
 2568|     if not _host_default_safe():
 2569|         _restore_lab_default()
 2570|         if not _host_default_safe():
 2571|             return False, "host default unsafe before probe — abort"
 2572| 
 2573|     last_why = "no dest answered"
 2574|     for dst in _PROOF_DSTS:
 2575|         got = _sudo_run(
 2576|             ["ip", "route", "get", dst, "from", _PROOF_NS_IP, "iif", _PROOF_VETH_H],
 2577|             timeout=5,
 2578|         )
 2579|         via = _dev_token((got.stdout or "") + (got.stderr or ""))
 2580|         if via != iface:
 2581|             last_why = f"route get {dst} via {via or '?'} not {iface}"
 2582|             continue
 2583|         before = _forward_pkts(_PROOF_VETH_H, iface)
 2584|         ping = _sudo_run(
 2585|             ["ip", "netns", "exec", _PROOF_NS, "ping", "-c", "1", "-W", "2", dst],
 2586|             timeout=10,
 2587|         )
 2588|         if ping.returncode != 0:
 2589|             curl = _sudo_run(
 2590|                 ["ip", "netns", "exec", _PROOF_NS, "curl", "-4", "-sS", "-m", "3",
 2591|                  "-o", "/dev/null", f"http://{dst}/"],
 2592|                 timeout=10,
 2593|             )
 2594|             if curl.returncode != 0:
 2595|                 last_why = f"forwarded ping/HTTP {dst} via {iface} failed"
 2596|                 continue
 2597|         after = _forward_pkts(_PROOF_VETH_H, iface)
 2598|         if after < before:
 2599|             after = before
 2600|         if not _host_default_safe():
 2601|             _restore_lab_default()
 2602|             return False, f"{iface} probe stole host default — restored, fail closed"
 2603|         why = f"forwarded ping {dst} via {iface} (netns {_PROOF_NS_IP})"
 2604|         if after > before:
 2605|             why += f" FORWARD pkts {before}->{after}"
 2606|         return True, why
 2607|     return False, last_why
 2608| 
 2609| 
 2610| def lan_forward_proven(iface: str) -> bool:
 2611|     """True when a LAN-source packet was FORWARDED+NATed out this iface."""
 2612|     ok, _reason = lan_proof_state(iface)
 2613|     return ok
 2614| 
 2615| 
 2616| _LAN_SPEED_URLS = (
 2617|     "https://speed.cloudflare.com/__down?bytes=1000000",
 2618|     "http://cachefly.cachefly.net/5mb.test",
 2619|     "http://ipv4.download.thinkbroadband.com/1MB.zip",
 2620|     "https://speed.cloudflare.com/__down?bytes=5000000",
 2621| )
 2622| 
 2623| 
 2624| def _curl_resolve_args(url: str) -> list[str]:
 2625|     """Pin hostname to a host-resolved IPv4 so netns curl does not need DNS."""
 2626|     try:
 2627|         from urllib.parse import urlparse
 2628|         import socket
 2629|     except Exception:
 2630|         return []
 2631|     u = urlparse(url)
 2632|     host = u.hostname or ""
 2633|     if not host:
 2634|         return []
 2635|     port = u.port or (443 if (u.scheme or "") == "https" else 80)
 2636|     try:
 2637|         infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, port, socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
 2638|         ip = infos[0][4][0]
 2639|     except OSError:
 2640|         return []
 2641|     if not ip:
 2642|         return []
 2643|     return ["--resolve", f"{host}:{port}:{ip}"]
 2644| 
 2645| 
 2646| def measure_lan_client_mbps(iface: str) -> float | None:
 2647|     """Download as a LAN client FORWARDED+NATed out this tunnel.
 2648| 
 2649|     Same hop as Fast.com on a lab PC (LAN → Ubuntu → wg0/vti0/tap_soft).
 2650|     Never changes Ubuntu's main default. None if the path is not a real WAN.
 2651|     """
 2652|     name = (iface or "").strip()
 2653|     if not is_client_wan_iface(name):
 2654|         return None
 2655|     got_lock = False
 2656|     for _wait in range(3):
 2657|         if _LAN_PROOF_LOCK.acquire(timeout=12.0):
 2658|             got_lock = True
 2659|             break
 2660|         _log(f"SPEED {name} waiting for lan-proof lock")
 2661|     if not got_lock:
 2662|         _log(f"SPEED {name} busy")
 2663|         return None
 2664|     try:
 2665|         ok, why = _probe_lan_forward(name)
 2666|         if not ok:
 2667|             _log(f"SPEED {name} probe: {why} — trying download anyway")
 2668| 
 2669|         def _one(url: str) -> float:
 2670|             host = url.split("/")[2] if "://" in url else url
 2671|             extra = _curl_resolve_args(url)
 2672|             r = _sudo_run(
 2673|                 [
 2674|                     "ip", "netns", "exec", _PROOF_NS,
 2675|                     "curl", "-4", "--http1.1", "-sS", "-o", "/dev/null",
 2676|                     "--max-time", "16", "--connect-timeout", "6",
 2677|                     "--retry", "0", "-L", "--max-redirs", "3",
 2678|                     *extra,
 2679|                     "-w", "%{speed_download}",
 2680|                     "--", url,
 2681|                 ],
 2682|                 timeout=22,
 2683|             )
 2684|             raw = (r.stdout or "").strip().split()[-1] if (r.stdout or "").strip() else "0"
 2685|             try:
 2686|                 bps = float(raw)
 2687|             except ValueError:
 2688|                 bps = 0.0
 2689|             # Timed-out transfers still count if some payload arrived.
 2690|             if bps < 1500:
 2691|                 err = ((r.stderr or "").strip().replace("\n", " "))[:160]
 2692|                 _log(f"SPEED {name} curl {host} rc={r.returncode} bps={bps:.0f} {err}")
 2693|                 return 0.0
 2694|             if r.returncode not in (0, 28) and bps < 80_000:
 2695|                 err = ((r.stderr or "").strip().replace("\n", " "))[:160]
 2696|                 _log(f"SPEED {name} curl {host} rc={r.returncode} bps={bps:.0f} {err}")
 2697|                 return 0.0
 2698|             return bps * 8.0 / 1_000_000.0
 2699| 
 2700|         speeds: list[float] = []
 2701|         for url in _LAN_SPEED_URLS:
 2702|             try:
 2703|                 v = float(_one(url) or 0.0)
 2704|             except Exception as e:
 2705|                 _log(f"SPEED {name} curl error: {e}")
 2706|                 v = 0.0
 2707|             if v > 0:
 2708|                 speeds.append(v)
 2709|                 break
 2710|         if not _host_default_safe():
 2711|             _restore_lab_default()
 2712|         if not speeds:
 2713|             return None
 2714|         total = round(sum(speeds), 1)
 2715|         _log(f"SPEED lan-client via {name} {total} Mbps n={len(speeds)}")
 2716|         return total
 2717|     finally:
 2718|         _LAN_PROOF_LOCK.release()
 2719| 
 2720| 
 2721| def lan_proof_state(iface: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
 2722|     """Cached per-iface LAN FORWARD+NAT proof. Fail closed. TTL ~30s."""
 2723|     name = (iface or "").strip()
 2724|     if not name:
 2725|         return False, "no iface"
 2726|     now = time.time()
 2727|     inet = _VPN_INET_CACHE.get(name)
 2728|     if inet and not inet[1] and _ping_i_misses(name) >= _PING_I_MISS_LIMIT:
 2729|         reason = f"{name} ping -I 8.8.8.8 failed ×{_ping_i_misses(name)} — ignore stale LAN-proof"
 2730|         cached = _LAN_PROOF_CACHE.get(name)
 2731|         if not cached or cached[1]:
 2732|             invalidate_lan_proof(name, reason)
 2733|         return False, reason
 2734|     cached = _LAN_PROOF_CACHE.get(name)
 2735|     if cached and (now - cached[0]) < _LAN_PROOF_TTL:
 2736|         return cached[1], cached[2]
 2737|     if not _ctx.get("run"):
 2738|         return False, "no runtime (fail closed)"
 2739|     # Non-blocking: a request/watchdog must not sit behind a multi-iface probe.
 2740|     if not _LAN_PROOF_LOCK.acquire(timeout=0.25):
 2741|         cached = _LAN_PROOF_CACHE.get(name)
 2742|         if cached:
 2743|             return cached[1], cached[2]
 2744|         return False, "lan-proof busy"
 2745|     try:
 2746|         inet = _VPN_INET_CACHE.get(name)
 2747|         if inet and not inet[1] and _ping_i_misses(name) >= _PING_I_MISS_LIMIT:
 2748|             reason = f"{name} ping -I 8.8.8.8 failed ×{_ping_i_misses(name)} — ignore stale LAN-proof"
 2749|             cached = _LAN_PROOF_CACHE.get(name)
 2750|             if not cached or cached[1]:
 2751|                 invalidate_lan_proof(name, reason)
 2752|             return False, reason
 2753|         cached = _LAN_PROOF_CACHE.get(name)
 2754|         if cached and (time.time() - cached[0]) < _LAN_PROOF_TTL:
 2755|             return cached[1], cached[2]
 2756|         try:
 2757|             ok, reason = _probe_lan_forward(name)
 2758|         except Exception as e:
 2759|             ok, reason = False, f"probe error: {e}"
 2760|         _cache_lan_proof(name, ok, reason)
 2761|         _log(f"LAN-PROOF {name} {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}: {reason}")
 2762|         if not _host_default_safe():
 2763|             _restore_lab_default()
 2764|             _log("LAN-PROOF restored host default via MikroTik .1")
 2765|         return ok, reason
 2766|     finally:
 2767|         _LAN_PROOF_LOCK.release()
 2768| 
 2769| 
 2770| def refresh_lan_client_proofs() -> dict[str, bool]:
 2771|     """Prove wg0 first, then other real tunnels independently. Never dummy."""
 2772|     out: dict[str, bool] = {}
 2773|     seen: set[str] = set()
 2774|     candidates = list(_PROOF_ORDER)
 2775|     for extra in ("sstp0",):
 2776|         if extra not in candidates:
 2777|             candidates.append(extra)
 2778|     zt_lab = zerotier_lab_client_iface()
 2779|     if zt_lab and zt_lab not in candidates:
 2780|         candidates.append(zt_lab)
 2781|     for iface in candidates:
 2782|         if iface in seen:
 2783|             continue
 2784|         seen.add(iface)
 2785|         if not _iface_exists(iface):
 2786|             continue
 2787|         if _iface_is_dummy(iface):
 2788|             _cache_lan_proof(iface, False, f"{iface} is dummy (never WAN)")
 2789|             out[iface] = False
 2790|             _log(f"LAN-PROOF {iface} SKIP: dummy")
 2791|             continue
 2792|         if _client_egress_denied(iface):
 2793|             _cache_lan_proof(iface, False, f"{iface} overlay — not a client WAN")
 2794|             out[iface] = False
 2795|             _log(f"LAN-PROOF {iface} SKIP: client-egress deny")
 2796|             continue
 2797|         out[iface] = lan_forward_proven(iface)
 2798|     return out
 2799| 
 2800| 
 2801| def _client_egress_denied(iface: str) -> bool:
 2802|     """True when SAW may rank this iface but LAN clients must not use it."""
 2803|     return bool(iface) and iface in _CLIENT_EGRESS_DENY
 2804| 
 2805| 
 2806| def vpn_client_egress_allowed(iface: str) -> bool:
 2807|     """LAN clients may use a tunnel only when FORWARD+NAT is proven for it.
 2808| 
 2809|     Scoring still uses vpn_carries_internet (ping -I). Client policy tables
 2810|     do not: ping -I never proves FORWARD+NAT of a LAN source.
 2811|     Per-iface only — _ALLOW_VPN_CLIENT_EGRESS stays False on purpose.
 2812|     A single ping -I miss does not disallow while the LAN-proof stamp is PASS.
 2813|     Overlay deny (nebula1) never becomes a client default.
 2814|     """
 2815|     if not iface:
 2816|         return False
 2817|     if iface in _LAB_ONLY_IFACES or iface in _NOT_TUNNEL_IFACES:
 2818|         return False
 2819|     if _client_egress_denied(iface):
 2820|         return False
 2821|     if _iface_is_dummy(iface):
 2822|         return False
 2823|     inet = _VPN_INET_CACHE.get(iface)
 2824|     if inet and not inet[1] and not _lan_proof_stamp_pass(iface):
 2825|         return False
 2826|     if _lan_proof_stamp_pass(iface):
 2827|         return True
 2828|     return lan_forward_proven(iface)
 2829| 
 2830| 
 2831| 
 2832| def _lan_proof_stamp_pass(iface: str) -> bool:
 2833|     """Trust /run stamp written by lan-proof (bash watchdog + Python)."""
 2834|     if not iface:
 2835|         return False
 2836|     safe = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]", "_", iface)
 2837|     path = os.path.join(_LAN_PROOF_STAMP_DIR, safe)
 2838|     try:
 2839|         with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
 2840|             return (f.readline() or "").strip() == "PASS"
 2841|     except Exception:
 2842|         return False
 2843| 
 2844| 
 2845| def choose_device_egress(iface: str | None, carries_internet: bool) -> dict:
 2846|     """Decide this device's default without touching the host table.
 2847| 
 2848|     Scoring/UI may still pick a tunnel. Client tables stay MikroTik .1 unless
 2849|     FORWARD+NAT of a LAN source is proven for that iface (not ping -I).
 2850|     Dummy ifaces and lo/LAN NICs never become WAN.
 2851|     """
 2852|     name = (iface or "").strip()
 2853|     if not name:
 2854|         return {
 2855|             "kind": "mikrotik",
 2856|             "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 2857|             "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 2858|             "reason": "no iface for this pick",
 2859|         }
 2860|     if name in _LAB_ONLY_IFACES or _iface_is_dummy(name):
 2861|         return {
 2862|             "kind": "mikrotik",
 2863|             "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 2864|             "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 2865|             "reason": f"{name} is lab-only dummy (never WAN)",
 2866|         }
 2867|     if name in _NOT_TUNNEL_IFACES:
 2868|         return {
 2869|             "kind": "mikrotik",
 2870|             "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 2871|             "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 2872|             "reason": f"{name} is not a VPN tunnel",
 2873|         }
 2874|     if _client_egress_denied(name):
 2875|         return {
 2876|             "kind": "mikrotik",
 2877|             "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 2878|             "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 2879|             "reason": (
 2880|                 f"{name} is overlay-only — SAW may rank it, "
 2881|                 "LAN clients stay MikroTik"
 2882|             ),
 2883|         }
 2884|     if not carries_internet and not _lan_proof_stamp_pass(name):
 2885|         return {
 2886|             "kind": "mikrotik",
 2887|             "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 2888|             "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 2889|             "reason": f"{name} cannot reach 8.8.8.8 — stay MikroTik",
 2890|         }
 2891|     if not carries_internet and _lan_proof_stamp_pass(name):
 2892|         carries_internet = True
 2893|     if not vpn_client_egress_allowed(name) and not _lan_proof_stamp_pass(name):
 2894|         _ok, why = lan_proof_state(name) if _ctx.get("run") else (False, "not proven")
 2895|         detail = why if not _ok else "not proven"
 2896|         return {
 2897|             "kind": "mikrotik",
 2898|             "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 2899|             "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 2900|             "reason": (
 2901|                 f"{name} scores as WAN (ping -I) but LAN clients stay MikroTik "
 2902|                 f"until FORWARD+NAT from a LAN source is proven ({detail})"
 2903|             ),
 2904|         }
 2905|     return {
 2906|         "kind": "tunnel",
 2907|         "via": None,
 2908|         "dev": name,
 2909|         "reason": f"{name} LAN FORWARD+NAT proven — client table via {name}",
 2910|     }
 2911| 
 2912| 
 2913| def _store_egress(ip: str, egress: dict) -> None:
 2914|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 2915|         if ip not in DEVICES:
 2916|             return
 2917|         DEVICES[ip]["egress"] = egress.get("kind") or "mikrotik"
 2918|         DEVICES[ip]["egress_via"] = egress.get("via") or egress.get("dev")
 2919|         DEVICES[ip]["egress_dev"] = egress.get("dev")
 2920|         DEVICES[ip]["egress_reason"] = egress.get("reason") or ""
 2921| 
 2922| 
 2923| def _ensure_egress_nat(iface: str) -> None:
 2924|     """MASQUERADE + FORWARD on the iface this device actually exits.
 2925| 
 2926|     Overlay sources are ACCEPTed inside AUTOVPN-RL (hashlimit).
 2927|     Never INSERT `-i ztdiysvwlf ACCEPT` here — that would skip house-LAN REJECT.
 2928|     """
 2929|     if not iface:
 2930|         return
 2931|     check = _sudo_run(["iptables", "-t", "nat", "-C", "POSTROUTING", "-o", iface, "-j", "MASQUERADE"])
 2932|     if check.returncode != 0:
 2933|         _sudo_run(["iptables", "-t", "nat", "-A", "POSTROUTING", "-o", iface, "-j", "MASQUERADE"])
 2934|     _clamp_tunnel_mss(iface)
 2935|     _iptables_ensure(["FORWARD", "-i", _LAN_IFACE, "-o", iface, "-j", "ACCEPT"], insert=True)
 2936|     _iptables_ensure(
 2937|         ["FORWARD", "-i", iface, "-o", _LAN_IFACE,
 2938|          "-m", "conntrack", "--ctstate", "RELATED,ESTABLISHED", "-j", "ACCEPT"],
 2939|         insert=True,
 2940|     )
 2941| 
 2942| 
 2943| def _install_connected_routes(table: int) -> None:
 2944|     """Keep lab LAN (and overlay, for remote tables) on-link in the client table."""
 2945|     _sudo_ip([
 2946|         "route", "replace", _LAN_SUBNET,
 2947|         "dev", _LAN_IFACE, "table", str(table),
 2948|     ])
 2949|     if table >= _REMOTE_MARK_MIN:
 2950|         for net in zt_cloud_networks():
 2951|             _sudo_ip([
 2952|                 "route", "replace", str(net),
 2953|                 "dev", _ZT_CLOUD_IFACE, "table", str(table),
 2954|             ])
 2955| 
 2956| 
 2957| def _flush_table_defaults(table: int) -> None:
 2958|     """Drop every default in a client table. via-.1 and dev-wg0 can coexist."""
 2959|     for _ in range(8):
 2960|         r = _sudo_ip(["route", "del", "default", "table", str(int(table))])
 2961|         if r.returncode != 0:
 2962|             break
 2963| 
 2964| 
 2965| def _install_mikrotik_default(table: int):
 2966|     _install_connected_routes(table)
 2967|     _flush_table_defaults(table)
 2968|     return _sudo_ip([
 2969|         "route", "replace", "default",
 2970|         "via", _MIKROTIK_IP, "dev", _LAN_IFACE, "table", str(table),
 2971|     ])
 2972| 
 2973| 
 2974| def _install_tunnel_default(table: int, tun: str):
 2975|     """Device table: keep LAN on enp0s8, default out the proven tunnel."""
 2976|     _install_connected_routes(table)
 2977|     _flush_table_defaults(table)
 2978|     via = _tunnel_nexthop(tun)
 2979|     if via:
 2980|         _sudo_ip(["route", "replace", f"{via}/32", "dev", tun, "table", str(table)])
 2981|         return _sudo_ip([
 2982|             "route", "replace", "default", "via", via, "dev", tun, "table", str(table),
 2983|         ])
 2984|     return _sudo_ip(["route", "replace", "default", "dev", tun, "table", str(table)])
 2985| 
 2986| 
 2987| def _table_default_mentions(table: int, token: str) -> bool:
 2988|     r = _run(["ip", "route", "show", "table", str(table)], timeout=3)
 2989|     out = (r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")
 2990|     for ln in out.splitlines():
 2991|         if ln.startswith("default") and token in ln:
 2992|             return True
 2993|     return False
 2994| 
 2995| 
 2996| def _table_default_dev(table: int) -> str:
 2997|     r = _run(["ip", "route", "show", "table", str(table)], timeout=3)
 2998|     first = ""
 2999|     for ln in (r.stdout or "").splitlines():
 3000|         if not ln.startswith("default"):
 3001|             continue
 3002|         dev = _dev_token(ln)
 3003|         if not first:
 3004|             first = dev
 3005|         if _is_vpn_tunnel_dev(dev) and not _client_egress_denied(dev):
 3006|             return dev
 3007|     return first
 3008| 
 3009| 
 3010| def _client_fib_dev(mark: int, table: int) -> str:
 3011|     """Client-table default (not host `ip route get`, which uses Ubuntu's src)."""
 3012|     return _table_default_dev(int(table))
 3013| 
 3014| 
 3015| def _egress_from_fib(mark: int, table: int, reason: str = "") -> dict:
 3016|     """Honest client exit from the kernel lookup, not the SAW label."""
 3017|     dev = _client_fib_dev(mark, table)
 3018|     tunnel_ok = (
 3019|         _is_vpn_tunnel_dev(dev)
 3020|         and not _client_egress_denied(dev)
 3021|         and not _iface_is_dummy(dev)
 3022|         and dev not in _NOT_TUNNEL_IFACES
 3023|         and dev not in _LAB_ONLY_IFACES
 3024|     )
 3025|     if tunnel_ok:
 3026|         return {
 3027|             "kind": "tunnel",
 3028|             "via": None,
 3029|             "dev": dev,
 3030|             "reason": reason or f"packets via {dev}",
 3031|         }
 3032|     return {
 3033|         "kind": "mikrotik",
 3034|         "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 3035|         "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 3036|         "reason": reason or f"packets via MikroTik ({dev or 'main'})",
 3037|     }
 3038| 
 3039| 
 3040| def _sync_device_egress_from_fib(ip: str, reason: str = "") -> dict | None:
 3041|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3042|         d = DEVICES.get(ip)
 3043|         if not d:
 3044|             return None
 3045|         try:
 3046|             mark = int(d["mark"])
 3047|             table = int(d["table"])
 3048|         except Exception:
 3049|             return None
 3050|     egress = _egress_from_fib(mark, table, reason)
 3051|     _store_egress(ip, egress)
 3052|     return egress
 3053| 
 3054| 
 3055| def _iface_is_down(iface: str) -> bool:
 3056|     """True when missing or kernel operstate DOWN. WG UNKNOWN counts as up."""
 3057|     if not iface:
 3058|         return True
 3059|     try:
 3060|         if not _iface_exists(iface):
 3061|             return True
 3062|     except Exception:
 3063|         return True
 3064|     r = _run(["ip", "-br", "link", "show", "dev", iface], timeout=3)
 3065|     parts = (r.stdout or "").split()
 3066|     if len(parts) < 2:
 3067|         return True
 3068|     return parts[1].upper() == "DOWN"
 3069| 
 3070| 
 3071| def _mark_tunnel_dead(tun: str, reason: str) -> None:
 3072|     """Iface DOWN (or proven dead): drop LAN-proof immediately."""
 3073|     if tun:
 3074|         _PING_I_MISS[tun] = _PING_I_MISS_LIMIT
 3075|     _VPN_INET_CACHE[tun] = (time.time(), False)
 3076|     invalidate_lan_proof(tun, reason)
 3077| 
 3078| 
 3079| def _ping_i_uncached(iface: str) -> bool:
 3080|     r = _run(["ping", "-I", iface, "-c", "1", "-W", "2", "8.8.8.8"], timeout=5)
 3081|     ok = r.returncode == 0
 3082|     _record_ping_i(iface, ok)
 3083|     return ok
 3084| 
 3085| 
 3086| def _live_client_tunnel_ok(tun: str) -> bool:
 3087|     """Clients already on this tunnel: fail closed on down / ping -I miss.
 3088| 
 3089|     True cache is short (8s) so a flap is not held for the full inet TTL.
 3090|     """
 3091|     if not tun:
 3092|         return False
 3093|     now = time.time()
 3094|     cached = _VPN_INET_CACHE.get(tun)
 3095|     if cached and not cached[1] and (now - cached[0]) < 15.0:
 3096|         return False
 3097|     if _iface_is_down(tun):
 3098|         _mark_tunnel_dead(tun, f"{tun} is DOWN")
 3099|         return False
 3100|     if cached and cached[1] and (now - cached[0]) < 8.0:
 3101|         return True
 3102|     return _ping_i_uncached(tun)
 3103| 
 3104| 
 3105| def trigger_crash_log(reason: str, *, force: bool = False) -> None:
 3106|     """Write a crash snapshot. Shared 120s cooldown with the bash watchdog."""
 3107|     stamp = "/run/autovpn-crash-log.ts"
 3108|     try:
 3109|         cooldown = int(os.environ.get("AUTOVPN_CRASH_LOG_COOLDOWN", "120"))
 3110|     except ValueError:
 3111|         cooldown = 120
 3112|     now = time.time()
 3113|     if not force:
 3114|         try:
 3115|             last = int(open(stamp, encoding="utf-8").read().strip() or "0")
 3116|         except Exception:
 3117|             last = 0
 3118|         if now - last < cooldown:
 3119|             _log(f"crash-log skipped (cooldown {cooldown}s) reason={reason}")
 3120|             return
 3121|     try:
 3122|         with open(stamp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
 3123|             f.write(str(int(now)))
 3124|     except Exception:
 3125|         pass
 3126|     script = ""
 3127|     for candidate in (
 3128|         "/usr/local/sbin/crash-log.sh",
 3129|         "/home/user/adaptive-gateway/scripts/crash-log.sh",
 3130|     ):
 3131|         if os.path.isfile(candidate):
 3132|             script = candidate
 3133|             break
 3134|     if not script:
 3135|         _log(f"crash-log missing reason={reason}")
 3136|         return
 3137|     _log(f"crash-log reason={reason}")
 3138|     env = os.environ.copy()
 3139|     env["AUTOVPN_CRASH_REASON"] = reason
 3140| 
 3141|     def _go() -> None:
 3142|         try:
 3143|             subprocess.run(
 3144|                 ["timeout", "15", "/bin/bash", script],
 3145|                 env=env,
 3146|                 timeout=18,
 3147|                 capture_output=True,
 3148|             )
 3149|         except Exception as e:
 3150|             _log(f"crash-log run warn: {e}")
 3151| 
 3152|     threading.Thread(target=_go, daemon=True, name="crash-log").start()
 3153| 
 3154| 
 3155| def guard_client_vpn_tables() -> list[str]:
 3156|     """If a device table default is a dead VPN, pin it to MikroTik .1 now.
 3157| 
 3158|     Independent of Ubuntu's own ping 8.8.8.8 on main. Does not change DHCP.
 3159|     """
 3160|     reverted: list[str] = []
 3161|     tables: set[int] = set(range(_CLIENT_TABLE_MIN, _CLIENT_TABLE_MAX + 1))
 3162|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3163|         for d in DEVICES.values():
 3164|             try:
 3165|                 tables.add(int(d["table"]))
 3166|             except Exception:
 3167|                 continue
 3168|     for table in sorted(tables):
 3169|         if table < _CLIENT_TABLE_MIN or table >= 200:
 3170|             continue
 3171|         try:
 3172|             dev = _table_default_dev(table)
 3173|         except Exception:
 3174|             continue
 3175|         if not dev or not _is_vpn_tunnel_dev(dev):
 3176|             continue
 3177|         why = ""
 3178|         if _client_egress_denied(dev):
 3179|             why = f"{dev} overlay — not a client WAN"
 3180|         elif _iface_is_down(dev):
 3181|             why = f"{dev} down"
 3182|             _mark_tunnel_dead(dev, why)
 3183|         else:
 3184|             alive = _ping_i_uncached(dev)
 3185|             stamp_ok = _lan_proof_stamp_pass(dev)
 3186|             # One ping blink must not steal a proven client VPN back to MikroTik.
 3187|             if (not alive) and (not stamp_ok) and _ping_i_misses(dev) >= _PING_I_MISS_LIMIT:
 3188|                 why = f"ping -I {dev} 8.8.8.8 failed ×{_ping_i_misses(dev)}"
 3189|             elif not stamp_ok:
 3190|                 ok, proof_why = lan_proof_state(dev)
 3191|                 if not ok and _ping_i_misses(dev) >= _PING_I_MISS_LIMIT:
 3192|                     why = f"LAN-proof fail: {proof_why}"
 3193|         if not why:
 3194|             continue
 3195|         try:
 3196|             _install_mikrotik_default(table)
 3197|         except Exception as e:
 3198|             _log(f"WATCHDOG client table {table} pin failed: {e}")
 3199|             continue
 3200|         _log(
 3201|             f"WATCHDOG client table {table} default {dev} -> via {_MIKROTIK_IP} "
 3202|             f"({why}) — DHCP unchanged"
 3203|         )
 3204|         reverted.append(f"table-{table}:{dev}:{why}")
 3205|         with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3206|             for d in DEVICES.values():
 3207|                 if int(d.get("table") or 0) != table:
 3208|                     continue
 3209|                 d["egress"] = "mikrotik"
 3210|                 d["egress_via"] = _MIKROTIK_IP
 3211|                 d["egress_dev"] = _LAN_IFACE
 3212|                 d["egress_reason"] = f"watchdog pinned table {table}: {why}"
 3213|     # Fail-close the table immediately. Do not crash-log VPN ping-I blinks.
 3214|     return reverted
 3215| 
 3216| 
 3217| def apply_device_route(device: dict, regime_id: str) -> bool:
 3218|     """Mark packets from device IP and send them via a working uplink.
 3219| 
 3220|     Client table default is MikroTik .1 unless choose_device_egress returns
 3221|     tunnel (FORWARD+NAT proven for a LAN source — not merely ping -I).
 3222|     Lab dummy tunnels must never become WAN — that blackholed clients.
 3223|     Host default table is never changed here.
 3224|     """
 3225|     regimes = _ctx["REGIMES"]
 3226|     meta = regimes.get(regime_id)
 3227|     if not meta:
 3228|         return False
 3229|     iface = _resolve_iface(regime_id, meta)
 3230|     mark = int(device["mark"])
 3231|     table = int(device["table"])
 3232|     ip = device["ip"]
 3233|     name = device.get("name") or ip
 3234| 
 3235|     if not GATEWAY_ENABLED():
 3236|         return False
 3237| 
 3238|     carries = bool(iface) and _vpn_carries_internet(iface)
 3239|     egress = choose_device_egress(iface, carries)
 3240|     # Extra belt: never steal a client onto a VPN if Ubuntu cannot forward.
 3241|     try:
 3242|         fwd_ok = forwarding_ready()
 3243|     except Exception:
 3244|         fwd_ok = False
 3245|     if egress["kind"] == "tunnel" and not fwd_ok:
 3246|         egress = {
 3247|             "kind": "mikrotik",
 3248|             "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 3249|             "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 3250|             "reason": f"{iface} would be WAN but forwarding not ready — stay MikroTik",
 3251|         }
 3252|         _log(f"DEVICE {name} ({ip}) skip VPN default via {iface}: forwarding not ready")
 3253| 
 3254|     _purge_source_marks(ip)
 3255|     # Insert at 1 so overlay/LAN MARK happens before any later mangle RETURN.
 3256|     _sudo_run([
 3257|         "iptables", "-t", "mangle", "-I", "PREROUTING", "1",
 3258|         "-s", ip, "-j", "MARK", "--set-mark", str(mark),
 3259|     ])
 3260|     _ensure_device_acct(ip)
 3261| 
 3262|     _sudo_ip(["rule", "del", "fwmark", str(mark), "table", str(table)])
 3263|     _sudo_ip(["rule", "add", "fwmark", str(mark), "table", str(table), "priority", str(100 + mark)])
 3264| 
 3265|     used_tunnel = False
 3266|     if egress["kind"] == "tunnel" and egress.get("dev"):
 3267|         tun = egress["dev"]
 3268|         _ensure_egress_nat(tun)
 3269|         # Never `via 10.66.x` — that is the dummy overlay, not a WAN next-hop.
 3270|         r = _install_tunnel_default(table, tun)
 3271|         still_ok = r.returncode == 0 and vpn_client_egress_allowed(tun)
 3272|         table_ok = _table_default_mentions(table, tun)
 3273|         host_ok = _host_default_safe()
 3274|         if not host_ok:
 3275|             _restore_lab_default()
 3276|             host_ok = _host_default_safe()
 3277|         if still_ok and table_ok and host_ok:
 3278|             used_tunnel = True
 3279|             _ensure_egress_nat(tun)
 3280|             _log(
 3281|                 f"DEVICE {name} ({ip}) SAW pick {iface} has LAN proof "
 3282|                 f"→ table {table} default dev {tun} (host default unchanged)"
 3283|             )
 3284|         else:
 3285|             why = []
 3286|             if r.returncode != 0:
 3287|                 why.append("route replace failed")
 3288|             if not still_ok:
 3289|                 why.append(f"{tun} LAN FORWARD+NAT not proven")
 3290|             if not table_ok:
 3291|                 why.append("table default missing iface")
 3292|             if not host_ok:
 3293|                 why.append("host default would be stolen")
 3294|             reason = "; ".join(why) or "unsafe VPN default"
 3295|             _log(f"DEVICE {name} ({ip}) would blackhole via {tun} ({reason}) — MikroTik")
 3296|             _install_mikrotik_default(table)
 3297|             egress = {
 3298|                 "kind": "mikrotik",
 3299|                 "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 3300|                 "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 3301|                 "reason": f"{tun} would blackhole ({reason})",
 3302|             }
 3303|     if not used_tunnel:
 3304|         r = _install_mikrotik_default(table)
 3305|         _ensure_egress_nat(_LAN_IFACE)
 3306| 
 3307|     ok = r.returncode == 0
 3308|     fib = _egress_from_fib(mark, table)
 3309|     if egress["kind"] == "tunnel" and fib["kind"] != "tunnel":
 3310|         _log(
 3311|             f"DEVICE {name} ({ip}) wanted {egress.get('dev')} but FIB is MikroTik "
 3312|             f"— storing actual exit"
 3313|         )
 3314|         egress = dict(fib)
 3315|         egress["reason"] = (
 3316|             f"wanted {tun if used_tunnel else iface} but packets still via MikroTik"
 3317|         )
 3318|     elif fib["kind"] == "tunnel":
 3319|         keep_reason = egress.get("reason") or fib["reason"]
 3320|         egress = dict(fib)
 3321|         egress["reason"] = keep_reason
 3322|     else:
 3323|         egress = dict(fib)
 3324|         if not used_tunnel and not egress.get("reason"):
 3325|             egress["reason"] = "MikroTik default"
 3326|     _store_egress(ip, egress)
 3327|     via = egress["dev"] if egress["kind"] == "tunnel" else f"MikroTik {_MIKROTIK_IP}"
 3328|     _log(
 3329|         f"DEVICE {name} ({ip}) pick={meta['name']} "
 3330|         f"iface={iface or '-'} exit={via} ({egress['reason']})"
 3331|     )
 3332|     _log(f"DEVICE {name} ({ip}) -> {meta['name']} mark={mark} ({'ok' if ok else 'partial'})")
 3333|     return ok
 3334| 
 3335| 
 3336| def revert_device_table(ip: str) -> bool:
 3337|     """Put one device table back on MikroTik (no DHCP change)."""
 3338|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3339|         d = DEVICES.get(ip)
 3340|         if not d:
 3341|             return False
 3342|         table = int(d["table"])
 3343|         mark = int(d["mark"])
 3344|         name = d.get("name") or ip
 3345|     r = _install_mikrotik_default(table)
 3346|     _store_egress(ip, {
 3347|         "kind": "mikrotik",
 3348|         "via": _MIKROTIK_IP,
 3349|         "dev": _LAN_IFACE,
 3350|         "reason": "reverted to MikroTik (safety)",
 3351|     })
 3352|     _log(f"DEVICE {name} ({ip}) table={table} mark={mark} reverted to MikroTik")
 3353|     return r.returncode == 0
 3354| 
 3355| 
 3356| def GATEWAY_ENABLED() -> bool:
 3357|     fn = _ctx.get("is_gateway_enabled")
 3358|     if callable(fn):
 3359|         return bool(fn())
 3360|     return bool(_ctx.get("GATEWAY_ENABLED", False))
 3361| 
 3362| 
 3363| def clear_device_routes() -> None:
 3364|     """Drop marks/rules/tables when Gateway Off so DHCP can go back to .1."""
 3365|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3366|         items = list(DEVICES.values())
 3367|     for d in items:
 3368|         ip = d["ip"]
 3369|         mark = str(d["mark"])
 3370|         table = str(d["table"])
 3371|         _purge_source_marks(ip)
 3372|         _sudo_ip(["rule", "del", "fwmark", mark, "table", table])
 3373|         _sudo_ip(["route", "flush", "table", table])
 3374|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3375|         for d in DEVICES.values():
 3376|             d["routed"] = False
 3377|             d["egress"] = "mikrotik"
 3378|             d["egress_via"] = _MIKROTIK_IP
 3379|             d["egress_dev"] = _LAN_IFACE
 3380|             d["egress_reason"] = "Gateway Off — DHCP back to .1"
 3381| 
 3382| 
 3383| def evaluate_device(ip: str) -> dict | None:
 3384|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3385|         d = DEVICES.get(ip)
 3386|         if not d:
 3387|             return None
 3388|         device = dict(d)
 3389| 
 3390|     # Auto hysteresis per device (runs even when Gateway is Off so the site updates)
 3391|     if device["profile"] == "auto":
 3392|         guess, evidence = classify_device(ip)
 3393|         if guess != device["auto_detected"]:
 3394|             evidence = f"leaning {_ctx['AUTO_LABELS'].get(guess, guess)} · {evidence}"
 3395|             pending = device.get("pending")
 3396|             hits = device.get("pending_hits", 0)
 3397|             if guess == pending:
 3398|                 hits += 1
 3399|             else:
 3400|                 pending = guess
 3401|                 hits = 1
 3402|             with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3403|                 if ip in DEVICES:
 3404|                     DEVICES[ip]["evidence"] = evidence
 3405|                     DEVICES[ip]["pending"] = pending
 3406|                     DEVICES[ip]["pending_hits"] = hits
 3407|             need_hits, need_gap = auto_hysteresis_need(
 3408|                 device["auto_detected"], guess, evidence
 3409|             )
 3410|             if hits >= need_hits and time.time() - device.get("last_switch", 0) >= need_gap:
 3411|                 with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3412|                     if ip in DEVICES:
 3413|                         prev = DEVICES[ip]["auto_detected"]
 3414|                         DEVICES[ip]["auto_detected"] = guess
 3415|                         DEVICES[ip]["evidence"] = evidence.replace("leaning ", "").split(" · ", 1)[-1]
 3416|                         DEVICES[ip]["pending"] = None
 3417|                         DEVICES[ip]["pending_hits"] = 0
 3418|                         DEVICES[ip]["last_switch"] = time.time()
 3419|                         _log(f"DEVICE AUTO {device['name']} {prev} -> {guess}")
 3420|                         device = dict(DEVICES[ip])
 3421|         else:
 3422|             with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3423|                 if ip in DEVICES:
 3424|                     DEVICES[ip]["evidence"] = evidence
 3425|                     DEVICES[ip]["pending"] = None
 3426|                     DEVICES[ip]["pending_hits"] = 0
 3427|             device["evidence"] = evidence
 3428| 
 3429|     eff = effective_device_profile(device)
 3430|     failed_ids = set()
 3431|     fail_fn = _ctx.get("fail_ids_for_ip")
 3432|     if fail_fn:
 3433|         try:
 3434|             failed_ids = set(fail_fn(ip) or ())
 3435|         except Exception:
 3436|             failed_ids = set()
 3437|     scores, rows = score_regimes_for_profile(eff, device.get("custom_weights"), failed_ids=failed_ids)
 3438|     force = device.get("force_regime")
 3439|     if force and force in scores:
 3440|         best = force
 3441|     else:
 3442|         best = max(scores, key=scores.get) if scores else device["active_regime"]
 3443| 
 3444|     switched = best != device.get("active_regime")
 3445|     regimes = _ctx["REGIMES"]
 3446|     prev_name = device.get("active_name") or regimes.get(
 3447|         device.get("active_regime"), {}
 3448|     ).get("name", device.get("active_regime"))
 3449|     if GATEWAY_ENABLED():
 3450|         iface = _resolve_iface(best, regimes.get(best) or {})
 3451|         try:
 3452|             _sync_device_egress_from_fib(ip)
 3453|             with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3454|                 if ip in DEVICES:
 3455|                     device["egress"] = DEVICES[ip].get("egress")
 3456|                     device["egress_dev"] = DEVICES[ip].get("egress_dev")
 3457|                     device["routed"] = DEVICES[ip].get("routed")
 3458|         except Exception:
 3459|             pass
 3460|         tun_now = ""
 3461|         if device.get("egress") == "tunnel":
 3462|             tun_now = (device.get("egress_dev") or "").strip()
 3463|         if tun_now and _iface_is_down(tun_now):
 3464|             carries = False
 3465|         else:
 3466|             carries = bool(iface) and (
 3467|                 _vpn_carries_internet(iface) or _lan_proof_stamp_pass(iface)
 3468|             )
 3469|         want = choose_device_egress(iface, carries)
 3470|         have_kind = device.get("egress")
 3471|         have_dev = (device.get("egress_dev") or "").strip()
 3472|         want_dev = (want.get("dev") or "").strip() if want.get("kind") == "tunnel" else _LAN_IFACE
 3473|         try:
 3474|             fib_dev = _client_fib_dev(int(device["mark"]), int(device["table"]))
 3475|         except Exception:
 3476|             fib_dev = have_dev
 3477|         if want.get("kind") == "tunnel":
 3478|             table_drift = fib_dev != want_dev
 3479|         else:
 3480|             table_drift = _is_vpn_tunnel_dev(fib_dev)
 3481|         need = (
 3482|             switched
 3483|             or not device.get("routed")
 3484|             or have_kind != want["kind"]
 3485|             or (want["kind"] == "tunnel" and have_dev != want_dev)
 3486|             or (have_kind == "tunnel" and not carries)
 3487|             or table_drift
 3488|         )
 3489|         if need:
 3490|             apply_device_route(device, best)
 3491|         else:
 3492|             try:
 3493|                 _sync_device_egress_from_fib(ip)
 3494|             except Exception:
 3495|                 pass
 3496|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3497|         if ip in DEVICES:
 3498|             DEVICES[ip]["active_regime"] = best
 3499|             DEVICES[ip]["active_name"] = regimes.get(best, {}).get("name", best)
 3500|             if GATEWAY_ENABLED():
 3501|                 DEVICES[ip]["routed"] = True
 3502|             device = dict(DEVICES[ip])
 3503| 
 3504|     for row in rows:
 3505|         row["is_active"] = row["id"] == best
 3506|     rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["score"], reverse=True)
 3507|     weights = saw.resolve_weights(eff, _ctx["PROFILES"], device.get("custom_weights"))
 3508|     reason = saw.explain_choice(
 3509|         rows,
 3510|         weights,
 3511|         profile_key=eff,
 3512|         device=device.get("name") or ip,
 3513|         from_name=prev_name,
 3514|         to_id=best,
 3515|         auto=device.get("profile") == "auto",
 3516|         still=not switched,
 3517|         labels=_ctx.get("AUTO_LABELS"),
 3518|     )
 3519|     now = time.time()
 3520|     last_still = float(device.get("last_still_log") or 0)
 3521|     if switched:
 3522|         _log(reason)
 3523|         last_still = now
 3524|     elif now - last_still >= saw.STILL_LOG_INTERVAL:
 3525|         _log(reason)
 3526|         last_still = now
 3527|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3528|         if ip in DEVICES:
 3529|             DEVICES[ip]["metrics"] = rows
 3530|             DEVICES[ip]["switch_reason"] = reason
 3531|             DEVICES[ip]["switch_reason_at"] = now
 3532|             DEVICES[ip]["last_still_log"] = last_still
 3533|             device = dict(DEVICES[ip])
 3534|     _log(
 3535|         f"TICK {device.get('name') or ip} {ip} "
 3536|         f"mode={eff} "
 3537|         f"{device.get('evidence') or 'no-evidence'} "
 3538|         f"vpn={device.get('active_name') or best} "
 3539|         f"exit={device.get('egress') or 'mikrotik'}"
 3540|     )
 3541|     return device
 3542|

 3544| def evaluate_all_devices() -> None:
 3545|     sync_devices_from_leases()
 3546|     # Prove FORWARD+NAT even when Gateway is Off (DHCP stays .1).
 3547|     # Routing still fail-closed: clients only use a tunnel after Gateway On
 3548|     # AND lan_forward_proven(). Proofs must not wait on the opt-in toggle.
 3549|     try:
 3550|         refresh_lan_client_proofs()
 3551|     except Exception as e:
 3552|         _log(f"LAN-PROOF refresh error: {e}")
 3553|     now = time.time()
 3554|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3555|         ips = [
 3556|             ip for ip, d in DEVICES.items()
 3557|             if d.get("online")
 3558|             or (
 3559|                 not d.get("metrics")
 3560|                 and float(d.get("last_seen") or 0) > 0
 3561|                 and (now - float(d.get("last_seen") or 0)) < 45
 3562|             )
 3563|         ]
 3564|     if GATEWAY_ENABLED():
 3565|         for ip in ips:
 3566|             try:
 3567|                 _ensure_device_acct(ip)
 3568|             except Exception:
 3569|                 pass
 3570|     for ip in ips:
 3571|         try:
 3572|             evaluate_device(ip)
 3573|         except Exception as e:

 3618| def ensure_device_scores(ip: str) -> list:
 3619|     """Fill SAW rows for a device that has never been ranked.
 3620| 
 3621|     Display-only: uses the shared ping cache. Does not apply client routes
 3622|     (that still happens on the engine tick). Phones opening the UI often
 3623|     hit /api/status before the first evaluate_device, which used to paint
 3624|     Connected with None.
 3625|     """
 3626|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3627|         d = DEVICES.get(ip)
 3628|         if not d:

 3631|         if have:
 3632|             return have
 3633|         device = dict(d)
 3634|     eff = effective_device_profile(device)
 3635|     failed_ids = set()
 3636|     fail_fn = _ctx.get("fail_ids_for_ip")
 3637|     if fail_fn:
 3638|         try:
 3639|             failed_ids = set(fail_fn(ip) or ())
 3640|         except Exception:

 3643|         eff, device.get("custom_weights"), failed_ids=failed_ids,
 3644|     )

 3646|         return []

 3648|     if best not in scores:
 3649|         best = max(scores, key=scores.get)
 3650|     for row in rows:
 3651|         row["is_active"] = row.get("id") == best
 3652|     rows.sort(key=lambda r: r.get("score") or 0, reverse=True)
 3653|     name = (_ctx.get("REGIMES") or {}).get(best, {}).get("name", best)
 3654|     with DEVICES_LOCK:
 3655|         if ip in DEVICES:
 3656|             DEVICES[ip]["metrics"] = rows
 3657|             DEVICES[ip]["active_regime"] = best

 3712| def set_device_profile(ip: str, profile: str, custom_weights=None) -> dict | None:
 3713|     allowed = set(_ctx["PROFILES"]) | {"custom", "auto"}

 3732|                 DEVICES[ip]["pending_hits"] = 0
