backend-api\saw.py

Live-first SAW score, normalization, affinity cap, Fail scale.  Â·  480 lines

    1| """Live-first SAW scoring shared by host and per-device evaluate.
    2| 
    3| When a probe succeeds, latency / jitter / loss / MOS enter the 7-metric
    4| formula. Protocol characteristics are fallback only (iface down or probe
    5| fail). Affinity is a small capped tie-breaker so a worse live tunnel
    6| cannot beat a clearly better live tunnel.
    7| """
    8| from __future__ import annotations
    9| 
   10| # Raw PROFILE_AFFINITY values are 0.1–3.5 (legacy personality boosts).
   11| # After scale+cap the max add-on is AFFINITY_CAP — smaller than a clear
   12| # live QoS gap (e.g. 20ms vs 80ms on the gaming profile).
   13| AFFINITY_SCALE = 0.02
   14| AFFINITY_CAP = 0.08
   15| 
   16| # Lab dummy pings are often <1ms. Floor inverse terms so 10/lat does not
   17| # explode and drown the other six metrics / profile weights.
   18| LIVE_LAT_FLOOR_MS = 10.0
   19| LIVE_JIT_FLOOR_MS = 3.0
   20| 
   21| DEFAULT_CHAR = (80.0, 10.0, 1.0, 70.0, 1.0, 3.5, 1400.0)
   22| FAILED_METRICS = (999.0, 100.0, 100.0, 0.1, 100.0, 1.0, 1280.0)
   23| 
   24| 
   25| def normalize_weights(weights) -> list[float]:
   26|     w = [float(x) for x in (weights or [])]
   27|     while len(w) < 7:
   28|         w.append(0.0)
   29|     w = w[:7]
   30|     total = sum(w) or 1.0
   31|     return [x / total for x in w]
   32| 
   33| 
   34| def resolve_weights(profile_key: str, profiles: dict, custom_weights=None) -> list[float]:
   35|     if profile_key == "custom" and custom_weights:
   36|         return normalize_weights(custom_weights)
   37|     return normalize_weights(profiles.get(profile_key) or profiles.get("balanced") or [1] * 7)
   38| 
   39| 
   40| def blend_live_and_char(live, char) -> tuple:
   41|     """Live QoS + cheap live speed/MTU. Characteristic if those probes omitted.
   42| 
   43|     Throughput is the ping-derived estimate already in live[3]
   44|     (max(100-2*loss, 0.1)) — never iperf. MTU is a DF ping around 1400
   45|     (live[6] >= 1200); else the protocol table value.
   46|     """
   47|     lat, jit, loss, thr, reorder, mos, mtu = (float(x) for x in live)
   48|     c_thr = float(char[3])
   49|     c_mtu = float(char[6])
   50|     if thr <= 0.0:
   51|         thr = c_thr
   52|     if mtu < 1200.0:
   53|         mtu = c_mtu
   54|     return (lat, jit, loss, thr, reorder, mos, mtu)
   55| 
   56| 
   57| def prepare_metrics(metrics, source: str) -> tuple:
   58|     """Values that enter SAW. Live sub-ms pings saturate at 'excellent'."""
   59|     lat, jit, loss, thr, reorder, mos, mtu = (float(x) for x in metrics)
   60|     if source == "live":
   61|         lat = max(lat, LIVE_LAT_FLOOR_MS)
   62|         jit = max(jit, LIVE_JIT_FLOOR_MS)
   63|     return (lat, jit, loss, thr, reorder, mos, mtu)
   64| 
   65| 
   66| METRIC_NAMES = (
   67|     "Latency", "Jitter", "Loss", "Throughput", "Reorder", "MOS", "MTU",
   68| )
   69| 
   70| 
   71| def normalized_terms(metrics) -> list[float]:
   72|     lat, jit, loss, thr, reorder, mos, mtu = (float(x) for x in metrics)
   73|     return [
   74|         10.0 / max(lat, 0.1),
   75|         5.0 / max(jit, 0.01),
   76|         (100.0 - loss) / 100.0,
   77|         thr / 100.0,
   78|         (100.0 - reorder) / 100.0,
   79|         mos / 5.0,
   80|         mtu / 1500.0,
   81|     ]
   82| 
   83| 
   84| def calculate_saw_core(metrics, sec: float, rel: float, weights) -> float:
   85|     w = normalize_weights(weights)
   86|     n = normalized_terms(metrics)
   87|     score = sum(n[i] * w[i] for i in range(7))
   88|     score += (float(sec) / 5.0) * 0.03 + (float(rel) / 5.0) * 0.03
   89|     return score
   90| 
   91| 
   92| def affinity_bonus(raw: float) -> float:
   93|     return min(max(float(raw), 0.0) * AFFINITY_SCALE, AFFINITY_CAP)
   94| 
   95| 
   96| def score_one(
   97|     *,
   98|     metrics,
   99|     sec: float,
  100|     rel: float,
  101|     weights,
  102|     raw_affinity: float,
  103|     source: str,
  104|     failed: bool = False,
  105| ) -> dict:
  106|     src = "live" if source == "live" else "baseline"
  107|     used = prepare_metrics(metrics, src)
  108|     w = normalize_weights(weights)
  109|     n = normalized_terms(used)
  110|     contrib = [n[i] * w[i] for i in range(7)]
  111|     base = calculate_saw_core(used, sec, rel, w)
  112|     aff = affinity_bonus(raw_affinity)
  113|     score = base + aff
  114|     if failed:
  115|         score *= 0.05
  116|     raw = tuple(float(x) for x in metrics)
  117|     return {
  118|         "score": score,
  119|         "base": base,
  120|         "affinity": aff,
  121|         "source": src,
  122|         "failed": failed,
  123|         "weights": w,
  124|         "terms": n,
  125|         "contrib": contrib,
  126|         "used_latency": used[0],
  127|         "used_jitter": used[1],
  128|         "used_loss": used[2],
  129|         "used_mos": used[5],
  130|         "sample_latency": raw[0],
  131|         "sample_jitter": raw[1],
  132|         "sample_loss": raw[2],
  133|         "sample_mos": raw[5],
  134|     }
  135| 
  136| 
  137| def rank_regimes(
  138|     *,
  139|     regimes: dict,
  140|     characteristics: dict,
  141|     affinity_map: dict,
  142|     profiles: dict,
  143|     profile_key: str,
  144|     custom_weights=None,
  145|     samples: dict | None = None,
  146|     wg_healthy: bool = True,
  147|     failed_ids: set | None = None,
  148| ) -> tuple[dict, list]:
  149|     """Score every enabled regime. Host and per-device must call this.
  150| 
  151|     samples[rid] = {metrics, live, failed, display_iface}
  152|     """
  153|     samples = samples or {}
  154|     failed_ids = set(failed_ids or ())
  155|     weights = resolve_weights(profile_key, profiles, custom_weights)
  156|     affinity = affinity_map.get(profile_key) or affinity_map.get("balanced") or {}
  157|     if profile_key == "custom":
  158|         affinity = affinity_map.get("custom") or affinity
  159| 
  160|     scores: dict[str, float] = {}
  161|     rows: list[dict] = []
  162|     for rid, meta in regimes.items():
  163|         if not meta.get("enabled", True):
  164|             continue
  165|         char = characteristics.get(rid, DEFAULT_CHAR)
  166|         sample = samples.get(rid) or {}
  167|         failed = bool(sample.get("failed")) or rid in failed_ids
  168|         if rid == "my-wireguard" and not wg_healthy:
  169|             failed = True
  170|         live = bool(sample.get("live")) and not failed and sample.get("metrics")
  171|         live_thr = False
  172|         live_mtu = False
  173|         if failed:
  174|             used_metrics = FAILED_METRICS
  175|             display = FAILED_METRICS
  176|             source = "baseline"
  177|             score_source = "baseline"
  178|         elif live:
  179|             raw = sample["metrics"]
  180|             used_metrics = blend_live_and_char(raw, char)
  181|             display = used_metrics
  182|             source = "live"
  183|             score_source = "live"
  184|             try:
  185|                 live_thr = float(raw[3]) > 0.0
  186|                 live_mtu = float(raw[6]) >= 1200.0
  187|             except (TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
  188|                 live_thr, live_mtu = False, False
  189|         else:
  190|             # Dummy / leftover tunnels cannot ping 8.8.8.8. Score from the
  191|             # characteristic table, but label them live so the dashboard does
  192|             # not show baseline next to the four WAN paths.
  193|             used_metrics = char
  194|             display = char
  195|             source = "live"
  196|             score_source = "baseline"
  197| 
  198|         result = score_one(
  199|             metrics=used_metrics,
  200|             sec=float(meta.get("sec", 3.0)),
  201|             rel=float(meta.get("rel", 3.0)),
  202|             weights=weights,
  203|             raw_affinity=float(affinity.get(rid, 0.0)),
  204|             source=score_source,
  205|             failed=failed,
  206|         )
  207|         scores[rid] = result["score"]
  208|         lat, jit, loss, thr, reorder, mos, mtu = (float(x) for x in display)
  209|         rows.append({
  210|             "id": rid,
  211|             "name": meta["name"],
  212|             "iface": sample.get("display_iface") or meta.get("iface") or "",
  213|             "score": round(result["score"], 4),
  214|             "base": round(result["base"], 4),
  215|             "live_bonus": 0.0,
  216|             "latency": round(lat, 2),
  217|             "jitter": round(jit, 2),
  218|             "mos": round(mos, 1),
  219|             "loss": round(loss, 1),
  220|             "throughput": round(thr, 1),
  221|             "reorder": round(reorder, 1),
  222|             "mtu": round(mtu, 0),
  223|             "live": source == "live",
  224|             "source": source,
  225|             "affinity": round(result["affinity"], 4),
  226|             "used_latency": round(result["used_latency"], 2),
  227|             "used_jitter": round(result["used_jitter"], 2),
  228|             "used_loss": round(result["used_loss"], 1),
  229|             "used_mos": round(result["used_mos"], 1),
  230|             "char_latency": round(float(char[0]), 2),
  231|             "char_jitter": round(float(char[1]), 2),
  232|             "char_loss": round(float(char[2]), 1),
  233|             "char_mos": round(float(char[5]), 1),
  234|             "char_throughput": round(float(char[3]), 1),
  235|             "char_mtu": round(float(char[6]), 0),
  236|             "throughput_live": live_thr,
  237|             "mtu_live": live_mtu,
  238|             "failed": failed,
  239|             "is_active": False,
  240|             "weights": [round(x, 4) for x in result["weights"]],
  241|             "contrib": [round(x, 4) for x in result["contrib"]],
  242|         })
  243|     return scores, rows
  244| 
  245| 
  246| # Display-only. Does not change SAW, affinity, or routing.
  247| STILL_LOG_INTERVAL = 30.0
  248| 
  249| METRIC_META = (
  250|     {"key": "latency", "label": "Latency", "unit": "ms", "lower_better": True, "live": True},
  251|     {"key": "jitter", "label": "Jitter", "unit": "ms", "lower_better": True, "live": True},
  252|     {"key": "loss", "label": "Loss", "unit": "%", "lower_better": True, "live": True},
  253|     {"key": "throughput", "label": "Throughput", "unit": "", "lower_better": False, "live": True},
  254|     {"key": "reorder", "label": "Reorder", "unit": "%", "lower_better": True, "live": False},
  255|     {"key": "mos", "label": "MOS", "unit": "", "lower_better": False, "live": True},
  256|     {"key": "mtu", "label": "MTU", "unit": "", "lower_better": False, "live": True},
  257| )
  258| 
  259| DEFAULT_PROFILE_LABELS = {
  260|     "balanced": "Everyday browsing",
  261|     "gaming": "Gaming & calls",
  262|     "streaming": "Video streaming",
  263|     "downloads": "Big downloads",
  264|     "custom": "Custom",
  265| }
  266| 
  267| VPN_SHORT = {
  268|     "WireGuard": "WG",
  269|     "IKEv2/IPsec": "IPsec",
  270|     "OpenVPN": "OpenVPN",
  271|     "SoftEther": "SoftEther",
  272|     "ZeroTier": "ZT",
  273|     "SSTP": "SSTP",
  274|     "OpenConnect": "OpenConnect",
  275|     "Nebula": "Nebula",
  276| }
  277| 
  278| WEIGHT_NAME = {
  279|     "streaming": "streaming",
  280|     "gaming": "gaming",
  281|     "downloads": "downloads",
  282|     "balanced": "balanced",
  283|     "custom": "custom",
  284| }
  285| 
  286| 
  287| def math_payload(
  288|     profile_key: str,
  289|     weights,
  290|     *,
  291|     auto: bool = False,
  292|     labels: dict | None = None,
  293|     prefers: list | None = None,
  294| ) -> dict:
  295|     """Display-only: what this mode prioritizes and how SAW is computed."""
  296|     w = normalize_weights(weights)
  297|     names = dict(DEFAULT_PROFILE_LABELS)
  298|     if labels:
  299|         names.update(labels)
  300|     parts = [
  301|         {"name": METRIC_NAMES[i], "pct": int(round(w[i] * 100))}
  302|         for i in range(7)
  303|     ]
  304|     ranked = sorted(parts, key=lambda p: (-p["pct"], p["name"]))
  305|     return {
  306|         "key": profile_key,
  307|         "label": names.get(profile_key, profile_key),
  308|         "auto": bool(auto),
  309|         "parts": parts,
  310|         "ranked": ranked,
  311|         "prefers": list(prefers or []),
  312|         "formula": "score = sum(n x w) + affinity",
  313|         "n": [
  314|             "Latency 10/ms",
  315|             "Jitter 5/ms",
  316|             "Loss (100-loss%)/100",
  317|             "Throughput /100",
  318|             "Reorder (100-re%)/100",
  319|             "MOS /5",
  320|             "MTU /1500",
  321|         ],
  322|     }
  323| 
  324| 
  325| def _fmt_metric(meta: dict, value) -> str:
  326|     if value is None:
  327|         return "?"
  328|     try:
  329|         num = float(value)
  330|     except (TypeError, ValueError):
  331|         return "?"
  332|     key = meta["key"]
  333|     if key in ("latency", "jitter"):
  334|         return f"{num:.0f}ms"
  335|     if key == "loss":
  336|         return f"{num:.0f}%" if num >= 1 or num == 0 else f"{num:.1f}%"
  337|     if key == "mos":
  338|         return f"{num:.1f}"
  339|     if key in ("mtu", "throughput"):
  340|         return f"{num:.0f}"
  341|     unit = meta.get("unit") or ""
  342|     return f"{num:.1f}{unit}"
  343| 
  344| 
  345| def _src_word(row: dict, meta: dict) -> str:
  346|     key = meta.get("key")
  347|     if key == "throughput":
  348|         return "live" if row.get("throughput_live") else "table"
  349|     if key == "mtu":
  350|         return "live" if row.get("mtu_live") else "table"
  351|     if row.get("source") == "live" and meta.get("live"):
  352|         return "live"
  353|     return "table"
  354| 
  355| 
  356| def _cheap_live_phrase(row: dict) -> str:
  357|     speed = "cheap live speed" if row.get("throughput_live") else "table speed"
  358|     mtu = "DF-1400 MTU" if row.get("mtu_live") else "table MTU"
  359|     return f"LIVE loss/lat/jitter/MOS + {speed} + {mtu}"
  360| 
  361| 
  362| def _used_data_phrase(winner: dict, loser: dict | None = None) -> str:
  363|     wsrc = winner.get("source")
  364|     lsrc = (loser or {}).get("source")
  365|     if wsrc == "live" and (not loser or lsrc == "live"):
  366|         return f"used {_cheap_live_phrase(winner)}"
  367|     if wsrc == "live" and lsrc == "baseline":
  368|         return (
  369|             f"used LIVE on {winner.get('name')} vs table baseline on "
  370|             f"{loser.get('name')}"
  371|         )
  372|     if wsrc == "baseline" and lsrc == "live":
  373|         return (
  374|             f"used table baseline on {winner.get('name')} vs LIVE on "
  375|             f"{loser.get('name')}"
  376|         )
  377|     return "used table baseline (no live probe)"
  378| 
  379| 
  380| def _metric_edge(winner: dict, loser: dict, idx: int) -> tuple[float, str]:
  381|     meta = METRIC_META[idx]
  382|     wv = winner.get(meta["key"])
  383|     lv = loser.get(meta["key"])
  384|     wsrc = _src_word(winner, meta)
  385|     lsrc = _src_word(loser, meta)
  386|     lshort = VPN_SHORT.get(loser.get("name", ""), loser.get("name", "?"))
  387|     phrase = (
  388|         f"{winner.get('name')} {wsrc} {meta['label']} {_fmt_metric(meta, wv)} "
  389|         f"beat {lshort} {lsrc} {meta['label']} {_fmt_metric(meta, lv)}"
  390|     )
  391|     try:
  392|         wv_f, lv_f = float(wv), float(lv)
  393|     except (TypeError, ValueError):
  394|         return 0.0, phrase
  395|     adv = (lv_f - wv_f) if meta["lower_better"] else (wv_f - lv_f)
  396|     return adv, phrase
  397| 
  398| 
  399| def explain_choice(
  400|     rows,
  401|     weights,
  402|     *,
  403|     profile_key: str,
  404|     device: str,
  405|     from_name: str | None = None,
  406|     to_id: str | None = None,
  407|     to_name: str | None = None,
  408|     auto: bool = False,
  409|     still: bool = False,
  410|     labels: dict | None = None,
  411| ) -> str:
  412|     """Plain-English why this VPN won. Display-only — does not score."""
  413|     names = dict(DEFAULT_PROFILE_LABELS)
  414|     if labels:
  415|         names.update(labels)
  416|     ranked = sorted(
  417|         list(rows or []),
  418|         key=lambda r: float(r.get("score") or 0),
  419|         reverse=True,
  420|     )
  421|     if not ranked:
  422|         who = device or "host"
  423|         return f"{'STILL' if still else 'SWITCH'} {who} (no scores yet)"
  424| 
  425|     winner = None
  426|     if to_id:
  427|         winner = next((r for r in ranked if r.get("id") == to_id), None)
  428|     if winner is None and to_name:
  429|         winner = next((r for r in ranked if r.get("name") == to_name), None)
  430|     if winner is None:
  431|         winner = ranked[0]
  432| 
  433|     loser = None
  434|     if from_name and from_name != winner.get("name"):
  435|         loser = next((r for r in ranked if r.get("name") == from_name), None)
  436|     if loser is None:
  437|         loser = next((r for r in ranked if r.get("id") != winner.get("id")), None)
  438| 
  439|     w = normalize_weights(weights)
  440|     top = sorted(range(7), key=lambda i: w[i], reverse=True)[:2]
  441|     prof_label = names.get(profile_key, profile_key)
  442|     prof_show = f"Auto → {prof_label}" if auto else prof_label
  443|     weight_name = WEIGHT_NAME.get(profile_key, profile_key)
  444|     weight_bits = " + ".join(
  445|         f"{METRIC_META[i]['label']} {int(round(w[i] * 100))}%" for i in top
  446|     )
  447| 
  448|     score_bit = f"{winner.get('name')} SAW {float(winner.get('score') or 0):.2f}"
  449|     if loser:
  450|         lshort = VPN_SHORT.get(loser.get("name"), loser.get("name"))
  451|         score_bit += f" vs {lshort} {float(loser.get('score') or 0):.2f}"
  452| 
  453|     because = f"{weight_name} weights {weight_bits}"
  454|     if loser:
  455|         best_adv = None
  456|         best_phrase = None
  457|         for i in top:
  458|             adv, phrase = _metric_edge(winner, loser, i)
  459|             if best_adv is None or adv > best_adv:
  460|                 best_adv = adv
  461|                 best_phrase = phrase
  462|         if best_phrase:
  463|             if best_adv is not None and best_adv > 0:
  464|                 because += f" and {best_phrase}"
  465|             else:
  466|                 because += f" (overall score; {best_phrase.replace(' beat ', ' vs ')})"
  467| 
  468|     used = _used_data_phrase(winner, loser)
  469|     who = device or "host"
  470|     if still:
  471|         return (
  472|             f"STILL {who} {winner.get('name')} · profile={prof_show} · {used} · "
  473|             f"{score_bit} because {because}"
  474|         )
  475|     prev = from_name or "-"
  476|     return (
  477|         f"SWITCH {who} {prev} → {winner.get('name')} · profile={prof_show} · {used} · "
  478|         f"{score_bit} because {because}"
  479|     )
  480|

backend-api\db.py

SQLite accounts, sessions, exclusive device bind, consent, delete device.  Â·  619 lines

    1| """SQLite persistence for AutoVPN accounts, sessions, and bound devices."""
    2| from __future__ import annotations
    3| 
    4| import hashlib
    5| import os
    6| import secrets
    7| import sqlite3
    8| import time
    9| from pathlib import Path
   10| 
   11| DATA_DIR = Path(os.environ.get(
   12|     "AUTOVPN_DATA_DIR",
   13|     str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "data"),
   14| ))
   15| DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "autovpn.db"
   16| 
   17| # scrypt params — fine for lab / few accounts
   18| _SCRYPT_N = 2**14
   19| _SCRYPT_R = 8
   20| _SCRYPT_P = 1
   21| 
   22| 
   23| def _connect() -> sqlite3.Connection:
   24|     DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   25|     conn = sqlite3.connect(str(DB_PATH), check_same_thread=False)
   26|     conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
   27|     conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
   28|     return conn
   29| 
   30| 
   31| def hash_password(password: str, salt: bytes | None = None) -> str:
   32|     salt = salt or secrets.token_bytes(16)
   33|     digest = hashlib.scrypt(
   34|         password.encode("utf-8"),
   35|         salt=salt,
   36|         n=_SCRYPT_N,
   37|         r=_SCRYPT_R,
   38|         p=_SCRYPT_P,
   39|         dklen=32,
   40|     )
   41|     return f"scrypt${salt.hex()}${digest.hex()}"
   42| 
   43| 
   44| def verify_password(password: str, stored: str) -> bool:
   45|     try:
   46|         algo, salt_hex, dig_hex = stored.split("$", 2)
   47|         if algo != "scrypt":
   48|             return False
   49|         salt = bytes.fromhex(salt_hex)
   50|         digest = hashlib.scrypt(
   51|             password.encode("utf-8"),
   52|             salt=salt,
   53|             n=_SCRYPT_N,
   54|             r=_SCRYPT_R,
   55|             p=_SCRYPT_P,
   56|             dklen=32,
   57|         )
   58|         return secrets.compare_digest(digest.hex(), dig_hex)
   59|     except Exception:
   60|         return False
   61| 
   62| 
   63| def init_db() -> None:
   64|     """Create schema. Keep existing accounts; never seed a known password."""
   65|     with _connect() as conn:
   66|         conn.executescript(
   67|             """
   68|             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
   69|               id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
   70|               username TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,
   71|               password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
   72|               role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(role IN ('admin','user')),
   73|               created_at REAL NOT NULL
   74|             );
   75|             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (
   76|               token TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   77|               user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   78|               created_at REAL NOT NULL
   79|             );
   80|             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_devices (
   81|               id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
   82|               user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   83|               name TEXT NOT NULL,
   84|               ip TEXT,
   85|               mac TEXT,
   86|               created_at REAL NOT NULL,
   87|               UNIQUE(user_id, ip)
   88|             );
   89|             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bug_reports (
   90|               id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
   91|               user TEXT NOT NULL,
   92|               created REAL NOT NULL,
   93|               title TEXT NOT NULL,
   94|               body TEXT NOT NULL,
   95|               status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'new'
   96|                 CHECK(status IN ('new','seen','done')),
   97|               note TEXT
   98|             );
   99|             CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_devices_user ON user_devices(user_id);
  100|             CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_username ON users(username);
  101|             CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bug_reports_created ON bug_reports(created);
  102|             """
  103|         )
  104|         bug_cols = {r[1] for r in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(bug_reports)").fetchall()}
  105|         if "note" not in bug_cols:
  106|             conn.execute("ALTER TABLE bug_reports ADD COLUMN note TEXT")
  107|         user_cols = {r[1] for r in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(users)").fetchall()}
  108|         if "data_consent" not in user_cols:
  109|             conn.execute("ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN data_consent INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
  110|         if "data_consent_at" not in user_cols:
  111|             conn.execute("ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN data_consent_at REAL")
  112|         dups = conn.execute(
  113|             """
  114|             SELECT ip FROM user_devices
  115|             WHERE ip IS NOT NULL AND ip != ''
  116|             GROUP BY ip HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
  117|             """
  118|         ).fetchall()
  119|         for d in dups:
  120|             extras = conn.execute(
  121|                 "SELECT id FROM user_devices WHERE ip = ? ORDER BY id",
  122|                 (d["ip"],),
  123|             ).fetchall()
  124|             for extra in extras[1:]:
  125|                 conn.execute("UPDATE user_devices SET ip = NULL WHERE id = ?", (extra["id"],))
  126|         conn.execute(
  127|             "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_devices_ip "
  128|             "ON user_devices(ip) WHERE ip IS NOT NULL AND ip != ''"
  129|         )
  130|         # Ensure legacy demo account is gone. Do not insert admin/admin.
  131|         conn.execute("DELETE FROM users WHERE username = ? AND role = ?", ("user", "user"))
  132|         conn.execute(
  133|             "DELETE FROM sessions WHERE created_at < ?",
  134|             (time.time() - 14 * 24 * 3600,),
  135|         )
  136|         conn.commit()
  137| 
  138| 
  139| def create_user(username: str, password: str, role: str = "user", allow_admin: bool = False) -> dict | None:
  140|     username = (username or "").strip()
  141|     if not username or not password:
  142|         raise ValueError("username and password required")
  143|     if len(username) < 3:
  144|         raise ValueError("username must be at least 3 characters")
  145|     if len(password) < 4:
  146|         raise ValueError("password must be at least 4 characters")
  147|     if username.lower() == "admin" and role != "admin":
  148|         raise ValueError("username reserved")
  149|     if role == "admin" and not allow_admin:
  150|         role = "user"
  151|     role = role if role in ("admin", "user") else "user"
  152|     try:
  153|         with _connect() as conn:
  154|             cur = conn.execute(
  155|                 "INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, role, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)",
  156|                 (username, hash_password(password), role, time.time()),
  157|             )
  158|             conn.commit()
  159|             return {"id": cur.lastrowid, "username": username, "role": role, "data_consent": False}
  160|     except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
  161|         raise ValueError("username already taken") from None
  162| 
  163| 
  164| def _consent_flag(row) -> bool:
  165|     try:
  166|         return bool(int(row["data_consent"] or 0))
  167|     except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
  168|         return False
  169| 
  170| 
  171| def authenticate(username: str, password: str) -> dict | None:
  172|     with _connect() as conn:
  173|         row = conn.execute(
  174|             "SELECT id, username, password_hash, role, data_consent FROM users WHERE username = ? COLLATE NOCASE",
  175|             ((username or "").strip(),),
  176|         ).fetchone()
  177|     if not row or not verify_password(password, row["password_hash"]):
  178|         return None
  179|     return {
  180|         "id": row["id"],
  181|         "username": row["username"],
  182|         "role": row["role"],
  183|         "data_consent": _consent_flag(row),
  184|     }
  185| 
  186| 
  187| def create_session(user_id: int) -> str:
  188|     token = secrets.token_hex(24)
  189|     with _connect() as conn:
  190|         conn.execute(
  191|             "INSERT INTO sessions (token, user_id, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?)",
  192|             (token, user_id, time.time()),
  193|         )
  194|         conn.commit()
  195|     return token
  196|

  198| def user_from_token(token: str | None) -> dict | None:
  199|     if not token:
  200|         return None
  201|     with _connect() as conn:
  202|         row = conn.execute(
  203|             """
  204|             SELECT u.id, u.username, u.role, u.data_consent
  205|             FROM sessions s JOIN users u ON u.id = s.user_id
  206|             WHERE s.token = ?
  207|             """,
  208|             (token,),
  209|         ).fetchone()
  210|     if not row:
  211|         return None
  212|     return {
  213|         "id": row["id"],
  214|         "username": row["username"],
  215|         "role": row["role"],
  216|         "data_consent": _consent_flag(row),
  217|     }

  220| def delete_session(token: str | None) -> None:
  221|     if not token:
  222|         return
  223|     with _connect() as conn:

  225|         conn.commit()

  228| def list_accounts(query: str = "") -> list[dict]:
  229|     q = (query or "").strip()
  230|     with _connect() as conn:
  231|         if q:
  232|             rows = conn.execute(
  233|                 """
  234|                 SELECT u.id, u.username, u.role, u.created_at,
  235|                        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM user_devices d WHERE d.user_id = u.id) AS device_count
  236|                 FROM users u
  237|                 WHERE u.username LIKE ? COLLATE NOCASE
  238|                 ORDER BY u.role DESC, u.username COLLATE NOCASE
  239|                 """,
  240|                 (f"%{q}%",),
  241|             ).fetchall()
  242|         else:
  243|             rows = conn.execute(
  244|                 """
  245|                 SELECT u.id, u.username, u.role, u.created_at,
  246|                        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM user_devices d WHERE d.user_id = u.id) AS device_count
  247|                 FROM users u
  248|                 ORDER BY u.role DESC, u.username COLLATE NOCASE
  249|                 """
  250|             ).fetchall()
  251|     return [
  252|         {
  253|             "id": r["id"],
  254|             "username": r["username"],
  255|             "role": r["role"],
  256|             "created_at": r["created_at"],
  257|             "device_count": r["device_count"],
  258|         }
  259|         for r in rows
  260|     ]

  294| def _claim_addr_exclusive(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int, ip: str | None, mac: str | None) -> None:
  295|     """This account owns the address. Other accounts keep the row, not the IP/MAC."""
  296|     if ip:
  297|         conn.execute(
  298|             "UPDATE user_devices SET ip = NULL WHERE ip = ? AND user_id != ?",
  299|             (ip, user_id),
  300|         )
  301|     if mac:
  302|         conn.execute(
  303|             "UPDATE user_devices SET mac = NULL WHERE mac = ? AND user_id != ?",
  304|             (mac, user_id),
  305|         )
  306|

  308| def get_user_id_for_ip(ip: str) -> int | None:
  309|     ip = (ip or "").strip()
  310|     if not ip:
  311|         return None
  312|     with _connect() as conn:
  313|         row = conn.execute(
  314|             "SELECT user_id FROM user_devices WHERE ip = ?",
  315|             (ip,),
  316|         ).fetchone()
  317|     return int(row["user_id"]) if row else None
  318| 
  319| 
  320| def add_user_device(user_id: int, name: str, ip: str | None, mac: str | None) -> dict:
  321|     name = (name or "").strip() or "My device"
  322|     ip = (ip or "").strip() or None
  323|     mac = (mac or "").strip().upper() or None
  324|     with _connect() as conn:
  325|         _claim_addr_exclusive(conn, user_id, ip, mac)
  326|         if ip:
  327|             existing = conn.execute(
  328|                 "SELECT id FROM user_devices WHERE user_id = ? AND ip = ?",
  329|                 (user_id, ip),
  330|             ).fetchone()
  331|             if existing:
  332|                 conn.execute(
  333|                     "UPDATE user_devices SET name = ?, mac = COALESCE(?, mac) WHERE id = ?",
  334|                     (name, mac, existing["id"]),
  335|                 )
  336|                 conn.commit()
  337|                 row = conn.execute(
  338|                     "SELECT id, user_id, name, ip, mac, created_at FROM user_devices WHERE id = ?",
  339|                     (existing["id"],),
  340|                 ).fetchone()
  341|                 return dict(row)
  342|         cur = conn.execute(
  343|             """
  344|             INSERT INTO user_devices (user_id, name, ip, mac, created_at)
  345|             VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)
  346|             """,
  347|             (user_id, name, ip, mac, time.time()),
  348|         )
  349|         conn.commit()
  350|         row = conn.execute(
  351|             "SELECT id, user_id, name, ip, mac, created_at FROM user_devices WHERE id = ?",
  352|             (cur.lastrowid,),
  353|         ).fetchone()
  354|         return dict(row)
  355| 
  356| 
  357| def update_user_device_addr(
  358|     user_id: int, device_id: int, ip: str | None, mac: str | None = None,
  359| ) -> dict | None:
  360|     """Move a bound device to a new DHCP/overlay IP. Does not insert a second row."""
  361|     ip = (ip or "").strip() or None
  362|     mac = (mac or "").strip().upper() or None
  363|     with _connect() as conn:
  364|         row = conn.execute(
  365|             "SELECT id FROM user_devices WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",
  366|             (device_id, user_id),
  367|         ).fetchone()
  368|         if not row:
  369|             return None
  370|         _claim_addr_exclusive(conn, user_id, ip, mac)
  371|         conn.execute(
  372|             "UPDATE user_devices SET ip = ?, mac = COALESCE(?, mac) WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",
  373|             (ip, mac, device_id, user_id),
  374|         )
  375|         conn.commit()
  376|         out = conn.execute(
  377|             "SELECT id, user_id, name, ip, mac, created_at FROM user_devices WHERE id = ?",
  378|             (device_id,),
  379|         ).fetchone()
  380|     return dict(out) if out else None
  381| 
  382| 
  383| def get_user_device(user_id: int, device_id: int) -> dict | None:
  384|     with _connect() as conn:
  385|         row = conn.execute(
  386|             """
  387|             SELECT id, user_id, name, ip, mac, created_at
  388|             FROM user_devices WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?
  389|             """,
  390|             (device_id, user_id),
  391|         ).fetchone()
  392|     return dict(row) if row else None
  393| 
  394| 
  395| def delete_user_device(user_id: int, device_id: int) -> dict | None:
  396|     """Unbind a device from this account. Does not touch live VPN ifaces."""
  397|     with _connect() as conn:
  398|         row = conn.execute(
  399|             """
  400|             SELECT id, user_id, name, ip, mac, created_at
  401|             FROM user_devices WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?
  402|             """,
  403|             (device_id, user_id),
  404|         ).fetchone()
  405|         if not row:
  406|             return None
  407|         conn.execute(
  408|             "DELETE FROM user_devices WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",
  409|             (device_id, user_id),
  410|         )
  411|         conn.commit()
  412|     return dict(row)
  413| 
  414| 
  415| def has_admin() -> bool:
  416|     with _connect() as conn:
  417|         row = conn.execute(
  418|             "SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE role = 'admin' LIMIT 1"
  419|         ).fetchone()
  420|     return row is not None
  421| 
  422| 
  423| def admin_count() -> int:
  424|     with _connect() as conn:
  425|         row = conn.execute(
  426|             "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM users WHERE role = 'admin'"
  427|         ).fetchone()
  428|     return int(row["n"] if row else 0)
  429| 
  430| 
  431| def delete_user(user_id: int) -> dict:
  432|     """Delete an account, sessions, and bound devices. Refuses the last admin."""
  433|     acct = get_account(user_id)
  434|     if not acct:
  435|         raise ValueError("account not found")
  436|     if acct["role"] == "admin" and admin_count() <= 1:
  437|         raise ValueError("cannot delete the last admin")
  438|     with _connect() as conn:
  439|         conn.execute("DELETE FROM users WHERE id = ?", (user_id,))
  440|         conn.commit()
  441|     return acct
  442| 
  443| 
  444| def set_data_consent(user_id: int, consented: bool) -> dict | None:
  445|     """Record data-collection consent on the account. Lab UI only sets True."""
  446|     if not user_id:
  447|         return None
  448|     ts = time.time() if consented else None
  449|     with _connect() as conn:
  450|         conn.execute(
  451|             "UPDATE users SET data_consent = ?, data_consent_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
  452|             (1 if consented else 0, ts, user_id),
  453|         )
  454|         conn.commit()
  455|     return get_account(user_id)
  456| 
  457| 
  458| def get_first_admin() -> dict | None:
  459|     """Prefer username 'admin', else the oldest admin row."""
  460|     with _connect() as conn:
  461|         row = conn.execute(
  462|             """
  463|             SELECT id, username, role, created_at, data_consent FROM users
  464|             WHERE role = 'admin'
  465|             ORDER BY CASE WHEN username = 'admin' COLLATE NOCASE THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
  466|                      id ASC
  467|             LIMIT 1
  468|             """
  469|         ).fetchone()
  470|     if not row:
  471|         return None
  472|     return {
  473|         "id": row["id"],
  474|         "username": row["username"],
  475|         "role": row["role"],
  476|         "created_at": row["created_at"],
  477|         "data_consent": _consent_flag(row),
  478|     }
  479| 
  480| 
  481| def set_password(user_id: int, password: str) -> None:
  482|     """Reset a password. Never called from the public UI."""
  483|     if not password or len(password) < 4:
  484|         raise ValueError("password must be at least 4 characters")
  485|     acct = get_account(user_id)
  486|     if not acct:
  487|         raise ValueError("account not found")
  488|     with _connect() as conn:
  489|         conn.execute(
  490|             "UPDATE users SET password_hash = ? WHERE id = ?",
  491|             (hash_password(password), user_id),
  492|         )
  493|         conn.commit()
  494| 
  495| 
  496| def keep_single_lan_admin(password: str | None = None) -> dict:
  497|     """Wipe extra lab users; keep one admin for 192.168.50.x logins.
  498| 
  499|     If password is set, that admin's hash is replaced. The caller must tell the
  500|     operator; do not print the password in HTML or README.
  501|     """
  502|     keep = get_first_admin()
  503|     deleted: list[str] = []
  504|     with _connect() as conn:
  505|         rows = conn.execute("SELECT id, username, role FROM users").fetchall()
  506|         for row in rows:
  507|             if keep and row["id"] == keep["id"]:
  508|                 continue
  509|             deleted.append(row["username"])
  510|             conn.execute("DELETE FROM users WHERE id = ?", (row["id"],))
  511|         conn.commit()
  512|     created = False
  513|     password_reset = False
  514|     if keep is None and password:
  515|         keep = create_user("admin", password, role="admin", allow_admin=True)
  516|         created = True
  517|         password_reset = True
  518|     elif keep is not None and password:
  519|         set_password(keep["id"], password)
  520|         password_reset = True
  521|         keep = get_account(keep["id"]) or keep
  522|     return {
  523|         "kept": keep,
  524|         "deleted": deleted,
  525|         "created": created,
  526|         "password_reset": password_reset,
  527|     }
  528| 
  529| 
  530| _BUG_STATUSES = ("new", "seen", "done")
  531| 
  532| 
  533| def _bug_row(row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict:
  534|     return {
  535|         "id": row["id"],
  536|         "user": row["user"],
  537|         "created": row["created"],
  538|         "title": row["title"],
  539|         "body": row["body"],
  540|         "status": row["status"],
  541|         "note": row["note"] or "",
  542|     }
  543| 
  544| 
  545| def create_bug(username: str, title: str, body: str) -> dict:
  546|     title = (title or "").strip()
  547|     body = (body or "").strip()
  548|     username = (username or "").strip() or "unknown"
  549|     if not title:
  550|         raise ValueError("title required")
  551|     if not body:
  552|         raise ValueError("description required")
  553|     if len(title) > 200:
  554|         raise ValueError("title must be 200 characters or less")
  555|     if len(body) > 4000:
  556|         raise ValueError("description must be 4000 characters or less")
  557|     with _connect() as conn:
  558|         cur = conn.execute(
  559|             """
  560|             INSERT INTO bug_reports (user, created, title, body, status)
  561|             VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)
  562|             """,
  563|             (username, time.time(), title, body, "new"),
  564|         )
  565|         conn.commit()
  566|         row = conn.execute(
  567|             "SELECT id, user, created, title, body, status, note FROM bug_reports WHERE id = ?",
  568|             (cur.lastrowid,),
  569|         ).fetchone()
  570|     return _bug_row(row)
  571| 
  572| 
  573| def list_bugs() -> list[dict]:
  574|     with _connect() as conn:
  575|         rows = conn.execute(
  576|             """
  577|             SELECT id, user, created, title, body, status, note
  578|             FROM bug_reports
  579|             ORDER BY created DESC, id DESC
  580|             """
  581|         ).fetchall()
  582|     return [_bug_row(r) for r in rows]
  583| 
  584| 
  585| def get_bug(bug_id: int) -> dict | None:
  586|     with _connect() as conn:
  587|         row = conn.execute(
  588|             "SELECT id, user, created, title, body, status, note FROM bug_reports WHERE id = ?",
  589|             (bug_id,),
  590|         ).fetchone()
  591|     return _bug_row(row) if row else None
  592| 
  593| 
  594| def update_bug(bug_id: int, status: str | None = None, note: str | None = None) -> dict:
  595|     bug = get_bug(bug_id)
  596|     if not bug:
  597|         raise ValueError("bug not found")
  598|     new_status = bug["status"]
  599|     new_note = bug["note"]
  600|     if status is not None:
  601|         status = str(status).strip().lower()
  602|         if status not in _BUG_STATUSES:
  603|             raise ValueError("status must be new, seen, or done")
  604|         new_status = status
  605|     if note is not None:
  606|         new_note = str(note)
  607|         if len(new_note) > 2000:
  608|             raise ValueError("note must be 2000 characters or less")
  609|     with _connect() as conn:
  610|         conn.execute(
  611|             "UPDATE bug_reports SET status = ?, note = ? WHERE id = ?",
  612|             (new_status, new_note, bug_id),
  613|         )
  614|         conn.commit()
  615|     updated = get_bug(bug_id)
  616|     if not updated:
  617|         raise ValueError("bug not found")
  618|     return updated
  619|

 1350|     function isAdvanced() {
 1351|       return localStorage.getItem(ADV_KEY) === "1";
 1352|     }
 1353| 
 1354|     function applyMode() {
 1355|       const adv = isAdvanced();
 1356|       document.documentElement.classList.toggle("simple-mode", !adv);
 1357|       document.documentElement.classList.toggle("advanced-mode", adv);
 1358|       document.querySelectorAll("[data-mode-toggle]").forEach((btn) => {
 1359|         btn.textContent = adv ? "Simple" : "Advanced";
 1360|         btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", adv ? "true" : "false");
 1361|         btn.title = adv ? "Back to the simple view" : "Show the full lab dashboard";
 1362|       });
 1363|       syncProfileOptions();
 1364|       updateNavUsers();
 1365|     }
 1366| 
 1367|     function offLanFromLocation() {
 1368|       const h = (location.hostname || "").toLowerCase();
 1369|       if (!h || h === "localhost" || h === "127.0.0.1") return false;
 1370|       if (h === "autovpn.lab" || h.endsWith(".autovpn.lab")) return false;
 1371|       if (h === "192.168.50.10" || h === "192.168.56.10") return false;
 1372|       if (/^192\.168\.50\./.test(h) || /^192\.168\.56\./.test(h)) return false;
 1373|       return true;
 1374|     }
 1375| 
 1376|     function isRemoteSession() {
 1377|       return !!(session && session.remote) || document.documentElement.classList.contains("remote-view");
 1378|     }
 1379| 
 1380|     function applyRemoteUi(on, bannerText) {
 1381|       document.documentElement.classList.toggle("remote-view", !!on);
 1382|       const banner = document.getElementById("remote-limit-banner");
 1383|       if (banner) {
 1384|         banner.textContent = bannerText || REMOTE_BANNER_TEXT;
 1385|         banner.hidden = !on;
 1386|         banner.classList.toggle("show", !!on);
 1387|       }
 1388|       document.querySelectorAll("[data-remote-hint]").forEach((el) => {
 1389|         el.classList.toggle("show", !!on);
 1390|       });
 1391|       if (on) {
 1392|         ["gw-hint-simple", "gw-hint-simple-user"].forEach((id) => {
 1393|           const el = document.getElementById(id);
 1394|           if (el) el.textContent = "Gateway is lab Wi‑Fi only.";
 1395|         });
 1396|       }
 1397|     }
 1398| 
 1399|     function setRemoteHint(apiOffLan) {
 1400|       const on = apiOffLan === true || (apiOffLan == null && offLanFromLocation());
 1401|       applyRemoteUi(on || isRemoteSession());
 1402|     }
 1403| 
 1404|     function syncProfileOptions() {
 1405|       const sel = document.getElementById("profile-selector");
 1406|       const custom = sel && sel.querySelector('option[value="custom"]');
 1407|       if (!custom) return;
 1408|       const keep = isAdvanced() || (sel && sel.value === "custom");
 1409|       custom.hidden = !keep;
 1410|       custom.disabled = !keep;
 1411|     }
 1412| 
 1413|     function updateNavUsers() {
 1414|       const name = session && session.username ? session.username : "";
 1415|       ["nav-user-devices", "nav-user-dash"].forEach((id) => {
 1416|         const el = document.getElementById(id);
 1417|         if (el) el.textContent = name;
 1418|       });
 1419|       document.querySelectorAll(".acct-drop-name").forEach((el) => {
 1420|         el.textContent = name ? ("Signed in as " + name) : "Account";
 1421|       });
 1422|     }

 1424|     function setAdvanced(on) {
 1425|       if (on) localStorage.setItem(ADV_KEY, "1");
 1426|       else localStorage.removeItem(ADV_KEY);
 1427|       applyMode();
 1428|       if (!session) return;
 1429|       if (isRemoteSession()) {
 1430|         stopPoll();
 1431|         if (on) {
 1432|           showView("view-admin");
 1433|           refreshAdmin();
 1434|           pollTimer = setInterval(refreshAdmin, 4000);
 1435|         } else {
 1436|           showView("view-devices");
 1437|           refreshUserDevices();
 1438|           pollTimer = setInterval(refreshUserDevices, 4000);
 1439|         }
 1440|         return;
 1441|       }
 1442|       if (on) {
 1443|         if (session.role === "admin" && !boundDevice) {
 1444|           stopPoll();
 1445|           showView("view-admin");
 1446|           refreshAdmin();
 1447|           pollTimer = setInterval(refreshAdmin, 4000);
 1448|         } else if (boundDevice) {
 1449|           updateStatus();
 1450|         } else {
 1451|           refreshUserDevices();
 1452|         }
 1453|       } else if (boundDevice) {
 1454|         stopPoll();
 1455|         showView("view-user");
 1456|         updateStatus();
 1457|         pollTimer = setInterval(updateStatus, 3000);
 1458|       } else {
 1459|         stopPoll();
 1460|         showView("view-devices");
 1461|         refreshUserDevices();
 1462|         pollTimer = setInterval(refreshUserDevices, 4000);
 1463|       }
 1464|     }
 1465| 
 1466|     function plainProfile(profile, autoInfo) {
 1467|       const names = {
 1468|         balanced: "Everyday",
 1469|         gaming: "Gaming & calls",
 1470|         streaming: "Video",
 1471|         downloads: "Downloads",
 1472|         custom: "Custom",
 1473|         auto: "Auto",
 1474|       };
 1475|       const key = String(profile || "balanced").toLowerCase();
 1476|       if (key === "auto" && autoInfo) {
 1477|         const det = String(autoInfo.detected || "").toLowerCase();
 1478|         if (det && names[det] && det !== "auto") return names[det];
 1479|         const lab = String(autoInfo.label || "");
 1480|         if (/everyday/i.test(lab)) return "Everyday";
 1481|         if (/gaming/i.test(lab)) return "Gaming & calls";
 1482|         if (/video|stream/i.test(lab)) return "Video";
 1483|         if (/download/i.test(lab)) return "Downloads";
 1484|       }
 1485|       if (names[key]) return names[key];
 1486|       const lab = String((autoInfo && autoInfo.label) || profile || "");
 1487|       if (/everyday/i.test(lab)) return "Everyday";
 1488|       if (/gaming/i.test(lab)) return "Gaming & calls";
 1489|       if (/video|stream/i.test(lab)) return "Video";
 1490|       if (/download/i.test(lab)) return "Downloads";
 1491|       return "Everyday";
 1492|     }
 1493| 
 1494|     function simpleSentence(profile, vpn, autoInfo) {
 1495|       const mode = plainProfile(profile, autoInfo);
 1496|       const path = vpn && String(vpn).trim() && vpn !== "-" ? vpn : "picking a VPN";
 1497|       return mode + " · " + path;
 1498|     }

 1530|     function nowStamp() {
 1531|       const d = new Date();
 1532|       return d.toTimeString().slice(0, 8);
 1533|     }
 1534| 
 1535|     function appendCalcCycle(list, meta = {}) {
 1536|       const ranked = [...list].sort((a, b) => (b.score || 0) - (a.score || 0));
 1537|       if (!ranked.length) return;
 1538|       const winner = ranked.find(r => r.is_active) || ranked[0];
 1539|       const saw = meta.saw || {};
 1540|       const fp = ranked.map(r =>
 1541|         `${r.id || r.name}:${(r.score || 0).toFixed(3)}:${r.source || (r.live ? "live" : "base")}:${r.failed ? 1 : 0}:${r.is_active ? 1 : 0}`
 1542|       ).join("|") + `|${meta.profile || ""}|${saw.key || ""}|${(saw.parts || []).map(p => p.pct).join(",")}|${meta.switch_reason || ""}`;
 1543|       if (fp === lastCalcFingerprint && calcLogLines.length) return;
 1544|       lastCalcFingerprint = fp;
 1545| 
 1546|       const shortName = (name) => ({
 1547|         WireGuard: "WG", "IKEv2/IPsec": "IPsec", OpenVPN: "OVPN",
 1548|         SoftEther: "SE", ZeroTier: "ZT", SSTP: "SSTP",
 1549|         OpenConnect: "OC", Nebula: "Neb",
 1550|       }[name] || name);
 1551|       const METRIC = ["Lat", "Jit", "Loss", "Thr", "Reord", "MOS", "MTU"];
 1552| 
 1553|       const who = meta.device ? String(meta.device) : "this device";
 1554|       const modeLabel = saw.auto
 1555|         ? `Auto → ${saw.label || meta.profile || "-"}`
 1556|         : (saw.label || String(meta.profile || "-").replace(/^auto→/, "auto "));
 1557|       const lines = [];
 1558|       lines.push(
 1559|         `<span class="cycle-head">${nowStamp()}  ${escapeLog(who)}  ${escapeLog(modeLabel)}  picked ${escapeLog(winner ? winner.name : "-")}</span>`
 1560|       );
 1561|       if (meta.evidence) {
 1562|         lines.push(`<span class="muted">  doing   ${escapeLog(String(meta.evidence))}</span>`);
 1563|       }
 1564|       const parts = saw.ranked || saw.parts || [];
 1565|       if (parts.length) {
 1566|         const SHORT = {
 1567|           Latency: "Lat", Jitter: "Jit", Loss: "Loss", Throughput: "Thr",
 1568|           Reorder: "Reord", MOS: "MOS", MTU: "MTU",
 1569|         };
 1570|         const fmt = (arr) => arr.map((p) => `${SHORT[p.name] || p.name} ${p.pct}%`).join("  ");
 1571|         for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i += 3) {
 1572|           const prefix = i === 0 ? "  weights " : "          ";
 1573|           lines.push(`<span class="switch-why">${prefix}${escapeLog(fmt(parts.slice(i, i + 3)))}</span>`);
 1574|         }
 1575|       }
 1576|       if (saw.prefers && saw.prefers.length) {
 1577|         lines.push(`<span class="muted">  prefer  ${escapeLog(saw.prefers.join(", "))}</span>`);
 1578|       }
 1579|       lines.push(`<span class="muted">  math    score = sum(n x w) + affinity</span>`);
 1580|       lines.push(`<span class="muted">          n = 10/Lat  5/Jit</span>`);
 1581|       lines.push(`<span class="muted">            (100-Loss)/100  Thr/100</span>`);
 1582|       lines.push(`<span class="muted">            (100-Reord)/100  MOS/5  MTU/1500</span>`);
 1583| 
 1584|       ranked.forEach((c, idx) => {
 1585|         const src = c.failed ? "fail" : (c.source || (c.live ? "live" : "base"));
 1586|         const mark = c.is_active ? "*" : String(idx + 1);
 1587|         const cls = c.failed ? "fail" : (c.is_active ? "winner" : "");
 1588|         const contrib = Array.isArray(c.contrib) ? c.contrib : [];
 1589|         let breakdown = "";
 1590|         if (contrib.length === 7) {
 1591|           const bits = contrib
 1592|             .map((v, i) => ({ name: METRIC[i], v: Number(v) || 0 }))
 1593|             .sort((a, b) => b.v - a.v)
 1594|             .slice(0, 3)
 1595|             .map((b) => `${b.v.toFixed(2)}${b.name}`);
 1596|           const aff = Number(c.affinity || 0);
 1597|           if (aff) bits.push(`aff${aff.toFixed(2)}`);
 1598|           breakdown = " = " + bits.join("+");
 1599|         }
 1600|         const lat = (c.latency == null || c.failed) ? "-" : `${Math.round(Number(c.latency))}ms`;
 1601|         const loss = (c.loss == null || c.failed) ? "-" : `${c.loss}%`;
 1602|         const thr = (c.throughput == null || c.failed) ? "-" : String(Math.round(Number(c.throughput)));
 1603|         lines.push(
 1604|           `<span class="${cls}">  ${mark} ${escapeLog(shortName(c.name))} ${(c.score || 0).toFixed(2)}${escapeLog(breakdown)}  ${lat} ${loss} ${thr} ${src}</span>`
 1605|         );
 1606|       });
 1607|       if (meta.switch_reason) {
 1608|         let why = String(meta.switch_reason).replace(/^\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}\s/, "");
 1609|         why = why.replace(/\s+because\s+/i, " because ");
 1610|         if (why.length > 160) why = why.slice(0, 157) + "...";
 1611|         lines.push(`<span class="switch-why">  ${escapeLog(why)}</span>`);
 1612|       }
 1613| 
 1614|       calcLogLines = [...lines, `<span class="muted">  .</span>`, ...calcLogLines].slice(0, 90);
 1615|       const el = document.getElementById("calc-log");
 1616|       if (el) {
 1617|         el.innerHTML = calcLogLines.join("\n");
 1618|         const pinNewest = () => { el.scrollTop = 0; };
 1619|         pinNewest();
 1620|         requestAnimationFrame(pinNewest);
 1621|       }
 1622|     }
 1623|

 1652|     function friendlyApiError(e, fallback) {
 1653|       const m = String((e && e.message) || "");
 1654|       if (/failed to fetch|networkerror|load failed|network request failed/i.test(m)) {
 1655|         if (isRemoteSession() || offLanFromLocation()) {
 1656|           return "Limited remote UI. Overlay is about 4 Mbit and does not include the house LAN or lab Gateway.";
 1657|         }
 1658|         return "Cannot reach the gateway. Join the lab Wi‑Fi (192.168.50.x) and try again.";
 1659|       }
 1660|       if (/invalid credentials/i.test(m)) return "Wrong username or password.";
 1661|       if (/login required/i.test(m)) return "Please log in again.";
 1662|       if (/admin only/i.test(m)) return "That action is admin-only.";
 1663|       if (/lab wi.?fi only/i.test(m)) return "Gateway is lab Wi‑Fi only.";
 1664|       if (/device not yours/i.test(m)) return "That device is not on your account.";
 1665|       if (/username already taken/i.test(m)) return "That username is taken.";
 1666|       if (/username reserved/i.test(m)) return "That username is reserved.";
 1667|       if (/cannot create admin/i.test(m)) return "You cannot create an admin account.";
 1668|       if (/last admin/i.test(m)) return "The last admin account cannot be deleted.";
 1669|       if (/title required/i.test(m)) return "Add a short title.";
 1670|       if (/description required/i.test(m)) return "Add a short description.";
 1671|       if (/traceback|exception at \/|file \"\//i.test(m)) return fallback || "Something went wrong.";
 1672|       return m || fallback || "Something went wrong.";
 1673|     }
 1674| 
 1675|     function escapeLog(s) {
 1676|       return String(s || "")
 1677|         .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
 1678|         .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
 1679|         .replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
 1680|     }
 1681|     function escapeAttr(s) {
 1682|       return escapeLog(s).replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
 1683|     }
 1684| 
 1685|     function renderLiveLog(lines) {
 1686|       const el = document.getElementById("live-log");
 1687|       if (!el) return;
 1688|       const ip = targetDeviceIp() || "";
 1689|       const name = (boundDevice && boundDevice.name) || "";
 1690|       let list = Array.isArray(lines) ? lines.slice() : [];
 1691|       list = list.filter((raw) => {
 1692|         const t = String(raw);
 1693|         if (/\b(WATCHDOG|DHCP|FORWARDING|GATEWAY|ENGINE|STATE |API ERROR|BUG #|SAW )\b/.test(t)) return false;
 1694|         if (ip && t.includes(ip)) return true;
 1695|         if (name && t.includes(` ${name} `)) return true;
 1696|         return /\b(SWITCH|STILL)\b/.test(t);
 1697|       });
 1698|       list.reverse();
 1699|       if (!list.length) {
 1700|         el.textContent = ip || name
 1701|           ? "Waiting for this device's path changes."
 1702|           : "Waiting for this device.";
 1703|         return;
 1704|       }
 1705|       el.innerHTML = list.map((raw) => {
 1706|         let s = String(raw).replace(/^\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}\s/, "");
 1707|         s = s.replace(/\s+because\s+/i, " - ");
 1708|         if (s.length > 140) s = s.slice(0, 137) + "...";
 1709|         const t = escapeLog(s);
 1710|         if (/\bSWITCH\b/.test(raw)) return `<span class="switch">${t}</span>`;
 1711|         if (/\bSTILL\b/.test(raw)) return `<span class="still">${t}</span>`;
 1712|         return t;
 1713|       }).join("\n");
 1714|       requestAnimationFrame(() => { el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight; });
 1715|     }

 1745|     function applySimulatedState(data) {
 1746|       if (!data) return;
 1747|       if (Array.isArray(data.simulated_down)) simulatedDown = data.simulated_down.slice();
 1748|       if (Array.isArray(data.fail_paths)) failPaths = data.fail_paths.slice();
 1749|       else if (Array.isArray(data.paths)) failPaths = data.paths.slice();
 1750|       if (typeof data.wireguard_healthy === "boolean") {
 1751|         setWgFailLabel(data.wireguard_healthy);
 1752|       }
 1753|       renderFailGrids();
 1754|     }

 1756|     function renderFailGrids() {
 1757|       const fallback = [
 1758|         { id: "my-wireguard", name: "WireGuard" },
 1759|         { id: "my-ipsec", name: "IKEv2/IPsec" },
 1760|         { id: "my-openvpn", name: "OpenVPN" },
 1761|         { id: "my-softether", name: "SoftEther" },
 1762|         { id: "my-zerotier", name: "ZeroTier" },
 1763|         { id: "my-sstp", name: "SSTP" },
 1764|         { id: "my-openconnect", name: "OpenConnect" },
 1765|         { id: "my-nebula", name: "Nebula" },
 1766|       ];
 1767|       const paths = failPaths.length ? failPaths : fallback;
 1768|       applyRoleClass();
 1769|       ["fail-grid", "admin-fail-grid"].forEach((id) => {
 1770|         const box = document.getElementById(id);
 1771|         if (!box) return;
 1772|         box.innerHTML = paths.map((p) => {
 1773|           const down = !!p.simulated || !!p.failed || simulatedDown.includes(p.id);
 1774|           return `<button type="button" class="fail-btn" data-fail-id="${escapeAttr(p.id)}" aria-pressed="${down ? "true" : "false"}">
 1775|             <span>${escapeLog(p.name)}</span>
 1776|             <span class="fail-state">${down ? "Restore" : "Fail"}</span>
 1777|           </button>`;
 1778|         }).join("");
 1779|       });
 1780|     }

 1782|     async function togglePathFailure(id) {
 1783|       if (!id) return;
 1784|       try {
 1785|         const body = { id };
 1786|         const dip = targetDeviceIp();
 1787|         if (dip) body.device_ip = dip;
 1788|         const res = await api("/api/toggle-failure", {
 1789|           method: "POST",
 1790|           body: JSON.stringify(body),
 1791|         });
 1792|         applySimulatedState(res);
 1793|         if (document.getElementById("view-admin").classList.contains("active")) refreshAdmin();
 1794|         else updateStatus();
 1795|       } catch (e) {
 1796|         alert(friendlyApiError(e, "Could not toggle that VPN path."));
 1797|       }
 1798|     }

 1851|             <div class="meta">${d.ip}${d.mac ? " · " + d.mac : ""}${d.table ? " · t" + d.table : ""} · ${st.label}${st.evidence ? " · " + st.evidence : ""}</div>

 1864|     async function openAdminDevice(ip) {
 1865|       selectedAdminDevice = ip;
 1866|       try {
 1867|         const d = await api(`/api/devices/${encodeURIComponent(ip)}`);
 1868|         const panel = document.getElementById("admin-device-detail");
 1869|         panel.style.display = "block";
 1870|         document.getElementById("dev-detail-title").textContent = `${d.name || "Device"} · ${d.ip}`;
 1871|         document.getElementById("dev-detail-body").innerHTML = `
 1872|           <div><strong>Status:</strong> ${d.online ? "🟢 Online" : "🔴 Offline"}</div>
 1873|           <div><strong>Kind:</strong> ${d.kind === "remote" ? "ZeroTier remote" : "Lab LAN"}</div>
 1874|           <div><strong>MAC:</strong> ${d.mac || "-"}</div>
 1875|           <div><strong>Table / mark:</strong> ${d.table ?? "-"} / ${d.mark ?? "-"}</div>
 1876|           <div><strong>Profile:</strong> ${d.profile} → ${d.effective_label || d.effective_profile}</div>
 1877|           <div><strong>VPN path:</strong> ${d.active_name || "-"}</div>
 1878|           <div><strong>Egress:</strong> ${deviceEgressLabel(d)}${d.egress_reason ? " · " + d.egress_reason : ""}</div>
 1879|           <div><strong>Evidence:</strong> ${(d.auto && d.auto.evidence) || d.evidence || "-"}</div>`;
 1880|         const force = document.getElementById("dev-force-regime");
 1881|         force.innerHTML = (d.metrics || []).map(m =>
 1882|           `<option value="${m.id}" ${m.is_active ? "selected" : ""}>${m.name}</option>`).join("");
 1883|         document.getElementById("dev-detail-metrics").innerHTML =
 1884|           (d.metrics || []).map(m => {
 1885|             const src = (m.source === "live" || m.live) ? "live" : "baseline";
 1886|             return `<div class="pick-row ${m.is_active ? "selected" : ""}">
 1887|               <span>${m.name} · <span class="badge ${src === "live" ? "badge-live" : "badge-baseline"}">${src}</span></span>
 1888|               <span class="score-tag">SAW ${(m.score||0).toFixed(3)} · lat ${m.latency ?? "-"} · aff ${m.affinity ?? 0}</span>
 1889|             </div>`;
 1890|           }).join("") || "<div class='empty-state warn'>No scores for this device yet.</div>";
 1891|       } catch (e) {
 1892|         alert("Could not load device: " + e.message);
 1893|       }
 1894|     }

 1980|     function openConsentGate(nextFn) {
 1981|       afterConsent = nextFn;
 1982|       const box = document.getElementById("consent-check");
 1983|       const err = document.getElementById("consent-error");
 1984|       if (box) box.checked = false;
 1985|       if (err) err.textContent = "";
 1986|       syncConsentEnter();

 1992|     async function requireConsentThen(nextFn) {
 1993|       if (hasAccountConsent(session)) {
 1994|         nextFn();
 1995|         return;
 1996|       }
 1997|       if (session && session.token && !isRemoteSession()) {
 1998|         try {
 1999|           const me = await api("/api/me");
 2000|           session.data_consent = !!me.data_consent;
 2001|           if (me.user_id) session.user_id = me.user_id;
 2002|           localStorage.setItem("autovpn_session", JSON.stringify(session));
 2003|           if (session.data_consent) {
 2004|             nextFn();
 2005|             return;
 2006|           }
 2007|         } catch (_) { /* show the gate */ }
 2008|       }
 2009|       openConsentGate(nextFn);
 2010|     }

 2019|       if (fab) fab.hidden = !logged;
 2020|       if (!logged) closeAcctMenus();
 2021|     }

 2033|         : "Pick a mode. We pick the best protocol path.";

 2040|     async function api(path, opts = {}) {
 2041|       const headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...(opts.headers || {}) };
 2042|       if (session && session.token) headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${session.token}`;
 2043|       let res;
 2044|       try {
 2045|         res = await fetch(`${BACKEND_URL}${path}`, { ...opts, headers });
 2046|       } catch (net) {
 2047|         const err = new Error(friendlyApiError(net, "Cannot reach the gateway."));
 2048|         err.status = 0;
 2049|         throw err;
 2050|       }
 2051|       if (!res.ok) {
 2052|         const text = await res.text();
 2053|         let body = {};
 2054|         let msg = text || `HTTP ${res.status}`;
 2055|         try { body = JSON.parse(text); msg = body.error || msg; } catch (_) {}
 2056|         if (res.status === 401 && session && !/\/api\/(login|signup|session)/.test(path)) {
 2057|           if (isRemoteSession()) {
 2058|             const errRemote = new Error(friendlyApiError({ message: msg }, msg));
 2059|             errRemote.status = res.status;
 2060|             errRemote.body = body;
 2061|             throw errRemote;
 2062|           }
 2063|           session = null;
 2064|           boundDevice = null;
 2065|           myDeviceIp = null;
 2066|           localStorage.removeItem("autovpn_session");
 2067|           stopPoll();
 2068|           showView("view-login");
 2069|           const loginErr = document.getElementById("login-error");
 2070|           if (loginErr) loginErr.textContent = "Session expired. Please log in again.";
 2071|         }
 2072|         const err = new Error(friendlyApiError({ message: msg }, msg));
 2073|         err.status = res.status;
 2074|         err.body = body;
 2075|         throw err;
 2076|       }
 2077|       if (res.status === 204) return {};
 2078|       return res.json();
 2079|     }
 2080| 
 2081|     function qualityFromRow(row) {
 2082|       if (!row) return { label: "Unknown", cls: "badge-fair" };
 2083|       const mos = Number(row.mos) || 0;
 2084|       const loss = Number(row.loss) || 0;
 2085|       const lat = Number(row.latency) || 999;
 2086|       if (loss >= 50 || mos <= 2) return { label: "Poor", cls: "badge-poor" };
 2087|       if (mos >= 4.2 && lat < 80 && loss < 1) return { label: "Excellent", cls: "badge-excellent" };
 2088|       if (mos >= 3.6 && loss < 5) return { label: "Good", cls: "badge-good" };
 2089|       return { label: "Fair", cls: "badge-fair" };
 2090|     }
 2091| 
 2092|     function fitBars(score, maxScore) {
 2093|       const ratio = maxScore > 0 ? score / maxScore : 0;
 2094|       const filled = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(ratio * 5)));
 2095|       let html = '<div class="fit-bars">';
 2096|       for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) html += `<i class="${i <= filled ? "on" : ""}"></i>`;
 2097|       html += "</div>";
 2098|       const words = ["Weak", "Okay", "Good", "Strong", "Best"];
 2099|       html += `<div class="fit-label">${words[filled - 1]} fit</div>`;
 2100|       return html;
 2101|     }

 2105|     }

 2133|     function renderMetrics(list, opts = {}) {
 2134|       setTagline(list.length || 8);
 2135|       const ranked = [...list].sort((a, b) => (b.score || 0) - (a.score || 0));
 2136|       const maxScore = ranked[0] ? ranked[0].score : 1;
 2137|       const qosLabel = opts.qos_label || "";
 2138|       const qosMode = opts.qos_mode || "";
 2139|       const qosEl = document.getElementById("qos-line");
 2140|       if (qosEl) {
 2141|         qosEl.textContent = qosLabel
 2142|           || "Each path is marked live QoS or characteristic baseline.";
 2143|       }
 2144| 
 2145|       let html = "";
 2146|       if (!ranked.length) {
 2147|         html = `<div class="empty-state warn">${opts.empty ||
 2148|           "No scores for this view yet. Wait one SAW cycle - this is not the host ranking."}</div>`;
 2149|       }
 2150|       ranked.forEach((c, idx) => {
 2151|         const q = qualityFromRow(c);
 2152|         const src = qosSource(c);
 2153|         const rank = c.is_active ? "In use" : (idx === 0 ? "Top pick" : `#${idx + 1}`);
 2154|         html += `<div class="pick-row ${c.is_active ? "selected" : ""} ${c.failed || simulatedDown.includes(c.id) ? "failed" : ""}">

 2157|             <div class="pick-sub">${rank} · ${c.failed || simulatedDown.includes(c.id) ? "sim down" : statusLine(c)} · ${q.label}
 2158|               · ${c.latency ?? "-"}ms · ${(c.score || 0).toFixed(2)}</div>
 2159|           </div>
 2160|           <div class="pick-right">
 2161|             <span class="badge ${src === "live" ? "badge-live" : "badge-baseline"}">${src}</span>
 2162|             ${fitBars(c.score || 0, maxScore)}
 2163|           </div>

 2174|       }

 2178|           profile: opts.profile,
 2179|           evidence: opts.evidence,
 2180|           device: opts.device,
 2181|           qos_label: qosLabel,
 2182|           switch_reason: opts.switch_reason || "",
 2183|           saw: opts.saw || {},
 2184|         });
 2185|       }

 2187|       details.classList.toggle("open", showDetails);
 2188|       setDetailsToggleLabel();
 2189| 
 2190|       const named = String(opts.active_name || "").trim();
 2191|       const active = list.find(r => r.is_active)
 2192|         || ranked[0]
 2193|         || (named ? { name: named, is_active: true } : null);
 2194|       const heroVpn = document.getElementById("hero-vpn");
 2195|       const heroQ = document.getElementById("hero-quality");
 2196|       const heroK = document.getElementById("hero-kicker");
 2197|       const heroQos = document.getElementById("hero-qos");
 2198|       const routeDiv = document.getElementById("route-status");
 2199|       if (heroQos) heroQos.textContent = qosLabel || "Live vs baseline pending…";

 2205|         routeDiv.classList.add("paused");

 2255|       }
 2256|     }

 2258|     /* ---- Custom 100-point budget ---- */
 2259|     function readWeights() {
 2260|       return WEIGHT_IDS.map(id => parseInt(document.getElementById(id).value, 10) || 0);
 2261|     }

 2263|     function updateBudgetUI() {
 2264|       const vals = readWeights();
 2265|       const used = vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
 2266|       const fill = document.getElementById("budget-fill");
 2267|       const bar = document.getElementById("budget-bar");
 2268|       const text = document.getElementById("budget-text");
 2269|       fill.style.width = Math.min(100, used) + "%";
 2270|       bar.classList.toggle("over", used > BUDGET_TOTAL);
 2271|       const left = BUDGET_TOTAL - used;
 2272|       text.innerHTML = left >= 0
 2273|         ? `<strong>${used}</strong> / ${BUDGET_TOTAL} points used · <strong>${left}</strong> left`
 2274|         : `<strong>${used}</strong> / ${BUDGET_TOTAL} - over budget by ${-left}`;
 2275|       vals.forEach((v, i) => {
 2276|         document.getElementById(PCT_IDS[i]).textContent = v + "%";
 2277|       });
 2278|     }

 2280|     function clampSlider(changedId) {
 2281|       if (adjustingSlider) return;
 2282|       adjustingSlider = true;
 2283|       const vals = readWeights();
 2284|       const idx = WEIGHT_IDS.indexOf(changedId);
 2285|       const others = vals.reduce((a, b, i) => a + (i === idx ? 0 : b), 0);
 2286|       const maxAllowed = Math.max(0, BUDGET_TOTAL - others);
 2287|       let v = parseInt(document.getElementById(changedId).value, 10) || 0;
 2288|       if (v > maxAllowed) {
 2289|         v = maxAllowed;
 2290|         document.getElementById(changedId).value = String(v);
 2291|       }
 2292|       // Keep each slider's HTML max at remaining+current so UI can't overshoot easily
 2293|       WEIGHT_IDS.forEach((id, i) => {
 2294|         const el = document.getElementById(id);
 2295|         const rest = vals.reduce((a, b, j) => a + (j === i ? 0 : (j === idx ? v : b)), 0);
 2296|         el.max = String(Math.max(0, BUDGET_TOTAL - rest + (i === idx ? 0 : 0)));
 2297|         // simpler: max = remaining for others + this value
 2298|         const othersNow = WEIGHT_IDS.reduce((a, oid, j) => {
 2299|           if (j === i) return a;
 2300|           return a + (parseInt(document.getElementById(oid).value, 10) || 0);
 2301|         }, 0);
 2302|         el.max = String(Math.max(parseInt(el.value, 10) || 0, BUDGET_TOTAL - othersNow));
 2303|       });
 2304|       adjustingSlider = false;
 2305|       updateBudgetUI();
 2306|     }
 2307| 
 2308|     WEIGHT_IDS.forEach(id => {
 2309|       document.getElementById(id).addEventListener("input", () => clampSlider(id));
 2310|     });
 2311|     updateBudgetUI();

 2327|     const SPEED_ARC = 282.743; // π * 90
 2328|     const SPEED_TICKS = [0, 1, 5, 10, 20, 30, 50, 75, 100];
 2329|     let speedSweep = null;
 2330| 
 2331|     function speedPos(mbps, radius) {
 2332|       const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, mbps));
 2333|       const th = Math.PI * (1 - v / 100);
 2334|       return [120 + radius * Math.cos(th), 120 - radius * Math.sin(th)];
 2335|     }
 2336| 
 2337|     function initSpeedGauge() {
 2338|       const g = document.getElementById("speed-ticks");
 2339|       if (!g || g.childElementCount) return;
 2340|       const ns = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
 2341|       SPEED_TICKS.forEach((v) => {
 2342|         const [x1, y1] = speedPos(v, 82);
 2343|         const [x2, y2] = speedPos(v, 90);
 2344|         const tick = document.createElementNS(ns, "line");
 2345|         tick.setAttribute("class", "speed-tick");
 2346|         tick.setAttribute("x1", x1.toFixed(1));
 2347|         tick.setAttribute("y1", y1.toFixed(1));
 2348|         tick.setAttribute("x2", x2.toFixed(1));
 2349|         tick.setAttribute("y2", y2.toFixed(1));
 2350|         g.appendChild(tick);
 2351|         const [lx, ly] = speedPos(v, 102);
 2352|         const lab = document.createElementNS(ns, "text");
 2353|         lab.setAttribute("class", "speed-label");
 2354|         lab.setAttribute("x", lx.toFixed(1));
 2355|         lab.setAttribute("y", (ly + 3).toFixed(1));
 2356|         lab.textContent = String(v);
 2357|         g.appendChild(lab);
 2358|       });
 2359|       setSpeedNeedle(0, false);
 2360|     }
 2361| 
 2362|     function setSpeedNeedle(mbps, animate) {
 2363|       const gauge = document.getElementById("speed-gauge");
 2364|       const needle = document.getElementById("speed-needle");
 2365|       const fill = document.getElementById("speed-fill");
 2366|       const num = document.getElementById("speed-num");
 2367|       if (!needle || !fill || !num) return;
 2368|       const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(mbps) || 0));
 2369|       const ang = -90 + (v / 100) * 180;
 2370|       if (gauge) gauge.classList.toggle("sweeping", !animate);
 2371|       needle.style.transform = "rotate(" + ang + "deg)";
 2372|       fill.style.strokeDashoffset = String(SPEED_ARC * (1 - v / 100));
 2373|       if (mbps == null || !Number.isFinite(Number(mbps))) {
 2374|         num.textContent = "—";
 2375|       } else {
 2376|         const n = Number(mbps);
 2377|         num.textContent = Math.abs(n - Math.round(n)) < 0.05 ? String(Math.round(n)) : n.toFixed(1);
 2378|       }
 2379|     }
 2380| 
 2381|     function startSpeedSweep() {
 2382|       stopSpeedSweep();
 2383|       const t0 = performance.now();
 2384|       const tick = (now) => {
 2385|         const t = (now - t0) / 1000;
 2386|         const mbps = 16 + 28 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 3.1));
 2387|         setSpeedNeedle(mbps, false);
 2388|         speedSweep = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 2389|       };
 2390|       speedSweep = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 2391|     }
 2392| 
 2393|     function stopSpeedSweep() {
 2394|       if (speedSweep) cancelAnimationFrame(speedSweep);
 2395|       speedSweep = null;
 2396|     }

 2400|     document.getElementById("speed-btn").onclick = async () => {
 2401|       const btn = document.getElementById("speed-btn");
 2402|       const out = document.getElementById("speed-result");
 2403|       const err = document.getElementById("speed-error");
 2404|       const vpnEl = document.getElementById("speed-vpn-label");
 2405|       err.textContent = "";
 2406|       if (out) out.textContent = "";
 2407|       const name = lastActiveName || "the current VPN";
 2408|       if (vpnEl) vpnEl.textContent = "Testing " + name + "…";
 2409|       btn.disabled = true;
 2410|       startSpeedSweep();
 2411|       try {
 2412|         const body = {};
 2413|         const ip = targetDeviceIp();
 2414|         if (ip) body.device_ip = ip;
 2415|         const res = await api("/api/speed-test", {
 2416|           method: "POST",
 2417|           body: JSON.stringify(body),
 2418|         });
 2419|         const mbps = Number(res && res.mbps);
 2420|         const vpn = (res && res.name) || name;
 2421|         const iface = (res && res.iface) || "";
 2422|         if (!Number.isFinite(mbps) || mbps <= 0) {
 2423|           throw new Error("Could not measure " + vpn + ".");
 2424|         }
 2425|         stopSpeedSweep();
 2426|         setSpeedNeedle(mbps, true);
 2427|         if (vpnEl) vpnEl.textContent = iface ? (vpn + " · " + iface) : vpn;
 2428|         if (res && res.source === "dummy") {
 2429|           const hint = document.getElementById("speed-hint");
 2430|           if (hint) {
 2431|             hint.textContent = vpn + " is a leftover dummy path — Fast.com never rides it. Figure is for display.";
 2432|           }
 2433|         } else {
 2434|           setSpeedHint(iface ? (vpn + " · " + iface) : vpn);
 2435|         }
 2436|       } catch (e) {
 2437|         stopSpeedSweep();
 2438|         setSpeedNeedle(0, true);
 2439|         document.getElementById("speed-num").textContent = "—";
 2440|         if (vpnEl) vpnEl.textContent = "";
 2441|         err.textContent = friendlyApiError(e, "Speed test failed.");
 2442|       } finally {
 2443|         btn.disabled = false;
 2444|       }
 2445|     };
