 2551|     function deviceIsRemote(d) {
 2552|       if (!d) return false;
 2553|       if (d.kind === "remote") return true;
 2554|       const ip = d.ip || "";
 2555|       if (/^10\.71\.80\./.test(ip) && ip !== "10.71.80.237") return true;
 2556|       return /^10\.61\.136\./.test(ip) && ip !== "10.61.136.10";
 2557|     }
 2558| 
 2559|     function deviceEgressLabel(d) {
 2560|       if (!d) return "via MikroTik";
 2561|       if (d.egress === "tunnel" && (d.egress_dev || d.egress_via)) {
 2562|         return `via ${d.egress_dev || d.egress_via}`;
 2563|       }
 2564|       return "via MikroTik";
 2565|     }
 2566| 
 2567|     function deviceEvidence(d) {
 2568|       return (d && (d.evidence || (d.auto && d.auto.evidence))) || "";
 2569|     }
 2570| 
 2571|     function deviceStatusEmojis(d) {
 2572|       const online = d.online ? "🟢" : "🔴";
 2573|       const profKey = (d.effective_profile || d.profile || "").toLowerCase();
 2574|       const profMap = {
 2575|         auto: "🤖",
 2576|         gaming: "🎮",
 2577|         streaming: "📺",
 2578|         browsing: "🌐",
 2579|         balanced: "⚖️",
 2580|         custom: "🎛️",
 2581|         secure: "🔒",
 2582|       };
 2583|       const profile = profMap[profKey] || "📶";
 2584|       const path = (d.active_name || "").toLowerCase();
 2585|       let vpn = "❔";
 2586|       if (!d.online) vpn = "💤";
 2587|       else if (path.includes("wireguard")) vpn = "🛡️";
 2588|       else if (path.includes("ike") || path.includes("ipsec")) vpn = "🔑";
 2589|       else if (path.includes("openvpn")) vpn = "🔐";
 2590|       else if (path.includes("softether")) vpn = "🧩";
 2591|       else if (path.includes("nebula")) vpn = "🌌";
 2592|       else if (path.includes("sstp")) vpn = "🧱";
 2593|       else if (path.includes("openconnect") || path.includes("anyconnect")) vpn = "🛰️";
 2594|       else if (d.active_name) vpn = "🚀";
 2595|       const ev = deviceEvidence(d);
 2596|       const label = d.online
 2597|         ? `${d.effective_label || d.profile || "-"} · ${d.active_name || "-"} · ${deviceEgressLabel(d)}`
 2598|         : "offline";
 2599|       return { online, profile, vpn, label, evidence: ev };
 2600|     }

 2602|     function deviceDeleteBtn(d) {
 2603|       if (!d || !d.id) return "";
 2604|       return `<button type="button" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm device-del" data-id="${d.id}" data-name="${escapeAttr(d.name || "Device")}">Delete</button>`;
 2605|     }

 2607|     async function removeBoundDevice(deviceId, name, opts) {
 2608|       const ok = await askConfirm(
 2609|         "Remove this device?",
 2610|         (name || "This device") + " will leave this account. Real VPN tunnels stay up.",
 2611|         "Delete"
 2612|       );
 2613|       if (!ok) return;
 2614|       const adminAccountId = opts && opts.adminAccountId;
 2615|       try {
 2616|         if (adminAccountId) {
 2617|           await api(`/api/admin/accounts/${adminAccountId}/devices/${deviceId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
 2618|           await openAdminAccount(adminAccountId);
 2619|           return;
 2620|         }
 2621|         await api(`/api/me/devices/${deviceId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
 2622|         if (boundDevice && Number(boundDevice.id) === Number(deviceId)) {
 2623|           boundDevice = null;
 2624|           myDeviceIp = null;
 2625|           stopPoll();
 2626|           showView("view-devices");
 2627|         }
 2628|         await refreshUserDevices();
 2629|       } catch (e) {
 2630|         alert(friendlyApiError(e, "Could not remove device."));
 2631|       }
 2632|     }
 2633| 
 2634|     function wireDeviceDeleteButtons(root, opts) {
 2635|       if (!root) return;
 2636|       root.querySelectorAll(".device-del").forEach((btn) => {
 2637|         btn.onclick = (e) => {
 2638|           e.preventDefault();
 2639|           e.stopPropagation();
 2640|           removeBoundDevice(
 2641|             Number(btn.getAttribute("data-id")),
 2642|             btn.getAttribute("data-name") || "This device",
 2643|             opts || {}
 2644|           );
 2645|         };
 2646|       });
 2647|     }

 2649|     async function refreshUserDevices() {
 2650|       const box = document.getElementById("user-device-list");
 2651|       const err = document.getElementById("device-list-error");
 2652|       err.textContent = "";
 2653|       document.getElementById("devices-user-label").textContent =
 2654|         session ? `Signed in as ${session.username}` : "Signed in";
 2655|       updateNavUsers();
 2656|       try {
 2657|         const [data, status] = await Promise.all([
 2658|           api("/api/me/devices"),
 2659|           api("/api/status").catch(() => ({})),
 2660|         ]);
 2661|         if (typeof status.gateway_enabled === "boolean") setGatewayUi(status.gateway_enabled);
 2662|         if (status.remote && typeof status.remote.off_lan === "boolean") {
 2663|           setRemoteHint(status.remote.off_lan);
 2664|         }
 2665|         const list = data.devices || [];
 2666|         const focus = list.find(d => d.online) || list[0];
 2667|         if (focus) {
 2668|           setSimpleCopy(
 2669|             simpleSentence(
 2670|               focus.effective_profile || focus.profile,
 2671|               focus.active_name,
 2672|               focus.auto
 2673|             ),
 2674|             simpleStatusText({ gatewayOff: !gatewayEnabled, offline: focus.online === false })
 2675|           );
 2676|         } else {
 2677|           const active = (status.metrics || []).find(m => m.is_active) || (status.metrics || [])[0];
 2678|           setSimpleCopy(
 2679|             simpleSentence(status.profile, (active && active.name) || status.active_path, status.auto),
 2680|             simpleStatusText({ gatewayOff: !gatewayEnabled })
 2681|           );
 2682|         }
 2683|         if (!list.length) {
 2684|           box.innerHTML = isAdvanced()
 2685|             ? `<div class="empty-state">
 2686|             No devices yet. Connect this phone or PC to the lab Wi‑Fi (192.168.50.x)
 2687|             or ZeroTier overlay (<code>10.71.80.x</code>, join <code>cf719fd540b50c25</code>), then tap <strong>+ Add this device</strong>.
 2688|           </div>`
 2689|             : `<div class="empty-state">No devices yet. Connect to the lab Wi‑Fi or ZeroTier, then tap <strong>+ Add this device</strong>.</div>`;
 2690|           return;
 2691|         }
 2692|         box.innerHTML = list.map(d => {
 2693|           const st = deviceStatusEmojis(d);
 2694|           if (!isAdvanced()) {
 2695|             let line = "Offline";
 2696|             if (d.online) {
 2697|               line = gatewayEnabled
 2698|                 ? (`Online · ${d.active_name || "picking a VPN"}`)
 2699|                 : "Online";
 2700|             }
 2701|             return `
 2702|           <div class="device-row ${d.online ? "" : "offline"}" data-id="${d.id}" data-ip="${d.ip || ""}" data-mac="${d.mac || ""}" data-name="${(d.name || "Device").replace(/"/g, "&quot;")}">
 2703|             <div>
 2704|               <div class="name">${d.name || (deviceIsRemote(d) ? "Remote" : "Device")}</div>
 2705|               <div class="meta">${line}</div>
 2706|             </div>
 2707|             <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
 2708|               ${deviceDeleteBtn(d)}
 2709|               <div class="meta">Open →</div>
 2710|             </div>
 2711|           </div>`;
 2712|           }
 2713|           return `
 2714|           <div class="device-row ${d.online ? "" : "offline"}" data-id="${d.id}" data-ip="${d.ip || ""}" data-mac="${d.mac || ""}" data-name="${(d.name || "Device").replace(/"/g, "&quot;")}">
 2715|             <div>
 2716|               <div class="name">${d.name || "Device"}</div>
 2717|               <div class="meta">${d.ip || "no IP"} ${d.mac ? "· " + d.mac : ""} · ${st.label}${st.evidence ? " · " + st.evidence : ""}</div>
 2718|             </div>
 2719|             <div style="text-align:right;line-height:1.35">
 2720|               <div style="font-size:22px;letter-spacing:2px" title="${st.label}">${st.online} ${st.profile} ${st.vpn}</div>
 2721|               <div style="margin-top:6px;display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;align-items:center;gap:10px">
 2722|                 ${deviceDeleteBtn(d)}
 2723|                 <div class="meta">Open →</div>
 2724|               </div>
 2725|             </div>
 2726|           </div>`;
 2727|         }).join("");
 2728|         box.querySelectorAll(".device-row").forEach(row => {
 2729|           row.onclick = () => openBoundDevice({
 2730|             id: Number(row.getAttribute("data-id")),
 2731|             ip: row.getAttribute("data-ip") || "",
 2732|             mac: row.getAttribute("data-mac") || "",
 2733|             name: row.getAttribute("data-name") || "Device",
 2734|           });
 2735|         });
 2736|         wireDeviceDeleteButtons(box);
 2737|       } catch (e) {
 2738|         err.textContent = friendlyApiError(e, "Could not load devices.");
 2739|       }
 2740|     }

 2742|     function openBoundDevice(dev) {

 2745|         ip: dev.ip || "",
 2746|         mac: dev.mac || "",
 2747|         name: dev.name || "Device",
 2748|       };
 2749|       myDeviceIp = boundDevice.ip || null;

 2751|       showViewBanner("", "");

 2757|         ip: boundDevice.ip,
 2758|         effective_label: "…",
 2759|         active_name: "…",
 2760|         online: dev.online,
 2761|       }, { controlling: true });
 2762|       stopPoll();
 2763|       updateStatus();
 2764|       pollTimer = setInterval(updateStatus, 3000);
 2765|     }

 2821|     function enterRemoteAdmin(sess) {
 2822|       session = Object.assign({}, sess || {}, { role: "admin", remote: true });
 2823|       if (!hasAccountConsent(session)) {
 2824|         requireConsentThen(() => enterRemoteAdmin(session));
 2825|         return;
 2826|       }
 2827|       localStorage.setItem("autovpn_session", JSON.stringify(session));
 2828|       localStorage.setItem("autovpn_onboarded", "1");
 2829|       applyRemoteUi(true, session.banner || REMOTE_BANNER_TEXT);
 2830|       applyMode();
 2831|       updateNavUsers();
 2832|       stopPoll();
 2833|       boundDevice = null;
 2834|       myDeviceIp = null;
 2835|       if (isAdvanced()) {
 2836|         showView("view-admin");
 2837|         refreshAdmin();
 2838|         pollTimer = setInterval(refreshAdmin, 4000);
 2839|       } else {
 2840|         showView("view-devices");
 2841|         refreshUserDevices();
 2842|         pollTimer = setInterval(refreshUserDevices, 4000);
 2843|       }
 2844|     }
 2845| 
 2846|     function enterApp(sess) {
 2847|       if (sess) session = sess;
 2848|       if (!hasAccountConsent(session)) {
 2849|         requireConsentThen(() => enterApp(session));
 2850|         return;
 2851|       }
 2852|       stopPoll();
 2853|       boundDevice = null;
 2854|       updateNavUsers();
 2855|       applyMode();
 2856|       if (sess && (sess.remote || sess.skip_login)) {
 2857|         enterRemoteAdmin(sess);
 2858|         return;
 2859|       }
 2860|       applyRemoteUi(false);
 2861|       if (sess.role === "admin" && isAdvanced()) {
 2862|         showView("view-admin");
 2863|         refreshAdmin();
 2864|         pollTimer = setInterval(refreshAdmin, 4000);
 2865|       } else {
 2866|         showView("view-devices");
 2867|         refreshUserDevices();
 2868|         pollTimer = setInterval(refreshUserDevices, 4000);
 2869|       }
 2870|     }
 2871| 
 2872|     function stopPoll() {
 2873|       if (pollTimer) clearInterval(pollTimer);
 2874|       pollTimer = null;
 2875|     }

 2877|     async function logout() {
 2878|       closeModal("consent-modal");
 2879|       afterConsent = null;
 2880|       if (isRemoteSession()) {
 2881|         enterRemoteAdmin(session || { role: "admin", remote: true });
 2882|         return;
 2883|       }
 2884|       stopPoll();
 2885|       try { await api("/api/logout", { method: "POST", body: "{}" }); } catch (_) {}
 2886|       session = null;
 2887|       boundDevice = null;
 2888|       myDeviceIp = null;
 2889|       localStorage.removeItem("autovpn_session");
 2890|       applyRemoteUi(false);
 2891|       showView("view-login");
 2892|     }
 2893|     document.getElementById("logout-user").onclick = logout;
 2894|     document.getElementById("logout-admin").onclick = logout;
 2895|     document.getElementById("logout-devices").onclick = logout;

 2897|     document.getElementById("profile-selector").onchange = async (e) => {
 2898|       const profile = e.target.value;
 2899|       document.getElementById("custom-panel").style.display = profile === "custom" ? "block" : "none";
 2900|       if (profile === "custom") {
 2901|         setProfileMeta(FRIENDLY_PROFILE.custom, "custom");
 2902|         updateBudgetUI();
 2903|         // Must persist - otherwise the 3s status poll snaps the selector back to Auto
 2904|         try {
 2905|           const res = await api("/api/set-profile", {
 2906|             method: "POST",
 2907|             body: JSON.stringify({ profile: "custom", device_ip: targetDeviceIp() }),
 2908|           });
 2909|           if (res.device_ip && !boundDevice) myDeviceIp = res.device_ip;
 2910|           if (res.device) updateDeviceChip({ ...res.device, name: (boundDevice && boundDevice.name) || res.device.name }, { controlling: !!boundDevice });
 2911|         } catch (_) { /* keep panel open; apply still works when gateway is up */ }
 2912|         return;
 2913|       }
 2914|       setLoading(true, profile === "auto" ? "Starting Auto mode…" : "Switching mode…");
 2915|       try {
 2916|         const res = await api("/api/set-profile", {
 2917|           method: "POST",
 2918|           body: JSON.stringify({ profile, device_ip: targetDeviceIp() }),
 2919|         });
 2920|         if (res.device_ip && !boundDevice) myDeviceIp = res.device_ip;
 2921|         if (res.device) updateDeviceChip({ ...res.device, name: (boundDevice && boundDevice.name) || res.device.name }, { controlling: !!boundDevice });
 2922|         setProfileMeta(res.profile_info || FRIENDLY_PROFILE[profile], profile, res.auto);
 2923|         if (res.auto && res.auto.detected) lastAutoDetected = res.auto.detected;
 2924|         if (res.metrics) renderMetrics(res.metrics, {
 2925|           switched: !!res.switched,
 2926|           profile: res.effective_profile || profile,
 2927|           evidence: (res.auto && res.auto.evidence) || "",
 2928|           device: res.device_ip || targetDeviceIp() || "",
 2929|           qos_mode: res.qos_mode,
 2930|           qos_label: res.qos_label,
 2931|           controlling: !!boundDevice,
 2932|         });
 2933|         else await updateStatus();
 2934|       } catch (e) {
 2935|         document.getElementById("hero-vpn").textContent = "Unavailable";
 2936|         document.getElementById("hero-quality").innerHTML =
 2937|           `<span class="badge badge-poor">Could not switch</span>`;
 2938|         showViewBanner(friendlyApiError(e, "Could not switch mode."), "bad");
 2939|       } finally {
 2940|         setLoading(false);
 2941|       }
 2942|     };
 2943| 
 2944|     document.getElementById("apply-weights").onclick = async () => {
 2945|       const raw = readWeights();
 2946|       const total = raw.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
 2947|       if (!total) return alert("Give at least one priority a few points.");
 2948|       if (total > BUDGET_TOTAL) return alert("You're over the 100-point budget. Lower some sliders first.");
 2949|       const weights = raw.map(v => v / BUDGET_TOTAL);
 2950|       setLoading(true, "Applying priorities…");
 2951|       try {
 2952|         const res = await api("/api/set-weights", {
 2953|           method: "POST",
 2954|           body: JSON.stringify({ weights, device_ip: targetDeviceIp() }),
 2955|         });
 2956|         const sel = document.getElementById("profile-selector");
 2957|         if (sel) sel.value = "custom";
 2958|         document.getElementById("custom-panel").style.display = "block";
 2959|         setProfileMeta(FRIENDLY_PROFILE.custom, "custom");
 2960|         if (res.device) updateDeviceChip({ ...res.device, name: (boundDevice && boundDevice.name) || res.device.name }, { controlling: !!boundDevice });
 2961|         if (res.metrics) renderMetrics(res.metrics, {
 2962|           switched: !!res.switched,
 2963|           profile: "custom",
 2964|           device: res.device_ip || targetDeviceIp() || "",
 2965|           qos_mode: res.qos_mode,
 2966|           qos_label: res.qos_label,
 2967|           controlling: !!boundDevice,
 2968|         });
 2969|       } catch (e) {
 2970|         alert(friendlyApiError(e, "Priorities not applied."));
 2971|       } finally { setLoading(false); }
 2972|     };

 2974|     async function changeGateway(enabled, opts) {
 2975|       if (syncingGatewayToggle) return;
 2976|       if (isRemoteSession()) {
 2977|         setSimpleGwError("Gateway is lab Wi‑Fi only.");
 2978|         return;
 2979|       }
 2980|       const quiet = !isAdvanced();
 2981|       if (!quiet) setLoading(true, enabled ? "Turning gateway on…" : "Turning gateway off…");
 2982|       try {
 2983|         const res = await api("/api/gateway", {
 2984|           method: "POST",
 2985|           body: JSON.stringify({ enabled }),
 2986|         });
 2987|         lastGwError = "";
 2988|         setSimpleGwError("");
 2989|         setGatewayUi(!!res.gateway_enabled);
 2990|         showViewBanner("", "");
 2991|         if (opts && opts.admin) refreshAdmin();
 2992|         else if (res.metrics) renderMetrics(res.metrics, { switched: !!res.switched, qos_mode: res.qos_mode, qos_label: res.qos_label });
 2993|         else await updateStatus();
 2994|       } catch (e) {
 2995|         const stillOff = e.status === 409 || (e.body && e.body.gateway_enabled === false);
 2996|         setGatewayUi(stillOff ? false : !enabled);
 2997|         lastGwError = friendlyApiError(e, "Could not change gateway.");
 2998|         setSimpleGwError(lastGwError);
 2999|         if (!quiet) {
 3000|           showViewBanner(lastGwError, "bad");
 3001|           alert(lastGwError);
 3002|         }
 3003|       } finally {
 3004|         if (!quiet) setLoading(false);
 3005|       }
 3006|     }
 3007| 
 3008|     document.getElementById("gateway-toggle").onchange = (e) => {
 3009|       changeGateway(!!e.target.checked, {});
 3010|     };
 3011|     document.getElementById("gateway-toggle-admin").onchange = (e) => {
 3012|       changeGateway(!!e.target.checked, { admin: true });
 3013|     };
 3014|     document.getElementById("gateway-toggle-simple").onchange = (e) => {
 3015|       changeGateway(!!e.target.checked, {});
 3016|     };
 3017|     document.getElementById("gateway-toggle-simple-user").onchange = (e) => {
 3018|       changeGateway(!!e.target.checked, {});

 3021|     async function updateStatus() {
 3022|       try {
 3023|         const data = await api(statusUrl());
 3024|         const isDeviceView = !!(boundDevice || data.view === "device");
 3025|         // Never paint host rankings while controlling a device.
 3026|         const list = isDeviceView
 3027|           ? ((data.device && data.device.metrics) || data.metrics || [])
 3028|           : (Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.metrics || []));
 3029|         if (typeof data.gateway_enabled === "boolean") setGatewayUi(data.gateway_enabled);
 3030|         if (data.remote && typeof data.remote.off_lan === "boolean") {
 3031|           setRemoteHint(data.remote.off_lan);
 3032|         }
 3033| 
 3034|         const live = (isDeviceView && data.device) ? data.device : (data.device || null);
 3035|         if (boundDevice) {
 3036|           if (data.device_ip) boundDevice.ip = data.device_ip;
 3037|           myDeviceIp = boundDevice.ip || myDeviceIp;
 3038|           updateDeviceChip({
 3039|             name: boundDevice.name || (live && live.name),
 3040|             ip: boundDevice.ip || (live && live.ip),
 3041|             online: live ? live.online : false,
 3042|             effective_label: (live && (live.effective_label || live.profile)) || "-",
 3043|             profile: live && live.profile,
 3044|             active_name: (live && live.active_name) || data.active_path,
 3045|             egress: live && live.egress,
 3046|             egress_dev: live && live.egress_dev,
 3047|             egress_via: live && live.egress_via,
 3048|           }, { controlling: true });
 3049|         } else if (live) {
 3050|           myDeviceIp = live.ip;
 3051|           updateDeviceChip(live, { controlling: data.view === "device" });
 3052|         } else {
 3053|           updateDeviceChip(null);
 3054|         }
 3055| 
 3056|         const offline = !!(isDeviceView && live && live.online === false);
 3057|         const vpnName = (live && live.active_name) || data.active_path
 3058|           || ((list.find(r => r.is_active) || list[0] || {}).name) || "";
 3059|         setSimpleCopy(
 3060|           simpleSentence(data.profile || (live && live.profile), vpnName, data.auto || (live && live.auto)),
 3061|           simpleStatusText({ gatewayOff: !gatewayEnabled, offline })
 3062|         );
 3063|         if (lastGwError) {
 3064|           if (isAdvanced()) showViewBanner(lastGwError, "bad");
 3065|           else {
 3066|             showViewBanner("", "");
 3067|             setSimpleGwError(lastGwError);
 3068|           }
 3069|         } else if (offline) {
 3070|           if (isAdvanced()) {
 3071|             showViewBanner(
 3072|               `${boundDevice && boundDevice.name ? boundDevice.name : "This device"} is offline. `
 3073|               + "Scores below are for its saved profile (live tunnel QoS or characteristic fallback) - not the host or the browser you are using.",
 3074|               "warn"
 3075|             );
 3076|           } else {
 3077|             showViewBanner("", "");
 3078|           }
 3079|         } else if (isDeviceView && !list.length) {
 3080|           if (isAdvanced()) {
 3081|             showViewBanner(
 3082|               "Waiting for this device’s first SAW cycle. Host numbers are not shown here.",
 3083|               "info"
 3084|             );
 3085|           } else {
 3086|             showViewBanner("", "");
 3087|           }
 3088|         } else {
 3089|           showViewBanner("", "");
 3090|         }
 3091| 
 3092|         const profile = (data && data.profile) || "balanced";
 3093|         setProfileMeta(
 3094|           (data && data.profile_info) || FRIENDLY_PROFILE[profile],
 3095|           profile,
 3096|           data && data.auto
 3097|         );
 3098|         const sel = document.getElementById("profile-selector");
 3099|         const loading = document.getElementById("loading-overlay").classList.contains("show");
 3100|         if (sel && sel.value !== profile && !loading) sel.value = profile;
 3101|         document.getElementById("custom-panel").style.display =
 3102|           profile === "custom" ? "block" : "none";
 3103| 
 3104|         let autoSwitched = false;
 3105|         if (profile === "auto" && data.auto && data.auto.detected) {
 3106|           if (lastAutoDetected && lastAutoDetected !== data.auto.detected) {
 3107|             autoSwitched = true;
 3108|             setLoading(true, `Auto → ${data.auto.label || data.auto.detected}…`);
 3109|             setTimeout(() => setLoading(false), 700);
 3110|           }
 3111|           lastAutoDetected = data.auto.detected;
 3112|         }
 3113| 
 3114|         const who = isDeviceView
 3115|           ? ((live && live.ip) || (boundDevice && boundDevice.ip) || data.device_ip || "")
 3116|           : "";
 3117|         const switchReason = (isDeviceView && live && live.switch_reason)
 3118|           || data.switch_reason
 3119|           || "";
 3120|         renderMetrics(list, {
 3121|           switched: autoSwitched,
 3122|           profile: (data.auto && data.auto.active)
 3123|             ? `auto→${data.auto.detected || data.effective_profile || profile}`
 3124|             : (data.effective_profile || profile),
 3125|           evidence: (data.auto && data.auto.evidence) || (live && live.evidence) || "",
 3126|           device: who,
 3127|           qos_mode: data.qos_mode,
 3128|           qos_label: data.qos_label,
 3129|           switch_reason: switchReason,
 3130|           controlling: isDeviceView,
 3131|           offline,
 3132|           egress: live && live.egress,
 3133|           egress_dev: live && live.egress_dev,
 3134|           empty: isDeviceView
 3135|             ? "No scores for this device yet. This is not the host ranking."
 3136|             : "Waiting for the host SAW cycle…",
 3137|           saw: data.saw || {},
 3138|           active_name: (live && live.active_name) || data.active_path || vpnName || "",
 3139|         });
 3140|         const liveLines = Array.isArray(data.events) ? data.events.slice() : [];
 3141|         if (switchReason && !liveLines.some((ln) => String(ln).includes(switchReason))) {
 3142|           liveLines.unshift(switchReason);
 3143|         }
 3144|         renderLiveLog(liveLines);
 3145|         applySimulatedState(data);
 3146|       } catch (e) {
 3147|         const msg = friendlyApiError(e, "Cannot reach the gateway.");
 3148|         document.getElementById("hero-vpn").textContent = "Offline";
 3149|         document.getElementById("hero-quality").innerHTML =
 3150|           `<span class="badge badge-poor">Cannot reach API</span>`;
 3151|         setSimpleCopy("Can't reach the gateway", "Offline");
 3152|         if (isAdvanced()) showViewBanner(msg, "bad");
 3153|         else {
 3154|           showViewBanner("", "");
 3155|           setSimpleGwError(msg);
 3156|         }
 3157|       }
 3158|     }

 3160|     async function renderAdminAccounts(query = "") {
 3161|       const box = document.getElementById("admin-account-list");
 3162|       if (!box) return;
 3163|       try {
 3164|         const q = encodeURIComponent(query || "");
 3165|         const data = await api(`/api/admin/accounts?q=${q}`);
 3166|         const list = data.accounts || [];
 3167|         if (!list.length) {
 3168|           box.innerHTML = "<div style='color:#888;font-size:13px'>No accounts match.</div>";
 3169|           return;
 3170|         }
 3171|         const adminCount = list.filter((a) => a.role === "admin").length;
 3172|         const meId = session && session.user_id;
 3173|         box.innerHTML = list.map(a => {
 3174|           const lastAdmin = a.role === "admin" && adminCount <= 1;
 3175|           const self = meId != null && Number(a.id) === Number(meId);
 3176|           const delLabel = lastAdmin ? "Last admin" : (self ? "You" : "Delete");
 3177|           const delDisabled = lastAdmin ? "disabled" : "";
 3178|           return `
 3179|           <div class="device-row" data-id="${a.id}">
 3180|             <div>
 3181|               <div class="name">${escapeLog(a.username)} ${a.role === "admin" ? "· admin" : ""}</div>
 3182|               <div class="meta">${a.device_count} device${a.device_count === 1 ? "" : "s"}</div>
 3183|             </div>
 3184|             <div class="acct-row-actions">
 3185|               <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm admin-del-user" data-id="${a.id}" data-name="${escapeAttr(a.username)}" ${delDisabled}>${delLabel}</button>
 3186|               <div style="color:var(--accent);font-weight:600">Open →</div>
 3187|             </div>
 3188|           </div>`;
 3189|         }).join("");
 3190|         box.querySelectorAll(".device-row").forEach(row => {
 3191|           row.onclick = () => openAdminAccount(Number(row.getAttribute("data-id")));
 3192|         });
 3193|         box.querySelectorAll(".admin-del-user").forEach((btn) => {
 3194|           btn.onclick = (ev) => {
 3195|             ev.stopPropagation();
 3196|             if (btn.disabled) return;
 3197|             const id = Number(btn.getAttribute("data-id"));
 3198|             const name = btn.getAttribute("data-name") || "this account";
 3199|             const self = meId != null && id === Number(meId);
 3200|             if (self) {
 3201|               confirmDeleteOwnAccount();
 3202|               return;
 3203|             }
 3204|             confirmDeleteOtherUser(id, name);
 3205|           };
 3206|         });
 3207|       } catch (e) {
 3208|         box.innerHTML = `<div style="color:#f66;font-size:13px">${e.message}</div>`;
 3209|       }
 3210|     }

 3212|     function setAccountsCrumb(name) {
 3213|       const root = document.getElementById("admin-acct-crumb-root");
 3214|       const sep = document.getElementById("admin-acct-crumb-sep");
 3215|       const nm = document.getElementById("admin-acct-crumb-name");
 3216|       const listWrap = document.getElementById("admin-accounts-list-wrap");
 3217|       const panel = document.getElementById("admin-account-devices");
 3218|       const drilled = !!name;
 3219|       if (root) {
 3220|         if (drilled) root.removeAttribute("aria-current");
 3221|         else root.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
 3222|       }
 3223|       if (sep) sep.hidden = !drilled;
 3224|       if (nm) {
 3225|         nm.hidden = !drilled;
 3226|         nm.textContent = name || "";
 3227|       }
 3228|       if (listWrap) listWrap.hidden = drilled;
 3229|       if (panel) panel.hidden = !drilled;
 3230|     }

 3237|     async function openAdminAccount(accountId) {
 3238|       selectedAccountId = accountId;
 3239|       try {
 3240|         const data = await api(`/api/admin/accounts/${accountId}/devices`);
 3241|         const uname = (data.account && data.account.username) || "Account";
 3242|         setAccountsCrumb(uname);
 3243|         const box = document.getElementById("admin-acct-device-list");
 3244|         const list = data.devices || [];
 3245|         if (!list.length) {
 3246|           box.innerHTML = "<div style='color:#888;font-size:13px'>This account has no bound devices yet.</div>";
 3247|           return;
 3248|         }
 3249|         box.innerHTML = list.map(d => {
 3250|           const st = deviceStatusEmojis(d);
 3251|           return `
 3252|           <div class="device-row ${d.online ? "" : "offline"}" data-id="${d.id || ""}" data-ip="${d.ip || ""}">
 3253|             <div>
 3254|               <div class="name">${st.online} ${d.name || "Device"}</div>
 3255|               <div class="meta">${d.ip || "-"} ${d.mac ? "· " + d.mac : ""} · ${st.label}${st.evidence ? " · " + st.evidence : ""}</div>
 3256|             </div>
 3257|             <div style="text-align:right">
 3258|               <div style="font-size:20px;letter-spacing:2px">${st.profile} ${st.vpn}</div>
 3259|               <div class="meta">${d.active_name || "-"}</div>
 3260|               <div style="margin-top:8px">${deviceDeleteBtn(d)}</div>
 3261|             </div>
 3262|           </div>`;
 3263|         }).join("");
 3264|         box.querySelectorAll(".device-row").forEach(row => {
 3265|           const ip = row.getAttribute("data-ip");
 3266|           if (ip) row.onclick = () => openAdminDevice(ip);
 3267|         });
 3268|         wireDeviceDeleteButtons(box, { adminAccountId: accountId });
 3269|       } catch (e) {
 3270|         closeAdminAccount();
 3271|         alert("Could not load account devices: " + e.message);
 3272|       }
 3273|     }

 3285|     async function refreshAdmin() {
 3286|       try {
 3287|         const [status, health, logs] = await Promise.all([
 3288|           api("/api/status"),
 3289|           api("/api/admin/health"),
 3290|           api("/api/admin/logs")
 3291|         ]);
 3292|         const list = Array.isArray(status) ? status : (status.metrics || []);
 3293|         setTagline(list.length || 5);
 3294| 
 3295|         const force = document.getElementById("force-regime");
 3296|         const prev = force.value;
 3297|         force.innerHTML = list.map(r =>
 3298|           `<option value="${r.id || r.name}">${r.name}</option>`).join("");
 3299|         if (prev) force.value = prev;
 3300| 
 3301|         document.getElementById("admin-table").innerHTML = list.map(r =>
 3302|           `<tr>
 3303|             <td>${r.name}</td>
 3304|             <td>${r.iface || "-"}</td>
 3305|             <td>${(r.score || 0).toFixed(3)}</td>
 3306|             <td>${r.source || (r.live ? "live" : "baseline")}</td>
 3307|             <td>${r.latency} ms</td>
 3308|             <td class="${r.is_active ? "ok" : ""}">${r.is_active ? "YES" : ""}</td>
 3309|           </tr>`).join("");
 3310| 
 3311|         const lab = health.lab || {};
 3312|         const flag = (yes, label, detail) =>
 3313|           `<div class="regime-row"><span>${label}</span><span class="${yes ? "ok" : "bad"}">${yes ? "UP" : "DOWN"}${detail ? " · " + detail : ""}</span></div>`;
 3314|         const ifaces = lab.ifaces || {};
 3315|         const ifaceRow = (key) => {
 3316|           const i = ifaces[key] || {};
 3317|           return flag(!!i.up, key, i.ipv4 || (i.present ? (i.state || "") : "missing"));
 3318|         };
 3319|         const wgSimOk = health.wireguard_healthy !== false;
 3320|         document.getElementById("admin-lab").innerHTML = [
 3321|           flag(!!lab.forwarding_ready, "Forwarding ready", lab.dhcp_gateway ? ("DHCP " + lab.dhcp_gateway) : ""),
 3322|           flag(!!lab.mikrotik_reachable, "MikroTik", lab.mikrotik_host || "192.168.50.1"),
 3323|           flag(!!lab.forwarding, "ip_forward"),
 3324|           flag(wgSimOk, "WireGuard (SAW)", wgSimOk ? "healthy" : "simulated down"),
 3325|           ifaceRow("enp0s8"),
 3326|           ifaceRow("enp0s9"),
 3327|           ifaceRow("wg0"),
 3328|           ifaceRow("sstp0"),
 3329|           ifaceRow("tun1"),
 3330|           ifaceRow("nebula1"),
 3331|           (lab.notes || []).map(n => `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#999;margin-top:4px">${n}</div>`).join(""),
 3332|         ].join("");
 3333|         setWgFailLabel(wgSimOk);
 3334|         const adminProf = document.getElementById("admin-profile");
 3335|         if (adminProf && status.profile && adminProf !== document.activeElement) {
 3336|           adminProf.value = status.profile;
 3337|         }
 3338| 
 3339|         document.getElementById("admin-containers").innerHTML =
 3340|           (health.containers || []).map(c => {
 3341|             const mixed = !!c.iface_up !== !!c.running;
 3342|             const cls = (c.iface_up && c.running) ? "ok" : (mixed ? "warn" : "bad");
 3343|             const kind = c.dummy ? "dummy" : (c.vpn_carries_internet ? "WAN" : "no-WAN");
 3344|             const wan = c.vpn_carries_internet ? " · 8.8.8.8 OK" : " · 8.8.8.8 FAIL";
 3345|             return `<div class="regime-row">
 3346|               <span>${c.name}${kind ? ` <span style="color:#888;font-size:12px">${kind}</span>` : ""}</span>
 3347|               <span class="${cls}">iface ${c.iface_up ? "UP" : "DOWN"} · proc ${c.running ? "UP" : "DOWN"}${c.iface_up ? wan : ""}</span>
 3348|             </div>`;
 3349|           }).join("") || "<div>No path data</div>";
 3350| 
 3351|         document.getElementById("admin-log").textContent =
 3352|           (logs.lines || []).join("\n") || "No handovers yet.";
 3353|         const deviceList = (health.devices && health.devices.length)
 3354|           ? health.devices
 3355|           : ((status.devices) || []);
 3356|         renderAdminDevices(deviceList);
 3357|         await renderAdminAccounts(document.getElementById("admin-account-search")?.value || "");
 3358|         await renderAdminBugs();
 3359|         if (selectedAccountId) openAdminAccount(selectedAccountId);
 3360|         if (selectedAdminDevice) openAdminDevice(selectedAdminDevice);
 3361|         if (typeof status.gateway_enabled === "boolean") setGatewayUi(status.gateway_enabled);
 3362|         else if (typeof health.gateway_enabled === "boolean") setGatewayUi(health.gateway_enabled);
 3363|         if (status.remote && typeof status.remote.off_lan === "boolean") {
 3364|           setRemoteHint(status.remote.off_lan);
 3365|         }
 3366|         applySimulatedState({
 3367|           simulated_down: health.simulated_down || status.simulated_down,
 3368|           fail_paths: health.fail_paths || status.fail_paths,
 3369|           wireguard_healthy: health.wireguard_healthy,
 3370|         });
 3371|       } catch (e) {
 3372|         document.getElementById("admin-log").textContent = "Admin API offline: " + e.message;
 3373|       }
 3374|     }

 3385|     document.getElementById("admin-fail").onclick = async () => {

scripts\autovpn-port80-redirect.py

iptables REDIRECT 80 to 5006 so http://autovpn.lab/ stays the name.  Â·  39 lines

    1| #!/usr/bin/env python3
    2| """Keep http://autovpn.lab/ on port 80 serving Flask (:5006).
    3| 
    4| LAN browsers type autovpn.lab (no port). A 302 to the IP would rewrite the
    5| address bar. REDIRECT leaves the name in the URL.
    6| """
    7| from __future__ import annotations
    8| 
    9| import subprocess
   10| import time
   11| 
   12| _RULES = (
   13|     ("192.168.50.10", "5006"),
   14|     ("192.168.56.10", "5006"),
   15| )
   16| 
   17| 
   18| def _run(args: list[str]) -> int:
   19|     return subprocess.run(args, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).returncode
   20| 
   21| 
   22| def _ensure() -> None:
   23|     for dest, port in _RULES:
   24|         spec = [
   25|             "PREROUTING", "-p", "tcp", "-d", dest,
   26|             "--dport", "80", "-j", "REDIRECT", "--to-ports", port,
   27|         ]
   28|         if _run(["iptables", "-t", "nat", "-C", *spec]) != 0:
   29|             _run(["iptables", "-t", "nat", "-I", *spec[:1], "1", *spec[1:]])
   30| 
   31| 
   32| if __name__ == "__main__":
   33|     while True:
   34|         try:
   35|             _ensure()
   36|         except Exception:
   37|             pass
   38|         time.sleep(15)
   39|

scripts\autovpn-port80.service

systemd unit for the port-80 redirect loop.  Â·  14 lines

    1| [Unit]
    2| Description=AutoVPN port-80 name stays autovpn.lab (REDIRECT to :5006)
    3| After=network-online.target
    4| Wants=network-online.target
    5| 
    6| [Service]
    7| Type=simple
    8| ExecStart=/usr/bin/python3 /home/user/adaptive-gateway/scripts/autovpn-port80-redirect.py
    9| Restart=always
   10| RestartSec=2
   11| 
   12| [Install]
   13| WantedBy=multi-user.target
   14|

scripts\defense-watchdog.sh

Host default stays MikroTik; client tables pinned if a VPN is unsafe.  Â·  306 lines

    1| #!/bin/bash
    2| # Defense watchdog (~10s via systemd timer).
    3| # - Ubuntu default stays via MikroTik .1 (never a VPN / VBox NAT steal).
    4| # - Device tables 110+ (LAN clients): if default is a VPN that is down,
    5| #   fails ping -I 8.8.8.8 with a FAIL LAN-proof stamp, or is overlay-only
    6| #   (nebula1) → pin that table to default via 192.168.50.1.
    7| #   wg0 / vti0 / tap_soft are real client WAN when LAN-proof PASSes.
    8| # - Never `ip route del default dev wg0` without `table main` — that also
    9| #   deleted client table 110+ `default dev wg0`.
   10| # - DHCP .10 → .1 ONLY after 3 consecutive ticks (~30s) of
   11| #   `ping -I enp0s8 8.8.8.8` failing (3 retries/tick) AND forwarding
   12| #   not ready. Client-table / VPN ping-I flaps do not revert DHCP.
   13| # - Crash-log only for real events: stolen 10.0.2.2 / stolen default,
   14| #   Ubuntu enp0s8 WAN dead (consecutive), DHCP actually changed.
   15| #   Do not crash-log default-route ping blinks or table-110 ping-I.
   16| set -uo pipefail
   17| 
   18| WAN_IF="enp0s8"
   19| WAN_GW="192.168.50.1"
   20| VBOX_GW="10.0.2.2"
   21| VBOX_IF="enp0s3"
   22| MT_HOST="${AUTOVPN_MIKROTIK_HOST:-192.168.50.1}"
   23| MT_USER="${AUTOVPN_MIKROTIK_USER:-admin}"
   24| MT_PASS="${AUTOVPN_MIKROTIK_PASS:-capstone}"
   25| FAIL_FILE="/run/autovpn-watchdog.fail"
   26| FAIL_LIMIT=3
   27| CRASH_COOLDOWN="${AUTOVPN_CRASH_LOG_COOLDOWN:-120}"
   28| CRASH_STAMP="/run/autovpn-crash-log.ts"
   29| CRASH_LOG=""
   30| if [[ -f /usr/local/sbin/crash-log.sh ]]; then
   31|   CRASH_LOG="/usr/local/sbin/crash-log.sh"
   32| elif [[ -f /home/user/adaptive-gateway/scripts/crash-log.sh ]]; then
   33|   CRASH_LOG="/home/user/adaptive-gateway/scripts/crash-log.sh"
   34| fi
   35| 
   36| log() { logger -t autovpn-watchdog -- "$*"; printf '%s\n' "$*"; }
   37| 
   38| maybe_crash_log() {
   39|   local reason="${1:-unknown}"
   40|   local force="${2:-0}"
   41|   [[ -n "$CRASH_LOG" ]] || return 0
   42|   local now last=0
   43|   now="$(date +%s)"
   44|   if [[ -f "$CRASH_STAMP" ]]; then
   45|     last="$(cat "$CRASH_STAMP" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
   46|   fi
   47|   last="${last:-0}"
   48|   [[ "$last" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || last=0
   49|   if [[ "$force" != "1" ]] && (( now - last < CRASH_COOLDOWN )); then
   50|     log "crash-log skipped (cooldown ${CRASH_COOLDOWN}s) reason=${reason}"
   51|     return 0
   52|   fi
   53|   printf '%s\n' "$now" >"$CRASH_STAMP"
   54|   log "crash-log reason=${reason}"
   55|   export AUTOVPN_CRASH_REASON="$reason"
   56|   if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then
   57|     timeout 15 /bin/bash "$CRASH_LOG" || true
   58|   else
   59|     /bin/bash "$CRASH_LOG" || true
   60|   fi
   61| }
   62| 
   63| protect_default() {
   64|   ip route del 192.168.50.1 via "$VBOX_GW" dev "$VBOX_IF" 2>/dev/null || true
   65|   ip route del 8.8.8.8 via "$VBOX_GW" dev "$VBOX_IF" 2>/dev/null || true
   66|   ip route del 1.1.1.1 via "$VBOX_GW" dev "$VBOX_IF" 2>/dev/null || true
   67|   ip route del default via 192.168.100.1 dev "$WAN_IF" table main 2>/dev/null || true
   68|   # Only drop VPN defaults that are actually on main. Bare `del default
   69|   # dev wg0` also removed client table 110+ `default dev wg0`.
   70|   while read -r ln; do
   71|     [[ -n "$ln" ]] || continue
   72|     dev="$(echo "$ln" | awk '{for (i=1;i<=NF;i++) if ($i=="dev") { print $(i+1); exit }}')"
   73|     [[ -n "$dev" ]] || continue
   74|     if is_vpn_iface "$dev"; then
   75|       ip route del default dev "$dev" table main 2>/dev/null || true
   76|     fi
   77|   done < <(ip route show table main 2>/dev/null | awk '/^default/')
   78|   ip route del 8.8.8.8/32 2>/dev/null || true
   79|   # Leftover demo rule sent 1.1.1.1 via WG table 200.
   80|   while ip rule del to 1.1.1.1 table 200 2>/dev/null; do :; done
   81|   ip route flush table 200 2>/dev/null || true
   82|   ip route replace default via "$WAN_GW" dev "$WAN_IF" metric 10 src 192.168.50.10 table main
   83|   ip route replace default via "$VBOX_GW" dev "$VBOX_IF" metric 100 table main 2>/dev/null || true
   84| }
   85| 
   86| # Bind to enp0s8. Default-route ping follows NAT metric 100 and false-positives.
   87| ping_enp0s8() {
   88|   local i
   89|   for i in 1 2 3; do
   90|     ping -I "$WAN_IF" -c 1 -W 2 8.8.8.8 >/dev/null 2>&1 && return 0
   91|   done
   92|   return 1
   93| }
   94| 
   95| forwarding_cfg_ok() {
   96|   local fwd
   97|   fwd="$(sysctl -n net.ipv4.ip_forward 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
   98|   [[ "$fwd" == "1" ]] || return 1
   99|   iptables -t nat -C POSTROUTING -o "$WAN_IF" -j MASQUERADE >/dev/null 2>&1
  100| }
  101| 
  102| forwarding_ready() {
  103|   forwarding_cfg_ok || return 1
  104|   ping_enp0s8
  105| }
  106| 
  107| dhcp_gw() {
  108|   command -v sshpass >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  109|   sshpass -p "$MT_PASS" ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o ConnectTimeout=6 \
  110|     "$MT_USER@$MT_HOST" '/ip dhcp-server network print detail' 2>/dev/null \
  111|     | grep -oE 'gateway=[0-9.]+' | head -1 | cut -d= -f2
  112| }
  113| 
  114| revert_dhcp() {
  115|   command -v sshpass >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 1
  116|   sshpass -p "$MT_PASS" ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o ConnectTimeout=8 \
  117|     "$MT_USER@$MT_HOST" \
  118|     '/ip dhcp-server network set [find address="192.168.50.0/24"] gateway=192.168.50.1 dns-server=192.168.50.1' \
  119|     >/dev/null 2>&1
  120| }
  121| 
  122| is_vpn_iface() {
  123|   local d="${1:-}"
  124|   [[ -n "$d" ]] || return 1
  125|   case "$d" in
  126|     wg0|tun0|tun1|sstp0|vti0|nebula1|tap_soft|ppp0|ppp1) return 0 ;;
  127|     zt*) return 0 ;;
  128|     *) return 1 ;;
  129|   esac
  130| }
  131| 
  132| # Overlay / lab-local that can ping -I but must never carry LAN client internet.
  133| # Match Python _CLIENT_EGRESS_DENY: nebula1 only. wg0 is a real client WAN.
  134| client_egress_denied() {
  135|   case "${1:-}" in
  136|     nebula1) return 0 ;;
  137|     *) return 1 ;;
  138|   esac
  139| }
  140| 
  141| table_default_dev() {
  142|   ip route show table "$1" 2>/dev/null | awk '/^default/ {
  143|     for (i=1;i<=NF;i++) if ($i=="dev") { print $(i+1); exit }
  144|   }'
  145| }
  146| 
  147| iface_down() {
  148|   local d="$1" st
  149|   st="$(ip -br link show "$d" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $2}')"
  150|   [[ -z "$st" || "$st" == "DOWN" ]]
  151| }
  152| 
  153| lan_proof_failed() {
  154|   local iface="$1"
  155|   local stamp="/run/autovpn-lan-proof/${iface}"
  156|   local age=9999 line now mtime
  157|   now="$(date +%s)"
  158|   if [[ -f "$stamp" ]]; then
  159|     mtime="$(stat -c %Y "$stamp" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
  160|     age=$((now - mtime))
  161|     if (( age >= 0 && age < 45 )); then
  162|       line="$(head -1 "$stamp" 2>/dev/null || true)"
  163|       [[ "$line" == FAIL ]] && return 0
  164|     fi
  165|   fi
  166|   if ! iptables -t nat -C POSTROUTING -o "$iface" -j MASQUERADE >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  167|     return 0
  168|   fi
  169|   return 1
  170| }
  171| 
  172| client_tables() {
  173|   # 110–125: kiyo 110, phone 111, cloud ZT remotes 112+. Plus any lookup 110–199.
  174|   {
  175|     seq 110 125
  176|     ip rule show 2>/dev/null | awk '{
  177|       for (i=1;i<NF;i++) if ($i=="lookup") {
  178|         t=$(i+1)+0
  179|         if (t>=110 && t<200) print t
  180|       }
  181|     }'
  182|   } | sort -n | uniq
  183| }
  184| 
  185| pin_table_mikrotik() {
  186|   local table="$1"
  187|   ip route replace 192.168.50.0/24 dev "$WAN_IF" table "$table" 2>/dev/null || true
  188|   ip route replace default via "$WAN_GW" dev "$WAN_IF" table "$table"
  189| }
  190| 
  191| # Snapshot first (before protect_default / table pins) so crash logs show the broken state.
  192| stolen=0
  193| stolen_nat=0
  194| if ! ip -4 route show default 2>/dev/null | grep -Eq "via ${WAN_GW}[[:space:]].*dev[[:space:]]${WAN_IF}|via ${WAN_GW}[[:space:]]dev[[:space:]]${WAN_IF}"; then
  195|   stolen=1
  196| fi
  197| got="$(ip route get 8.8.8.8 2>/dev/null || true)"
  198| if echo "$got" | grep -q "via ${VBOX_GW}"; then
  199|   stolen=1
  200|   stolen_nat=1
  201| fi
  202| 
  203| # Client path: independent of Ubuntu's own ping. Do not pin a proven tunnel
  204| # on one ping -I blink. wg0 is allowed (Python LAN-proof).
  205| client_hits=()
  206| while read -r t; do
  207|   [[ -n "$t" ]] || continue
  208|   defdev="$(table_default_dev "$t")"
  209|   is_vpn_iface "$defdev" || continue
  210|   why=""
  211|   if client_egress_denied "$defdev"; then
  212|     why="overlay-no-client-wan"
  213|   elif iface_down "$defdev"; then
  214|     why="iface-down"
  215|   elif ! ping -I "$defdev" -c 1 -W 2 8.8.8.8 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  216|     if lan_proof_failed "$defdev"; then
  217|       why="ping-I-fail"
  218|     fi
  219|   elif lan_proof_failed "$defdev"; then
  220|     why="lan-proof-fail"
  221|   fi
  222|   [[ -n "$why" ]] || continue
  223|   client_hits+=("table-${t}:${defdev}:${why}")
  224| done < <(client_tables)
  225| 
  226| if [[ "$stolen" -eq 1 ]]; then
  227|   if [[ "$stolen_nat" -eq 1 ]]; then
  228|     maybe_crash_log "stolen-default via-${VBOX_GW}"
  229|   else
  230|     maybe_crash_log "stolen-default"
  231|   fi
  232| fi
  233| 
  234| protect_default
  235| 
  236| if [[ ${#client_hits[@]} -gt 0 ]]; then
  237|   for h in "${client_hits[@]}"; do
  238|     t="${h#table-}"
  239|     t="${t%%:*}"
  240|     pin_table_mikrotik "$t"
  241|     log "client table ${t} pinned via ${WAN_GW} (${h}) — DHCP unchanged"
  242|   done
  243| fi
  244| 
  245| # VPN path self-heal (wg0 + tun0/tap_soft/vti0/sstp0/tun1/nebula1/ztfn5ujzh6).
  246| # Per-path /tmp/ag-heal-PATH cooldown 90s — this timer is 10s; do not restart the stack.
  247| if [[ -f /usr/local/sbin/heal-vpn-paths.sh ]]; then
  248|   /bin/bash /usr/local/sbin/heal-vpn-paths.sh || true
  249| elif [[ -f /home/user/adaptive-gateway/scripts/heal-vpn-paths.sh ]]; then
  250|   /bin/bash /home/user/adaptive-gateway/scripts/heal-vpn-paths.sh || true
  251| elif [[ -f /usr/local/sbin/heal-wireguard.sh ]]; then
  252|   /bin/bash /usr/local/sbin/heal-wireguard.sh || true
  253| elif [[ -f /home/user/adaptive-gateway/scripts/heal-wireguard.sh ]]; then
  254|   /bin/bash /home/user/adaptive-gateway/scripts/heal-wireguard.sh || true
  255| fi
  256| 
  257| changed=0
  258| def="$(ip -4 route show default | awk '/metric 10|dev '"$WAN_IF"'/{print; exit}')"
  259| if ! echo "$def" | grep -q "via ${WAN_GW}"; then
  260|   got="$(ip route get 8.8.8.8 2>/dev/null || true)"
  261|   if ! echo "$got" | grep -q "via ${WAN_GW}.*dev ${WAN_IF}"; then
  262|     log "restored default via ${WAN_GW} dev ${WAN_IF}"
  263|     changed=1
  264|   fi
  265| fi
  266| 
  267| gw="$(dhcp_gw || true)"
  268| 
  269| if ping_enp0s8; then
  270|   echo 0 > "$FAIL_FILE"
  271|   exit 0
  272| fi
  273| 
  274| n=0
  275| [[ -f "$FAIL_FILE" ]] && n="$(cat "$FAIL_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
  276| n=$((n + 1))
  277| echo "$n" > "$FAIL_FILE"
  278| log "Ubuntu ping -I ${WAN_IF} 8.8.8.8 FAIL (${n}/${FAIL_LIMIT}) DHCP=${gw:-?}"
  279| if [[ "$n" -lt "$FAIL_LIMIT" ]]; then
  280|   exit 0
  281| fi
  282| 
  283| # Last-chance: forwarding_ready re-pings enp0s8. A recovered WAN must not
  284| # crash-log or yank Windows off .10.
  285| if forwarding_ready; then
  286|   echo 0 > "$FAIL_FILE"
  287|   log "enp0s8 ping missed ${FAIL_LIMIT} ticks but forwarding_ready true — leave DHCP ${gw:-?}"
  288|   exit 0
  289| fi
  290| 
  291| maybe_crash_log "ubuntu-enp0s8-wan-dead"
  292| 
  293| if [[ "$gw" != "192.168.50.10" ]]; then
  294|   exit 0
  295| fi
  296| 
  297| if revert_dhcp; then
  298|   echo 0 > "$FAIL_FILE"
  299|   log "DHCP reverted to 192.168.50.1 (Ubuntu enp0s8 ping failed + forwarding not ready). Windows still needs ipconfig /renew — Ubuntu cannot renew Windows leases."
  300|   maybe_crash_log "dhcp-revert-.1 ubuntu-enp0s8-ping-fail" 1
  301| else
  302|   log "DHCP revert FAILED — run restore-mikrotik-internet.sh"
  303|   maybe_crash_log "dhcp-revert-failed ubuntu-enp0s8-ping-fail" 1
  304| fi
  305| exit 0
  306|
